# Template catalog

> Generated by `npm run docs` from `src/kit/catalog.ts` and `src/kit/catalog.en.ts`. Do not edit by hand.
> Open `#/kit` in a browser for a live preview of every template. For the writing workflow see [README.md](README.md).

**How lengths are counted**: “chars” means one Han character; two Latin letters, digits or spaces count as one. “characters” counts every character and is used for numbers, codes and commands. Limits are hard: exceeding one breaks the layout. A field with a minimum looks empty below it.

## Quick reference: what do I want to say → which template

| Template | Position | One line |
| --- | --- | --- |
| [`Cover`](#cover) Cover | Opening · slide 1 | Editorial title and lead on the left, one oversized display numeral on the right, three key facts along the bottom. The numeral drifts slightly with the mouse. |
| [`Bento`](#bento) Bento overview | Overview · right after the cover | Six cards of different sizes form a global overview; each expands to a full-screen detail, with card title, number and main figure carried by a shared-element transition. |
| [`MetricBars`](#metric-bars) Metric bars | Data · size and share | Argument on the left; on the right 2–4 big numbers with share bars, plus an optional accent total row. A serif conclusion can sit at the lower left. |
| [`Timeline`](#timeline) Timeline | Time · periods and roadmap | Segment bars on a shared calendar axis; it labels each boundary date, draws today and hatches time already past; optionally 3 countdowns computed live from the viewer's clock. |
| [`SpotlightList`](#spotlight-list) Spotlight list | Data · name + value | The right column is a list of names with oversized values; the first item is spotlighted with accent colour and a larger size. The left column can carry a KeyValue detail table. |
| [`CompareTable`](#compare-table) Before and after table | Comparison · old → new | Row by row before / after; the column with the new state floats on a white panel, so the eye lands on the result first. |
| [`NumberedColumns`](#numbered-columns) Numbered points | Key points · parallel features | 3–4 points of equal weight, each column with a monospace tag, title and body; a huge serif number sits at the column foot and lights up on hover. |
| [`VerdictColumns`](#verdict-columns) Grouped recommendation | Recommendation · apply / wait / skip | Sorts cases into 2–3 verdict buckets: accent for recommended, ink for conditional, outline for not yet. A colour bar at the top states the stance. |
| [`Checklist`](#checklist) Tickable checklist | Procedure · preparation | Two columns of clickable checks, each with a title, one command and a reason. A large counter in the top right updates live with each tick, good for reading through live. |
| [`StepTrack`](#step-track) Step track | Procedure · ordered flow | 3–5 nodes on one track, the last step as the accent end point. Each step can carry one command (dark) or config line (light), with an optional bar of after commands below. |
| [`Teaser`](#teaser) Next stop | Preview · look ahead | A huge outlined numeral and a serif codename on the left; title, lead and 2–4 forward-looking notes on the right. Quieter than the cover. |
| [`Closing`](#closing) Conclusion | Closing · last slide | One large statement plus one grey call to action, with three numbered takeaways and references below. A small number in the top left echoes the cover. |
| [`Terminal`](#terminal) Terminal session | Procedure · the commands to type | A dark terminal window on the left where commands are typed in sequence, each numbered; the right side explains by number what each command shows. |
| [`CodeDiff`](#code-diff) Config comparison | Change · same config, old and new form | Two code panes: the old form on the left with changed lines struck through, the new form on the right with changed lines in accent; 2–3 notes at the bottom. |
| [`StatusGrid`](#status-grid) Status grid | Status · state of each object in a set | 6–10 small cards, each with a name and a status label (supported / new / partial / unsupported, renameable); a legend at the top counts each status. |
| [`SpecSheet`](#spec-sheet) Version table | Change · old and new versions of a set of components | A grouped table of 2–3 columns, one component per row from old to new version; rows with a major version change are marked with accent colour and a dot. |
| [`Cycle`](#cycle) Cycle diagram | Mechanism · stages that loop | A ring on the left with 3–6 stages clockwise around it and a small accent dot travelling the ring; one number or word in the centre; each stage explained on the right. |
| [`Faq`](#faq) FAQ | Questions · questions the audience asks most | Two columns of questions and answers, each with a serif number Q1–Q6 on the left, the question in bold, one or two sentences of answer below and an optional monospace tag. |
| [`Lanes`](#lanes) Swimlane board | Roadmap · now / next / later | 3–4 lanes, each with a time range and 2–4 cards below; one lane takes a pale accent background to mark what to do now. |
| [`LineChart`](#line-chart) Line chart | Trend · change over time | 1–3 lines; one focus line in accent with an area fill and value labels and its last point enlarged; 1–2 annotations hang below the line and connect to data points with a dashed line; the right column can carry interpretation. |
| [`Agenda`](#agenda) Contents | Navigation · after the cover | Title and lead on the left; 3–6 chapter rows on the right with number, icon, chapter name, one line of description and the opening page. Every row is a jump button. |
| [`SectionBreak`](#section-break) Section break | Section break · first slide of each chapter | A large chapter name and one line of lead, with the chapter's slides listed below; on the right a large accent icon on a double tray and a serif chapter number. The icon drifts slightly with the mouse. |
| [`QuoteHero`](#quote-hero) Definition and quotation | Concept · define a term / quote a line | A definition or quotation set centred at 76u, with keywords highlighted in accent one by one; three notes with icons below add the source, the stance or a caveat. |
| [`FlowDiagram`](#flow-diagram) Flow diagram | Mechanism · how it runs | 3–5 icon nodes run left to right with dashed arrows that keep flowing; one node can be marked as the focus and an input band can sit at the bottom. |
| [`ClaimCards`](#claim-cards) Claim check | Claim check · a verdict per claim | 4 or 6 cards, each with a common claim, a verdict stamp and one line of evidence. A claim judged false is struck through after the stamp lands. |
| [`LayerStack`](#layer-stack) Layer check | Troubleshooting · confirm layer by layer from outside in | 4–6 layers stacked like steps; each layer has a number, icon, layer name and the item to check, and the bottom layer is the accent-coloured “outcome” layer. Hovering one layer fades the others. |
| [`Matrix`](#matrix) Matrix comparison | Side-by-side · marks in a row × column grid | Rows are objects, columns are categories, and cells hold a monospace mark or “—”. One column can take an accent background as the focus of the slide. |
| [`IconGrid`](#icon-grid) Icon cards | Parallel · 6–8 objects | A 3 × 2 or 4 × 2 grid of icon cards; each card has an icon, title, description and a tag in the top right. On hover the card lifts and the icon lights up. |
| [`BigStat`](#big-stat) Single big number | Evidence · one number carries the slide | A very large number on the left (rising digit by digit, drifting with the pointer) with its definition; on the right the title, lead and 2–3 supporting numbers. |
| [`DeltaBars`](#delta-bars) Positive and negative change | Evidence · up or down | Diverging bars around a zero line: positive values to the right in the accent colour, negative to the left in ink. The left column holds the argument, an optional pull figure and the source. |
| [`Dumbbell`](#dumbbell) Two-point comparison | Evidence · two ways to measure one metric | Each row has two points (hollow ink a, solid accent b) joined by a gradient line; the length of the line is the gap, and the difference is listed on the right. |
| [`RangeBars`](#range-bars) Range estimate | Method · how reliable the number is | Each row has a point estimate and its error range; the narrower the range, the more reliable the number. One row can be highlighted, and the left column can hold an example block of “how to write the result”. |
| [`Gantt`](#gantt) Gantt chart | Plan · work laid out by week | A relative timeline in weeks, sprints or days, with optional month groups above, one work stream per row and deliverables that can be highlighted. |
| [`Milestones`](#milestones) Event timeline | Background · events in date order | 4–7 events on a horizontal axis, alternating above and below it, each with a date, a two-line title and a type tag; one key event can be highlighted. |
| [`QuoteWall`](#quote-wall) Quote wall | Support · a few verbatim lines | 3 or 5 verbatim quotes: the first in a large dark card on the left, the rest in a grid; each has the speaker, the source and a topic tag below it. |
| [`BeforeAfter`](#before-after) Before and after | Proof · two states of the same view | Two screenshots of the same size stacked together with a draggable divider; one label in each corner and the reading in the right column. On entry the divider slides in from the right to its start position. |
| [`Screenshot`](#screenshot) Annotated screenshot | Explanation · product interface | A screenshot in a browser frame on the left with 3–5 numbered dots on it; the right column lists the notes under the same numbers. Hovering a dot or a note highlights both sides. |
| [`Funnel`](#funnel) Conversion funnel | Diagnosis · stage-by-stage conversion and the stage to fix first | Centred bars that narrow stage by stage (evenly, not to scale) with counts on the right; the conversion rate between stages is worked out automatically and one chosen stage is marked in accent; the right column says why to fix that one first. |
| [`Quadrant`](#quadrant) Positioning quadrant | Positioning · two-dimensional competitive map | A 2 × 2 plane on the left with a label at each end of both axes and four quadrants that can be named; 3–7 points, the focus (usually you) with a pulsing ring; 2–3 notes on the right. |
| [`PersonaCards`](#persona-cards) Buyer personas | Customer · three roles in the buying group | Three cards side by side, one per role: icon, decision role, the need in their own words, two goals, two pains and the channels that reach them. The main buyer has an accent line on top. |
| [`PricingTiers`](#pricing-tiers) Tiered pricing | Pricing · good / better / best | 3–4 price cards; the recommended tier is a dark card, slightly raised and with a badge; each tier has a price, who it fits and 3–5 features, with one line on the value metric at the bottom. |
| [`KpiBoard`](#kpi-board) Metric board | Measurement · north star and input metrics | A large dark card on the left holds the north star metric (big number and trend line); 4 or 6 small cards on the right hold input metrics, each with a value, a change and either a trend line or a note on how it is counted. |
| [`ShareBar`](#share-bar) Composition bar | Allocation · how one whole is split | One 100% stacked bar per row with 2–5 segments and the percentage written inside; one segment can be set to the accent colour in every row; the legend sits on top. |
| [`Waterfall`](#waterfall) Revenue bridge | Breakdown · from opening to closing | A waterfall: an opening total, 3–6 changes and a closing total worked out automatically; increases in the accent colour, decreases hatched, dashed lines joining the bars; the reading goes in the right column. |
| [`Team`](#team) Team cards | Roles · who owns what | 4, 6 or 8 cards, each with an initials avatar, a name or role, a title, and one line at the bottom on what that person owns. The lead's avatar is in the accent colour. |
| [`IssueTree`](#issue-tree) Issue tree | Analysis · breaking the question apart | A dark card on the left holds the question to answer; 2–4 branches on the right, each followed by a row of 2–3 causes to check; the focus branch gets a light accent background. |
| [`Heatmap`](#heatmap) Heat table | Distribution · values where two dimensions cross | A row × column table of values; cells are shaded by value and numbers on dark cells turn white automatically; cells with no data keep a dashed frame; a colour scale sits below and the reading in the right column. |
| [`Roadmap`](#roadmap) Roadmap | Roadmap · direction by quarter | 2–4 work streams × 3–5 periods; items can span periods and are marked done (grey with a tick), doing (accent, with a breathing dot) or planned (dashed frame); a vertical “today” line marks where you are. |
| [`Statement`](#statement) One line | Rhythm · a pause between two dense passages | One sentence alone on the slide, 112u and centred, key words in the accent colour; a faint line of support can go below. With tone: "accent" the whole slide takes the accent colour. |
| [`HangingCover`](#hanging-cover) Hanging icon cover | Opening · slide 1 of a product launch | A large centred title with coloured icon tiles hanging from thin threads at the top, swaying slowly; one word of the title takes the accent colour, with the lead and 1–3 pills below. |
| [`PanelCards`](#panel-cards) Panel cards | Product · 3–4 capabilities, each with a small interface | A light grey rounded card: a small heading on top and a white mini interface below, as a list, big number, bars, progress bar, conversion or chips. |
| [`AskAnswer`](#ask-answer) Q&A card | Product · Show one question and its answer | A panel on the right: question bubble at the top right, a "found ..." line below it, then three result cards, each with one big number and a row of small bars. Title and notes on the left. |
| [`ProofSplit`](#proof-split) Proof split | Argument · One capability plus two evidence numbers | A dark panel on the left with one large icon tile and a few setting rows; on the right a title, lead, two big numbers and check items. |
| [`IndexStack`](#index-stack) Index stack | Structure · One slide for 3–5 items, one at a time | A contents column on the left, cards stacked like folders on the right. With build: "focus", each → brings the next card to the front and highlights it in the contents. |
| [`ContactPanel`](#contact-panel) Contact card | Closing · How to reach you next | A large dark rounded card: title, lead and promise checks on the left, a white card with contact details or entry points on the right, a black action bar at the bottom. |
| [`Orbit`](#orbit) Orbit | Structure · One core and what surrounds it | Two orbits on the right with a dark icon in the center and 4–8 colored icons on the inner and outer ring; title, lead and points on the left. |
| [`NumberOpener`](#number-opener) Big number opener | Rhythm · Open a chapter with one number | A 400u accent number on the left, one explanatory line and a sub line on the right. More minimal than BigStat; use it as the opener or on the first slide of a chapter. |
| [`ChapterProgress`](#chapter-progress) Chapter progress | Rhythm · First slide of a chapter, shows where the talk is | Chapter title and a large "02 / 04" on top, a progress line below: finished chapters ticked, the current one lit, later chapters faded. |
| [`Question`](#question) Question slide | Rhythm · Lead into the next part with one question | The whole slide is one question in large centered type, with a very faint giant question mark behind it. |
| [`Contrast`](#contrast) Two-line contrast | Rhythm · Past / now, misconception / fact | Two large lines stacked: the first greyed out and struck through, the second in body color, labels in the accent color. With build, the second line appears on →. |
| [`Recap`](#recap) Chapter recap | Rhythm · Review the points at the end of a chapter | 3–4 cards, each with a tick, a point title and one line; a black pill at the bottom previews the next chapter. |
| [`HeroPanel`](#hero-panel) Dark hero cover | Opening · Tool or platform launch | The whole slide is one dark rounded card: tag, title, lead and pills on the left, a real screenshot tilted in perspective on the right; up to 3 laurels below the card. |
| [`TabbedShot`](#tabbed-shot) Tabbed screenshot | Product · 2–4 screens of one product | Centered title and lead, a tab switcher below, then one large screenshot. Click a tab to switch the screenshot; the first one shows on open. |
| [`BrandKit`](#brand-kit) Design guidelines | Product · Type, color and size | A card on the left lists 2–3 typefaces with large Aa samples, rows of color dots at the top right and 4 size tokens at the bottom right. Here color is content, so hex values are allowed. |
| [`Laurels`](#laurels) Laurels | Argument · Awards, rankings or hard numbers | 3–4 cards, each with a title and subtitle ringed by laurel and one line below. |
| [`FullBleed`](#full-bleed) Full-page image | Rhythm · One image fills the slide | A real screenshot or photo fills the slide and darkens towards the bottom, with a white title and caption at the bottom left. Set tone: "dark" on the SlideDef. |
| [`Thanks`](#thanks) Thanks and questions | Closing · Thanks + questions + contact | A very large thank you and an invitation to ask on the left, 2–4 contact cards on the right, plus an optional real QR code. |
| [`SplitWord`](#split-word) Split word cover | Opening · Creative or brand proposal | Two huge words sit at the top left and the bottom right, with a stack of 2–3 cards tilted between them: real screenshots, or color blocks with icons. The signature cover of the mono style. |
| [`PillAgenda`](#pill-agenda) Pill agenda section break | Rhythm · Start of each chapter | A row of pills on top lists every chapter, past ones struck through and the current one inverted; a viewfinder at the bottom left frames a very large chapter number, with the chapter title and lead on the right. |
| [`StatRow`](#stat-row) Stat card row | Data · 3–4 scale numbers side by side | A row of dark cards like phone widgets: three dots at the top right, a mono label, a big number and one line; the first number uses the accent color. A row of name pills can go below. |
| [`CountCompare`](#count-compare) Rolling number comparison | Data · One number before and after | A card on each side with one big number that counts from 0 to its value on entry; an arrow and a change pill in between. The right card is inverted and stands for now. |
| [`Donut`](#donut) Donut chart | Data · 2–5 parts of one whole | One large ring, the focus part in the accent color and the rest in greys; the total or the focus share sits in the middle; a legend on the right gives the value and percentage of each part. |
| [`BarChart`](#bar-chart) Bar chart | Data · 2–8 categories, grouped or stacked | Vertical bars with the value above each bar; with mode="stack" they stack and only the total is labelled. One focus category can be set and the rest fade. Series 1 is the accent color, the rest are greys. |
| [`Bubble`](#bubble) Scatter / bubble chart | Data · Position on two dimensions | Two axes and 3–10 points; give size and it becomes a bubble chart, where area stands for a third value. One focus point uses the accent color. When labels collide, use side to move a label up, left or right. |
| [`Gauge`](#gauge) Gauge | Data · One value against its limit | A 240 degree dial filled to the current value in the accent color, with an optional target tick; the big number in the middle. 2–3 supporting numbers can go on the right. |
| [`ServiceList`](#service-list) Big word service list | Content · 4–6 items, one big word per row | A column of very large words, alternating ink and accent color row by row, a colored icon square on the left and one line of monospace note text on the right. |
| [`QuoteArc`](#quote-arc) Arc quote cards | Content · three quotes with sources | Three quote cards in an arc: the middle one inverted and in front, the side ones turned inward. Each holds one quote, a name and a source. |
| [`WorkCards`](#work-cards) Color block work cards | Content · 3–4 projects or options | Each card is one large block of color: title on top, a big icon in the middle (or a real screenshot or decor- illustration) and an outlined pill tag at bottom left. |
| [`RepeatClosing`](#repeat-closing) Repeated word closing | Closing · last slide | A Latin-script phrase fills the whole slide background (outlined, slowly drifting); a big line of text with an accent underline sits in the middle, a monospace line below it, and an optional chat bubble at bottom right. |
| [`StickerCover`](#sticker-cover) Sticker cover | Opening · informal sharing or personal intro | On grid paper, the last line of the title sits inside a design-tool selection box and a handwritten note points at the title with a curved arrow; 2–4 stickers with mouse cursors scatter around; on the right a Polaroid holds a real screenshot with a file-name label. |
| [`WordBlocks`](#word-blocks) Color word blocks | Content · 4–10 keywords | Each word is a thick-bordered color block with a small shape in front (circle, star, square, flower, triangle, half circle), laid out slightly tilted like stickers. |
| [`FolderCards`](#folder-cards) Stacked folder cards | Content · 3–4 projects, one at a time | A few folders stacked with offset tabs; the front one shows a title, a body, points and a real screenshot. With build set to focus, each → brings the next folder to the front. |
| [`SparkCards`](#spark-cards) Mini trend cards | Data · 3–4 metrics, each with a trend | Each card has a big number, a change pill and a small line chart (with area and a dot at the end); the period is noted at the bottom. |
| [`Pictogram`](#pictogram) Pictogram share | Data · “how many out of every N” | The conclusion and legend on the left; 10 large cells or a 10×10 grid on the right, filled in order from the first cell in proportion, in at most three colors. |
| [`SlopeRank`](#slope-rank) Rank change | Data · ranks at two time points or under two measures | Two columns of ranks with connecting lines: rising lines are darker, the focus line uses the accent color, and the change in places is marked on the right. |
| [`PixelClosing`](#pixel-closing) Pixel text closing | Closing · last slide | Very large pixel Latin text (for example LET'S TALK), over a yellow and black diagonal-striped block that holds a button-style call to action; a mascot at the top (a decor- illustration, replaced by an icon square when there is none). |
| [`FloatCover`](#float-cover) Floating geometry cover | Opening · slide 1 of a brand or launch deck | A very large centered title with a number card below it; four CSS-drawn 3D shapes (sphere, cube, torus, capsule) float around it, and a big-word marquee drifts slowly behind. The signature cover of the geometric style. |
| [`TileWall`](#tile-wall) Icon tile wall | Parallel · 6–9 features or parts | Title and lead on the left; three columns of large icon tiles on the right, with a big 3D sphere showing behind them. Good for abilities, integrations or parts. |
| [`ServiceIndex`](#service-index) Big number service columns | Procedure · 3–4 steps or services | 3–4 columns, each sitting on a very large pale number: a title plus a short body. A small cube sits in the top right. |
| [`GlowStack`](#glow-stack) Glow stack cards | Preview · this one, then the next | A clear card in front lists the current points, a slightly blurred card behind it is the next one, and a colored glow shows through underneath. |
| [`DuoCards`](#duo-cards) Two-color option cards | Comparison · two options or two uses | Two large solid cards, orange and purple, side by side; each has a big number, one line of text and a list with green dots. It reads like two price tiers and also suits two ways of using something. |
| [`Desktop`](#desktop) Desktop cover | Opening · informal product demo | A computer desktop: a column of desktop icons on the left, a window in the middle holding the title and lead, a clock widget in the top right and a dock along the bottom. |
| [`WindowStack`](#window-stack) Stacked windows | Procedure · 2–3 steps | 2–3 system windows stacked with an offset; each has a small title and one or two lines, and they appear in order during the build step. |
| [`ToggleCard`](#toggle-card) Toggle settings window | Key points · which are on, which are off | A settings window where each row has an icon, a name, a note and a toggle. The toggles can be clicked live, but it is only illustrative and controls nothing. |
| [`Pyramid`](#pyramid) Pyramid | Structure · layers of dependent conditions | 3–5 trapezoid layers stacked into a pyramid: the bottom layer is widest and palest, each layer has one line of text on its right, and they appear bottom-up during the build step. |
| [`Venn`](#venn) Venn diagram | Concept · the overlap of a few things | 2 or 3 translucent circles overlap; each circle holds a name and one line of text, and a dark pill in the middle holds their overlap. |
| [`Concentric`](#concentric) Concentric circles | Structure · from the core outward | 3–4 concentric rings on the left with the darkest core; a list on the right explains each ring, with dot colors matching the rings. |
| [`DecisionTree`](#decision-tree) Decision tree | Analysis · which one to pick when | A yes / no decision tree read from the top down, with at most two levels of questions and four results; the results are boxed in the accent color. |
| [`Fishbone`](#fishbone) Fishbone diagram | Analysis · causes of one problem | The fish head on the right holds the problem; 4 or 6 bones run in from above and below, each with a cause category and 1–3 specific causes. The bones slant toward the head and meet the spine. |
| [`Swimlane`](#swimlane) Swimlane flowchart | Roles · who does which step in a process | Each role gets a horizontal lane, the steps take one column in order and arrows connect them; a step handed to another role gets an accent outline. |
| [`ArchLayers`](#arch-layers) Architecture layers | Mechanism · which layers make up the system | 3–5 horizontal layers; the layer name and a mono tag sit on the left, a row of component blocks on the right. One layer can be highlighted. |
| [`Balance`](#balance) Pros and cons scale | Argument · weighing both sides | A balance with a set of reasons on each pan; the heavier pan sinks and the pans always hang vertically. The tilt angle comes from weight, so mark the tilt as illustrative in source when there is no measurement. |
| [`DoubleFunnel`](#double-funnel) Double funnel comparison | Comparison · two groups going through the same stages | Two funnels back to back, the stage names in a middle column, each side's bars scaled by value, with the percentage relative to the first stage. |
| [`KeyReveal`](#key-reveal) Gradient title reveal | Opening · slide 1 of a launch, or a one-line reveal | One oversized, tightly tracked title in the centre of a pure black canvas, the keywords filled with a gradient, one line of small grey text below and up to 3 small numbers at the bottom. |
| [`KeySpec`](#key-spec) Giant spec number | Evidence · one spec number | A huge gradient number and unit filling half the slide, one point above it, one caption and up to 3 small specs below. |
| [`OrbCover`](#orb-cover) Gradient orb cover | Opening · slide 1 for creative or brand decks | Large title and lead on the left, a diffuse gradient orb on the right, with 2–3 frosted-glass number cards floating over the orb. |
| [`GradientCards`](#gradient-cards) Gradient feature cards | Parallel · 3 features or options | Three large rounded frosted-glass cards; the top of each is a gradient panel in a different hue with a glass icon disc, and below it a tag, title and body. |
| [`BlockStats`](#block-stats) Colour block numbers | Data · 3–4 hard metrics | 3–4 saturated blocks with a thick black outline, hard shadow and slight tilt; each block has a large condensed number, a label and one note. The first block uses the theme colour. |
| [`PopPoster`](#pop-poster) Pop slogan poster | Opening / pause · one slogan | Two or three lines of oversized bold slogan, one word inside a tilted colour block, a round sticker number in the top right corner and a row of outlined tags at the bottom. |
| [`BentoBoard`](#bento-board) Grid overview | Overview · the whole picture on one slide | Six white blocks of different sizes: a large block top left for the title and lead, two number blocks and one vertical text block on the right, a horizontal icon block at the bottom and an accent-colour number block bottom right. Unlike the expandable Bento, it is a static one-slide overview. |
| [`BentoSpotlight`](#bento-spotlight) Bento spotlight | Note · one feature explained in full | A set of blocks under the title: a tall block on the left with icon, title and text, two number blocks top right and a wide block bottom right with mono key-value rows. |
| [`SlotPicker`](#slot-picker) Calendar time slots | Schedule · date and time slot | Title on the left, a booking card on the right: a month calendar marks the available dates and the right column lists that day's time slots. Both dates and time slots switch on click. |
| [`UiSteps`](#ui-steps) UI step cards | Procedure · getting started in three steps | Three rounded step cards; the top half of each is a small interface (input field, option pills or a done message) and the bottom half holds the step number, title and text. |
| [`FloatHero`](#float-hero) Floating tag cover | Opening · slide 1 for a product or feature | A quiet centred title and lead, a small card with a waveform below, and 4–6 slightly tilted icon tags floating around. |
| [`TranscriptCard`](#transcript-card) Transcript card | Citation · a stretch of dialogue or narration | Title on the left, a transcript card with a waveform on the right, 3–4 spoken lines with one highlighted, and 2–3 small tags floating beside the card. |
| [`RetroTabs`](#retro-tabs) Retro tab window | Grouping · 2–4 parallel lists | Title on the left, an old-style window with a thick black border on the right, clickable folder tabs along the top and 3–4 icon rows inside each tab. |
| [`RetroLog`](#retro-log) Retro changelog | Recap · version or stage changes | A changelog inside an old-style window: version, tag and one line each, with the newest tag in the accent colour; a small sticky note window sits at an angle beside it. |
| [`GlowMetric`](#glow-metric) Glowing big number | Data · one core number and three supporting numbers | Title and lead on the left; on the right a huge serif italic number floating on a warm glow, with a hairline-separated row of three supporting numbers below. The signature data slide of the warm dark style. |
| [`HairlineGrid`](#hairline-grid) Hairline feature grid | Parallel · 4 or 6 features | 1px hairlines under the title split the area into 2×2 or 3×2 cells; each cell has an icon, a mono tag, a title and one note, and warm light glows from the bottom of the focused cell. |
| [`RibbonCover`](#ribbon-cover) Ribbon cover | Opening · slide 1 for events and training | An oversized rounded title top left; two thick ribbons, one blue and one black, cross at an angle in the lower right and repeat one word, plus a thin lime ribbon. |
| [`RibbonSection`](#ribbon-section) Ribbon section break | Rhythm · first slide of each chapter | A wide accent-colour ribbon runs across the slide at an angle, holding the chapter number badge and the chapter title; below it the lead on the left and this chapter's slide titles on the right. |
| [`ChatThread`](#chat-thread) Chat Q&A | Q&A · 3–6 messages | Title, lead and a hand-drawn wavy arrow on the left, a column of chat bubbles on the right: questions on the left in white, answers on the right in the accent colour. Each bubble is one build step. |
| [`NoteCards`](#note-cards) Tilted sticky notes | Parallel · 3–4 rules or tips | 3–4 candy-coloured sticky notes, each slightly tilted with a strip of tape on top; each has an icon, a title and one line of text. |
| [`RevealStatement`](#reveal-statement) Word-by-word statement | Pause · one line to be remembered | One large line in the centre of the slide; on entry each character rises from below one by one (Latin words appear whole), then a small line fades in. Screen readers read the whole sentence. |
| [`SketchCover`](#sketch-cover) Drawing board cover | Opening · slide 1 for a product home page or portfolio | A large iridescent rounded panel with a two-line serif title in the centre, the keyword of the first line pressed into a tilted orange tag; below it a dotted drawing board with one line circled in orange pen, and a row of red dot pills at the bottom of the panel. |
| [`ArcCards`](#arc-cards) Arc dark cards | Parallel · five equally important mechanisms or services | A centred serif title, with five dark cards along a shallow arc below, tilted inward at both ends. Each card holds an icon in an orange corner frame, a title and one line of text. With a build step, they appear one by one. |
| [`CaseCard`](#case-card) Case card | Evidence · one project or feature with a real screenshot | One large white rounded card: a serif title, a paragraph and small grey tags at the bottom left; a real screenshot on the right, with a dark ticker under it carrying the project keywords. |
| [`AgencyHero`](#agency-hero) Growth site hero | Opening · slide 1 for a product or proposal | A small neon-green badge on top, two or three tight oversized black lines on the left that may embed coloured icon squares, a black status pill at bottom left, a lead with pill buttons at bottom right, and a row of light names along the bottom. |
| [`AgencyRows`](#agency-rows) Numbered service rows | Content · 4–5 items, one per row | A big title with an outlined pill on the right, then numbered rows divided by hairlines: number, bold title, short grey note, coloured icon square and a round plus. |
| [`AgencyManifesto`](#agency-manifesto) Point-of-view paragraph | Rhythm · one paragraph of a position | A small grey pill label, then a full block of tight oversized bold text, indented on the left, with 0–2 words in the accent colour. |
| [`HaloHero`](#halo-hero) Halo display cover | Opening / closing · first or last slide in a product- or brand-home style | Two or three centred light display lines; one word in line 2 turns italic serif; a striped pill sits at the end of line 1 and line 3 fades out; a soft multi-colour halo behind. Optionally a command pill and a link for the closing. |
| [`HaloRows`](#halo-rows) Hairline item list | Parallel · 3–6 features, rules or shortcuts | A small label, title and lead on the left; a column of hairline-separated items on the right: round outlined icon, title, one short line of detail and a plus. |
| [`HaloMarquee`](#halo-marquee) Marquee showcase | Showcase · two screenshots or pieces of work | One oversized line scrolling sideways in a loop, with two large image cards below, each with a title and small grey tags. |
| [`FanCover`](#fan-cover) Fan screenshot cover | Opening · slide 1, product-site style | A full page of saturated blue with tone: accent, a centred pill badge, a tight big title and lead, two pill buttons with round arrows, and a row of fan-tilted screenshot cards rising from the bottom edge. |
| [`PillSentence`](#pill-sentence) Sentence with icon pills | Point of view · one big line that states the problem | One full oversized left-aligned sentence with 2–3 coloured icon pills inside it; two columns at the bottom: a small note on the left and a line of text on the right. |
| [`OpenList`](#open-list) Expanding list | Checklist · 5–7 features or questions | Title, lead and one pill button on the left; a hairline-separated list on the right where each row may carry a keycap and a plus, and one row opens to show its answer. |
| [`PhotoCover`](#photo-cover) Photo cover | Opening · one image sets the tone | One image fills the page, with a vignette and a gradient darkening the bottom left, where a white title, subtitle and one line of meta sit. The image pushes in slowly. |
| [`PhotoSplit`](#photo-split) Half image, half text | Explanation · one image with one block of text | Half the page is a full-bleed image against the edge, left or right; the other half holds the title, lead and 0–3 numbered points that appear one by one. |
| [`PhotoMosaic`](#photo-mosaic) Photo mosaic | Showcase · 3–5 images seen together | An asymmetric image grid below the title; the first image takes the large cell and the rest stagger around it, each with an optional pill caption, appearing one at a time. |
| [`PolaroidWall`](#polaroid-wall) Polaroid wall | Showcase · a few images each with one line | 3–5 square instant photos in a row, each slightly rotated with tape at the top and one line of serif italic handwriting below, stuck up one at a time. |
| [`FilmStrip`](#film-strip) Film strip | Timeline · 3–5 points with images | A black film strip runs across the page with sprocket holes above and below; each frame holds an image with the year printed in its corner, and a numbered caption sits below. Frames not yet shown look undeveloped. |
| [`MagazineSpread`](#magazine-spread) Magazine spread | Explanation · one block of text to read | A full image on the left half with a shadow at the gutter; the right half holds the big title, a standfirst over a thick rule, body text with a drop cap and a pull quote, with a folio and source at the bottom. |
| [`CaseStudy`](#case-study) Case study card | Evidence · one case with three numbers | A large rounded hero image on the left; on the right a title, tags and a short intro, with three metric cards at the bottom appearing one by one. |
| [`DeviceFrame`](#device-frame) Device frame | Evidence · a product or site screenshot | A real screenshot inside a drawn laptop, tablet or phone frame, with 0–3 hairline callouts on the right appearing one by one. |
| [`CompareSlider`](#compare-slider) Before-and-after slider | Comparison · before and after in the same view | Two identically framed images stacked, with a divider and handle between them. Each press of → moves the divider to the next stop and lights the matching note below. |
| [`PhotoStack`](#photo-stack) Photo stack | List · 3–4 items revealed one at a time | A pile of white-bordered photos on the right; each press of → drops a new photo at an angle onto the top, and the matching numbered text appears on the left. |
| [`FlipCards`](#flip-cards) Flip cards | Reveal · question first, answer after | 2–4 cards with a claim or question on the front; each press of → flips one card in 3D to show the answer on the back. |
| [`CardStack`](#card-stack) Card stack deal | Sequence · 3–4 stages | The cards start as one pile on the right; each press of → deals the next card out of the pile into its place in the row. |
| [`FanCarousel`](#fan-carousel) Fan cards | Parallel · 4–6 points taken one by one | The cards spread along an arc like a hand of cards; each press of → turns the fan, and the next card stands up in the centre. |
| [`DepthCarousel`](#depth-carousel) Depth carousel | Route · 3–6 stops in order | The cards sit on a 3D track that recedes into the distance; each press of → brings the next stop to the front and pushes the rest back and dimmer. A row of small marks at the bottom shows the position. |
| [`Envelope`](#envelope) Envelope | Reveal · a letter or an announcement | Title and lead on the left, a CSS-drawn envelope on the right: step 1 opens the flap, step 2 pulls the letter out. Optional paper texture and stamp. |
| [`FlipBook`](#flip-book) Flip book | Narrative · 2–4 spreads | An open book; each page holds one big number, a title and a note. Each → press turns the right page around the spine in 3D to show the next spread. |
| [`Lanyard`](#lanyard) Lanyard badge | People · introduces the speaker or main character | A badge on a fabric strap on the right, swinging gently on entry then settling; the badge shows the event name, letter monogram, name and role. 2–4 points on the left appear one by one. |
| [`ProgressCards`](#progress-cards) Progress cards | Data · 2–4 percentages | Each card has a ring, a big percentage, a label, a bar and one note; each → press fills one card's ring and bar to its value. |
| [`ProcessAccordion`](#process-accordion) Process accordion | Process · 4–6 steps opened one at a time | A row of vertical panels; collapsed panels show only the number and a vertical title. Each → press expands the next step into a wide panel (accent background) with its note and folds the previous one back. |
| [`NotebookCover`](#notebook-cover) Notebook section | Chapter · divider page | A slightly tilted hardcover notebook on the right (accent cover, stitching, elastic band and paper label) with the chapter name on the label; on the left a very large chapter number and 2–4 items for this chapter. |
| [`Halftone`](#halftone) Halftone image | Atmosphere · photo turned into dots or dither | Title, lead and 0–3 small numbers on the left; on the right one image reprinted by an SVG filter as newspaper dots in the theme color (dots) or 1-bit dither (dither); the image itself needs no extra work. |
| [`ProgressiveBlur`](#progressive-blur) Progressive blur image | Atmosphere · full-page image with a title | A full-page image; the lower half is blurred and darkened in 6 growing layers; title and lead sit on the blurred area, with 0–3 tags and the source at the bottom right. Use tone: dark. |
| [`MaskReveal`](#mask-reveal) Mask reveal | Journey · uncover the image as you talk | 2–4 numbered points on the left; on the right a greyed image; each step opens the mask a little more (a circle growing from the center, or blinds opening group by group) and the image shows its original colors. |
| [`LogoWall`](#logo-wall) Logo wall | Trust · partners or participants | Below the title, a thin-line grid wall; each cell holds a placeholder geometric mark (12 built in, no real brands) with a name and role; each row appears as one step, and a line below states that the marks are illustrative. |
| [`PhotoClosing`](#photo-closing) Photo closing | Ending · full-page image closing | A full-page image pushes in slowly; a black gradient darkens the left; one large closing line, a lead and 0–3 numbered next steps. Use tone: dark. |
| [`RankingRace`](#ranking-race) Ranking race | Data · ranks changing over time | Horizontal bar ranking; each → press moves to the next period, the bars re-sort and change length with their values, and the current year shows at the bottom right. |
| [`BubbleCluster`](#bubble-cluster) Bubble cluster | Data · compare size | A tight cluster of circles whose area is proportional to the value; large circles show the name and value; 1–3 readings can sit on the right. |
| [`NodeGraph`](#node-graph) Node graph | Structure · what connects to what | One center node and the nodes around it, joined by lines that can carry a relation; when stepped, each node appears with its lines. |
| [`Sankey`](#sankey) Sankey diagram | Data · how a total splits | 2–4 columns of nodes joined by flow bands whose width is proportional to the value; when stepped, the flow runs column by column. |
| [`Treemap`](#treemap) Treemap | Data · composition of a whole | One rectangle is cut into blocks by value; a bigger block means a bigger value, and large blocks show name, value and share. |
| [`Radar`](#radar) Radar chart | Data · compare profiles across dimensions | A web chart with 5–8 axes; the first series is filled with the accent color, the second is a grey dashed line and appears after the first when stepped. |
| [`Scatter`](#scatter) Scatter quadrants | Data · distribution on two metrics | A scatter plot split into four quadrants by two dashed lines; the quadrant names appear one by one; a few points carry names and key points use the accent color. |
| [`StackedArea`](#stacked-area) Stacked area chart | Data · composition over time | 2–6 layers stack into the total (or 100%); the right end labels each layer with its name and latest value; when stepped, layers add from the bottom up. |
| [`DotMap`](#dot-map) Dot map | Data · where things are | An offline dot-matrix world map (Natural Earth land, public domain) with numbered dots placed by longitude and latitude and sized by value, plus a matching list on the right. |
| [`GlowMetrics`](#glow-metrics) Glow metric cards | Data · key numbers in the opening | 2–4 dark cards; the big number carries an accent glow and counts up from 0, with an optional small line chart and change note; made for dark styles. |
| [`Typewriter`](#typewriter) Typewriter | Quotation · one quote typed out letter by letter | Monospace large text types out 1–3 lines of a quote letter by letter with a blinking cursor; one line below holds the translation or source, and 0–3 label and value pairs sit at the bottom. Each line is one build step. |
| [`RotatingWord`](#rotating-word) Rotating word | Point of view · swap 2–4 words in one sentence | The headline leaves one word slot; each step swaps in the next word and lights the matching card below; a card can carry one number and one note. |
| [`MorphHeadline`](#morph-headline) Morph headline | Turn · one line becomes another | Two very large lines in the same place: the first blurs apart letter by letter, the second condenses in place; two columns at the bottom note the before and after lines. |
| [`TextArc`](#text-arc) Circular text | Atmosphere · one line bent into a ring | Title, lead and 0–3 points on the left; on the right one line set along a ring (real SVG text) that rotates into place; the center holds one large word or number and can sit on a decorative image. |
| [`Odometer`](#odometer) Rolling digits | Data · 1–3 big numbers | 1–3 big numbers, each digit rolling to its final value like an odometer; each number has a label and one line of note below. The DOM always holds the real final text; the rolling digits are decoration layered on top. |
| [`MarkerSweep`](#marker-sweep) Highlighter | Interpretation · mark key points in a passage | One large-type passage; a highlighter sweeps over 2–4 key phrases in turn, each numbered, with a matching margin note at the bottom. |
| [`TextMask`](#text-mask) Cut-out word | Opening · cover or section break | One huge word acts as a cut-out; the image shows only through the letterforms and slowly settles; below are a title, one subtitle and 0–3 numbers. |
| [`SplitFlap`](#split-flap) Split-flap board | Timeline · 3–6 moments | An airport split-flap board: each row has a date, an optional time, an event and a status, and the flaps turn character by character to their final value; each row is a build step. |
| [`NewsTicker`](#news-ticker) News ticker | Event · front-page headline | A front-page headline, dateline and lead; a news ticker at the bottom drives in from the right and stops (no loop), showing 2–4 items. |
| [`KaraokeLines`](#karaoke-lines) Script highlighter | Quotation · read a script line by line | 3–6 script lines; unread lines are light grey, each step lights the next line (accent colour and a sweeping underline), and read lines return to body colour; the right column holds the original text and the source. |
| [`ParadigmCards`](#paradigm-cards) Model comparison cards | Comparison · structure of 2–3 approaches | 2–3 cards side by side, each with a small structure sketch at the top (directory tree / blocks / flat) and aligned attribute rows below; each press → one card appears, and one card can be emphasised. |
| [`AnatomyCard`](#anatomy-card) Anatomy card | Structure · what one thing is made of | A large card on the left is cut into 2–4 strips (example values in monospace); matching notes sit on the right; each press → light one strip and its note. |
| [`ExplorerFlip`](#explorer-flip) Browser flip | Reveal · how it looks vs what it is | A file browser window first shows the familiar folder view; one press → every row flips to its full key or real path, and a note appears on the right. |
| [`VerbCards`](#verb-cards) Request cards | Interface · 3–4 operations | 3–4 cards, each with a method badge (PUT/GET…), a request line and one line of note; each press → one card prints its response line at the bottom like a receipt. |
| [`NinesDial`](#nines-dial) Nines dial | Number · explain how many nines | A row of digit cells spells out 99.9…%; each press → light the next group of nines and show that group's note below. |
| [`TierLadder`](#tier-ladder) Tier ladder | Rating · 3–5 tiers from hot to cold | 3–5 cards step down to the right, colour fading from the accent colour to grey; each press → drop the next tier. |
| [`LifecycleRail`](#lifecycle-rail) Lifecycle rail | Process · one thing changes position over time | A ticked rail holds 3–5 stops; a marker block moves to the next stop each step while that stop's card drops in. |
| [`MeterCards`](#meter-cards) Metering cards | Composition · what a bill is made of | 3–5 cards, each naming one billing or metering dimension; the unit sits large in a dial, with one line of note below. |
| [`ShelfGallery`](#shelf-gallery) Shelf gallery | Scenario · 3–4 examples with images | 3–4 image cards in a row; the current one widens and gets an accent outline while the others step back; each press → the next card comes forward. |
| [`TabSpec`](#tab-spec) Tabbed spec card | Parallel · introduce 2–4 options one by one | A row of tabs on top, one large spec card below (text on the left, parameter rows on the right); each press → switch to the next tab and the card swaps to the next item. |
| [`TokenStream`](#token-stream) Token stream | AI · how text is cut into tokens | One sentence is cut into numbered token pieces that appear group by group, with the total in the top-right corner; each group has its own background colour and one line of note below. |
| [`MoeRouter`](#moe-router) Expert routing | AI · how a mixture of experts picks experts | Tokens on the left, a router in the middle, routed expert cells on the right (up to 384) plus the shared experts; each step moves to the next token and the picked k cells light up. |
| [`ContextWindow`](#context-window) Context window | AI · capacity and length marks | A long capacity bar with ticks; several spans fill in from the left step by step (for example training length, max output, window limit), each with a big number and a note below. |
| [`ScoreMatrix`](#score-matrix) Benchmark matrix | AI · several models × several benchmarks | Rows are benchmarks and columns are models; each cell holds a score and a thin bar scaled to that row's top score; the focus model's column is coloured in, and the row's top score gets a dot. Rows appear one by one. |
| [`ModelSpecCard`](#model-spec-card) Model spec card | AI · identity card for one model | A card on the left: version tag, model name and capability chips, with bars comparing total and active parameters at the bottom; 4–8 spec rows appear one by one on the right. |
| [`TokenPriceGrid`](#token-price-grid) Token price grid | AI · priced per token | Rows are token types (cache hit, cache miss, output) and columns are tiers or models; each cell holds a price and a bar on a log scale, so a hundredfold gap stays readable. The focus column is coloured in. |
| [`AttentionMask`](#attention-mask) Attention mask | AI · illustrative attention pattern | A query × key cell grid, with 2–4 patterns on the right (full attention, causal, local window, sparse); each step switches the mask and the lit cells and the lit-cells / total-cells ratio change with it. |
| [`EffortDial`](#effort-dial) Parameter dial | AI · one adjustable parameter | A half-circle dial shows the parameter's min and max and 2–4 stops; each step turns the needle to the next stop, the current value shows below the dial, and the matching note on the right is highlighted. |
| [`PeakClock`](#peak-clock) Time-of-day clock | AI · time slots within one day | A 24-hour ring marks 1–4 windows (peak, maintenance window) in the accent colour, with the time zone in the centre; the legend and 2–4 notes appear one by one on the right. |
| [`AliasRoute`](#alias-route) Alias routing | AI · several names point to the same version | Left: 2–5 API names or aliases (old names dashed), with lines that converge on one accent card on the right naming the version actually serving requests. |
| [`GlobeCover`](#globe-cover) Globe cover | Opening · slide 1 | Title, lead and up to 3 facts on the left; on the right an offline dot-matrix globe (orthographic projection) with a few glowing arcs drifting along great circles. The globe is decorative only. |
| [`ArcGlobe`](#arc-globe) Arc globe | Data · one place to many | On an offline dot-matrix globe, glowing great-circle arcs run from one hub to 2–8 places, with a matching list on the right; in build steps the arcs draw one by one. |
| [`PopGrid`](#pop-grid) Site dot grid | Data · how many, split how | Each glowing dot stands for one site (city, store, node); dots are colored by group into a grid, with the total and each group's count on the right. |
| [`RequestJourney`](#request-journey) Request path | Mechanism · where one request goes | 3–6 stops sit along a glowing line, each with an icon, name and note; in build steps a glowing packet hops on to the next stop. |
| [`ShieldLayers`](#shield-layers) Shield layers | Mechanism · defense in depth | 2–5 glowing half rings on the left surround the protected core, with a list on the right of what each layer blocks, outer to inner; in build steps the layers light up one at a time. |
| [`SpikeChart`](#spike-chart) Spike chart | Data · a sudden surge | A glowing area line whose maximum is scaled up and labeled with a halo, plus up to 3 callouts that can appear one by one; suits dark styles. |
| [`OutageBoard`](#outage-board) Outage board | Evidence · how one incident unfolded | Like a status page: one time track per service, with outages and partial impact marked in two fills, and dashed vertical lines through every row at key moments. |
| [`ReachRings`](#reach-rings) Reach rings | Data · how far coverage reaches | 2–4 glowing concentric rings around a center point, with a radar sweep passing slowly; one row per ring on the right gives the distance or latency and how much it covers. |
| [`ServerRack`](#server-rack) Server rack | Mechanism · where it runs | A dark rack with 4–8 slots, each labeled with one service and a blinking indicator, plus 1–3 points on the right; in build steps the slots power on one at a time. |
| [`TraceTimeline`](#trace-timeline) Signal timeline | Background · how it developed | The history sits on a glowing signal line: each event is a waveform spike with a glowing node, text alternates above and below, and a pulse runs along the line. |
| [`QueryPlan`](#query-plan) Query plan tree | Technology · how one query is executed | An EXPLAIN-style plan tree with nodes and links drawn top-down; SQL can sit above it, and one node can be marked in the accent color. |
| [`SchemaEr`](#schema-er) Table relationship diagram | Technology · data model | 2–4 tables left to right, listing column names, types and PK/FK, with foreign keys drawn as elbow lines to the referenced column and optional cardinality labels. |
| [`ResultGrid`](#result-grid) Query and result | Technology · Data · one query and the table it returns | The query sits in a dark code window on the left, the result table on the right; one column can take the accent color and some rows a darker fill, with a status line at the bottom. |
| [`CodeCallouts`](#code-callouts) Code callouts | Technology · walking through code line by line | A dark code window with line numbers, numbered dots beside the annotated lines and matching notes in the right column; each build step lights one note and its line. |
| [`RequestResponse`](#request-response) Request and response | Technology · one round trip | Two code windows: the outgoing request on the left, the returned response on the right, with a labeled arrow between them and a light dot running left to right. |
| [`BeamFlow`](#beam-flow) Beam flow diagram | Technology · Architecture · many in, one out | Inputs in the left column, one hub in the middle, outputs on the right, joined by curves with light dots moving along them to show where data or requests go. |
| [`TupleVersions`](#tuple-versions) Version timeline | Technology · Concurrency · several versions of one row | On a transaction number axis each version is a bar from xmin to xmax, with versions still valid in the accent color; a snapshot is a dashed vertical line labeled below with the version it sees. |
| [`BTreeIndex`](#btree-index) B-tree lookup | Technology · Index · lookup path | Index pages and keys are drawn by level; pages on the lookup path get an accent outline and the matching key is inverted, with one note per level on the right. |
| [`JsonTree`](#json-tree) Document and paths | Technology · Data · querying a document | An indented JSON document sits in a dark window on the left; on the right are a few path expressions with their returned values, and each step marks the lines that expression picks. |
| [`SessionSequence`](#session-sequence) Session sequence diagram | Technology · Concurrency · what happens first | 2–3 sessions each get a column, events run top to bottom in time order with t1, t2… on the left; waits use dashed boxes and the final result uses the accent color. |
| [`BadgeHero`](#badge-hero) Badge hero | Opening · website-style hero | A centered pill badge (section name plus one status line), a large title, a lead and 1–2 drawn buttons; a wide image sits in a window that rises from the bottom edge. |
| [`TabPanels`](#tab-panels) Tab panels | Expansion · several sides of one topic | A horizontal tab bar with an accent underline under the current tab; below it one large card shows the current tab's title, body and 0–3 points, with room for one number on the right. Each press → switch to the next tab. |
| [`EditionPicker`](#edition-picker) Edition picker | Options · introducing a few editions or plans one by one | 3–6 single-choice cards (3 per row): icon, tag, name, description and a fit line at the bottom, with a radio dot in the top right. The selected card gets an accent outline and a check; each press → the next card is selected. |
| [`ReleaseTrain`](#release-train) Release train | Time · overlapping version lifecycles | One row per version: version number, state pill (end of life / maintained / latest / beta / in development) and dates on the left; on the right a bar from release to end of maintenance on a true time scale, and a vertical line marks today. Estimated end dates fade out. |
| [`MegaMenu`](#mega-menu) Mega menu | Overview · the categories of one family | A website navigation bar (a text brand name on the left, a few menu items, one of them expanded), with the expanded mega menu below: 2–4 columns, each column a monospaced heading and 2–5 entries (name + one-line description). |
| [`PathCrumbs`](#path-crumbs) Path breadcrumbs | Relationship · upstream, downstream and flow | A row of arrow-shaped breadcrumbs (name + small monospaced text), with the last section (or the one set by focus) filled in the accent colour; a note card sits under each section, joined by thin lines. |
| [`FilterChips`](#filter-chips) Filter chips | Classification · filter one set by tag | A row of filter chips (with counts) above, 4–12 small cards below; each press selects the next chip, cards that belong to it keep their outline and the rest turn grey. One card can belong to several chips. |
| [`CtaBlock`](#cta-block) Call to action block | Closing · what to do next | One large rounded block in a light accent tint across the slide: on the left the kicker, the big title, the lead, a copy box (monospaced address or command) and buttons; on the right 2–4 numbered step cards that appear one by one. |
| [`FooterClosing`](#footer-closing) Footer closing | Ending · resources and links | A closing slide shaped like a website footer: title, lead and buttons at the top left, 2–4 link columns at the top right, a thin rule at the bottom (note + source) and a line of huge fading text sitting on the bottom edge of the slide. |
| [`FaqAccordion`](#faq-accordion) Accordion Q&A | Q&A · open one at a time | Kicker, title, lead and optional buttons on the left; a column of questions divided by thin rules on the right, each row with a number, the question and a round +/− button. Only one answer is open at a time; each press closes the previous one and opens the next. |
| [`MediaExpand`](#media-expand) Small window expand | Motion · a screenshot or photo grows from a small window to the full slide | The screenshot first appears as a small window next to the title; with build: 'reveal', one press expands it to fill the slide, the title steps aside and a caption appears over the image. When jumping to the slide, printing or with reduced motion, the expanded view is shown directly. |
| [`ScatterOrder`](#scatter-order) Scatter to order | Motion · scattered cards fall into place one by one along a path | 4–6 cards start scattered and tilted; with build: 'reveal', each press drops one card into its place, and once all are in place the connecting line appears. When jumping to the slide, the arranged path is shown directly. |
| [`TiltShot`](#tilt-shot) Tilt to straight | Motion · a screenshot in perspective straightens, then the points are covered one by one | Title and 2–3 points on the left, a screenshot entering at a perspective angle on the right. With build: 'reveal', the first press straightens the screenshot, and each press after that reveals one point. When jumping to the slide, the straightened screenshot and all points are shown directly. |
| [`ConvergeShots`](#converge-shots) Images converge | Motion · several images stand side by side, then converge into one focus image | 3–4 images stand side by side, each with a label. With build: 'reveal', the first press brings them together, the focus image grows to the front and the others stack behind it; the second press shows the caption. When jumping to the slide, the converged view is shown directly. |
| [`StepVideo`](#step-video) Step through video | Motion · each press plays one segment and stops on the frame to talk about | A video on the left, 2–4 stop notes on the right. With build: 'reveal', each press plays up to the next stop and pauses there, and the matching note appears. Going back or jumping to the slide shows the stop frame directly; when jumping to the slide, printing or with reduced motion it stops at the last stop. |
| [`ExhibitCover`](#exhibit-cover) Exhibition cover | Opening · a huge narrow serif title with a date block in the corner | A huge title fills the left half, a light date block sits at the top right, and 3–4 columns of small text divided by thin rules run along the bottom. Designed for the biennale style; other styles work too. |
| [`YearBars`](#year-bars) Numbers with year bars | Data · two big numbers with thin bars by year | 1–2 big numbers on the left (unit as superscript), 3–7 thin horizontal bars ordered by year on the right, the latest year in the accent colour. Designed for the biennale style; other styles work too. |
| [`NumberTiles`](#number-tiles) Number tiles | Content · 3–4 tall colour blocks, each with a huge number | A row of tall colour blocks, each with a huge number above and a title and one-line description below. In the stencil style the four colours are ochre, magenta, orange and cyan; other styles use the card colour and accent-coloured numbers. With build: 'reveal' the blocks appear one by one. |
| [`ScoreTable`](#score-table) Score table | Comparison · a black header row, a coloured tag in every cell | 2–4 options side by side against 3–5 criteria, each cell a short coloured tag in one of four tones: good, mid, bad or plain. Designed for the stencil style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the rows appear one by one. |
| [`MetricMosaic`](#metric-mosaic) Metric mosaic | Data · a tall bar, a title block, small number tiles and a big number tile fit into one slide | A tall accent-coloured bar holding one number on the left, the title block in the middle, 2–3 small number tiles at the top right and one big number at the bottom right. Designed for the fluorescent grid style; other styles work too. |
| [`StepGrid`](#step-grid) Next-steps grid | Closing · a large title block plus three step blocks | The top row holds one large accent-coloured title block and one pattern block; the bottom row holds three step blocks (time, title, description, small note), the last one in dark. Designed for the fluorescent grid style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the blocks light up one by one. |
| [`FramedStats`](#framed-stats) Framed numbers | Data · 3–4 number boxes with heavy borders | A row of number boxes with thick black borders and hard shadows, each with a theme-coloured dot in the top-right corner. Designed for the block frame style; other styles work too. |
| [`IndexList`](#index-list) Column index | Content · numbered entries in two columns, divided by thin rules | 4–6 numbered entries split into two columns, each with a title and a one-line description, separated by thin rules, with the titles in the theme colour. Designed for the index style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the entries appear one by one. |
| [`AgendaMosaic`](#agenda-mosaic) Agenda mosaic | Opening · five topics as colour blocks of different sizes | One tall dark block holds the first topic; the other four are smaller blocks in the theme colour, the paper colour and a light theme colour, each with a number, a title and a small tag. Designed for the forest style; other styles work too. |
| [`MethodSteps`](#method-steps) Method steps | Process · numbered step cards alternating dark and light | 3–4 step cards alternating dark and paper colour, each with a big number, a title and a description, and a bottom line saying who does it and what it produces. Designed for the emerald style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the cards light up one by one. |
| [`PriorityColumns`](#priority-columns) Three priority columns | Content · three full-height columns, huge numbers, one column in the theme colour | A thick rule under the big title, then three full-height columns below, each with a huge number, a tag, a title and a description, with one column filled in the theme colour. Designed for the manifesto style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the columns appear one by one. |
| [`ChapterNumber`](#chapter-number) Chapter number | Section break · a huge chapter number with a short title | A glowing huge chapter number on the left, a short title and a paragraph on the right, and one line of small text at each bottom corner. Designed for the night pink style (dark); other styles work too. |
| [`StackBars`](#stack-bars) Stepped layers | Structure · horizontal bars offset layer by layer | 3–6 horizontal bars offset to the right layer by layer from top to bottom, the colour deepening from the theme colour, each with a name on the left and one line of description on the right. Designed for the broadside style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the layers appear one by one. |
| [`ChatDemo`](#chat-demo) Chat demo | Realistic UI · show how it works through a short conversation | Title, lead and 0–3 notes on the left; a hand-built chat window on the right, where the last reply can carry a result card. The window always carries the label 'illustrative interface'. With build: 'reveal', each press reveals one message. |
| [`AppCards`](#app-cards) Dashboard cards | Realistic UI · 2–4 filled-in app panels | A row of hand-built app panels below the title: window name, title, status pill, 2–5 fields and tags, all filled in. Each card is marked as an illustrative UI. With build: 'reveal' the cards appear one by one. |
| [`AtelierHero`](#atelier-hero) Atelier hero | Opening · studio home page | A centred status pill with a green "available" dot, a huge heavy title, a lead and two buttons, a row of client names, and a strip of work images drifting slowly sideways along the foot. Made for the atelier style; other styles work too. |
| [`ProjectPair`](#project-pair) Project pair | Evidence · two flagship projects | A centred pill label and title over two large landscape images side by side, each captioned with the client and the kind of work. The images wipe up and zoom slightly on hover. |
| [`ProcessQuote`](#process-quote) Process with quote | Method · how we work plus one quote | A title and 3–5 numbered steps with durations on the left; a tall portrait on the right carrying a rating pill and a client quote card. |
| [`ServiceTrio`](#service-trio) Service trio | Product · three services | Three soft grey rounded cards under a centred title, each with an image on top, then a number, the service name, one line and a few small tags. Cards lift and images zoom on hover. |
| [`TeamBanner`](#team-banner) Team banner | Background · team and figures | A team photo filling the slide, a pill label at the top left, and along the bottom a dark statement card plus 2–3 figure cards whose digits roll up. |
| [`BookingClose`](#booking-close) Booking close | Closing · book a call | A status pill, big title, lead, buttons and contacts on the left; on the right a booking card with the host's avatar, a month calendar and three time slots, all clickable on the slide. |
| [`MonographHero`](#monograph-hero) Monograph hero | Opening · agency introduction | A three-line introduction at the top left, a rating and a short note at the top right; below, a magazine-style story card (image, index, serif paragraph, play button) and a dark object picture. |
| [`ProjectIndex`](#project-index) Project index | Evidence · project list | A column of project rows with the current one inked black, and a large picture of that project at the right. Click or hover a row to switch the picture. |
| [`WhyStats`](#why-stats) Why stats | Proof · three figures | A short title, a few plus-sign reasons and a quote on the left; a large paragraph on the right above three figure cards whose digits roll in over a faint outline echo, with an optional progress bar. |
| [`TeamPortraits`](#team-portraits) Team portraits | Background · team members | Three black-and-white portrait photos plus a dark hiring card; portraits turn colour on hover. Name, role and number underneath. |
| [`ServiceLedger`](#service-ledger) Service ledger | Product · service list | A title and a ruled list on the left: accent numbers, a service name and one line each; a tall picture with a caption pill on the right. Rows appear one by one. |
| [`MonographClose`](#monograph-close) Monograph close | Closing · contact details | Three huge lines with the middle one in the theme colour, contacts ruled underneath, and a large picture at the right carrying a dark pill button. |
| [`NocturneHero`](#nocturne-hero) Nocturne hero | Opening · app home page | The theme colour glows in the middle of a dark stage: faces, a big title, a lead, buttons and three figures whose digits roll in on the left; a slowly floating phone on the right shows the result, thumbnails and a button. |
| [`FeatureBento`](#feature-bento) Feature bento | Product · feature overview | A five-cell bento under a centred title: a large accent card with three portraits fanned out, a large dark card full of labelled style thumbnails, and three small picture-and-text cards along the bottom. |
| [`GlowPricing`](#glow-pricing) Glow pricing | Pricing · three plans | Three plan cards with the recommended one glowing in an accent gradient; a monthly / yearly switch above flips every price at once. |
| [`StepTrio`](#step-trio) Step trio | Procedure · three steps to start | Three step cards whose top half acts out the step: photos dealt like prints, a style picker you can tap on the slide, and the finished picture. Step number, title and one line underneath. |
| [`RevealCompare`](#reveal-compare) Reveal compare | Comparison · before and after | A title, a lead and 2–4 spec rows on the left; a portrait comparison on the right whose middle handle can be dragged (or moved with the arrow keys) to see before and after. |
| [`WallClose`](#wall-close) Wall close | Closing · call to action | A tilted wall of photos filling the slide, columns drifting slowly up and down; the centre is darkened for the title, one line, buttons and a small note. |
| [`SunriseHero`](#sunrise-hero) Sunrise hero | Opening · SaaS home page | A warm yellow sunrise glow across the top: a mock nav bar, a centred title, lead and two buttons, two teammates' cursors drifting in the side margins, and a dashboard rising from the bottom edge (completion arc, bar trend, team faces and tasks). |
| [`KitBento`](#kit-bento) Kit bento | Product · what is inside | A three-cell bento: two big-figure cards on top whose right halves sketch stacked pages and a block layout, and a full-width card below with copy at the left and a dark design canvas of page frames at the right. |
| [`HomeTrio`](#home-trio) Home trio | Product · options side by side | Three cards under a centred title, each holding a homepage sketch (chart, calendar or dark footer) with a name and one line underneath. Hover and the sketch scrolls like a web page; click to pick one. |
| [`KanbanDemo`](#kanban-demo) Kanban demo | Product · interactive demo | A pill, title, lead and ticked points on the left; a task board on the right with view tabs and member faces on top, a teammate cursor gliding over the tabs, and three columns of cards. Click a card and it moves to the next column while the counts update; each column ends in a dashed "add task" slot. |
| [`ProofMosaic`](#proof-mosaic) Proof mosaic | Customer · quotes and reviews | A mosaic under a centred title: a large dark quote card with a portrait in its right half; on the right, a sun-yellow headline figure above two short quote cards, each with a star rating, a measured result chip and a signed face. |
| [`BuyoutPricing`](#buyout-pricing) Buyout pricing | Pricing · two plans and a close | Two plan cards and a sun-yellow closing card: each plan has a price, one line, a ticked list and two key facts in boxes, and clicking switches the picked plan; the closing card carries a call to action, a note, a row of faces with a user count and a black button. |

<a id="cover"></a>

## 01 · Cover `<Cover />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `cover`

Editorial title and lead on the left, one oversized display numeral on the right, three key facts along the bottom. The numeral drifts slightly with the mouse.

**Use when**

- The first slide of a deck
- The subject condenses into one number or short code: a version number, year, quarter, rank

**Avoid**

- The subject has no number that represents it: put an initial or the last two digits of a year in numeral instead of forcing one
- A section break: Teaser is more restrained for that

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- numeral 1–4 characters; at 3–4 characters the size shrinks automatically
- title 1–2 lines, ≤ 12 characters per line; subtitle ≤ 10 characters and narrower than numeral, otherwise it crowds the big numeral
- facts 2–4 items, each value ≤ 10 characters
- note ≤ 2 lines
- No source field: any fact on the cover must appear on a later slide with its source

**Interaction**: The numeral follows the mouse slightly; its digits rise out of a mask one by one.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small label above the title |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Main title |
| `subtitle` | `string` |  | Serif italic second line, quoted automatically; suits codenames and taglines |
| `lead` | `string` | yes | One sentence on what this deck covers, ≤ 60 chars |
| `facts` | `{ label, value }[]` | yes | Key facts along the bottom |
| `numeral` | `string` | yes | Oversized numeral on the right |
| `note` | `string \| string[]` |  | Small note in the top right; suits trivia |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Header operation hint; on the cover, use "Press → to advance" |

**Example**

```tsx
<Cover
  kicker="Release Briefing · 版本发布解读"
  title="Lumen 13"
  subtitle="aurora"
  lead="2025 年 8 月 9 日发布的新稳定版。这份简报梳理它的主要变化与升级路径。"
  facts={[
    { label: '发布', value: '2025.08.09' },
    { label: '支持', value: '5 年' },
    { label: '官方架构', value: '7 个' },
  ]}
  numeral="13"
  note={['代号 aurora', '取自北方夜空里的极光']}
/>
```

<a id="bento"></a>

## 02 · Bento overview `<Bento />`

**Position**: Overview · right after the cover; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `overview`

Six cards of different sizes form a global overview; each expands to a full-screen detail, with card title, number and main figure carried by a shared-element transition.

**Use when**

- One slide must explain what this is and let the audience go deeper by interest
- There are 6 parallel topics and 1 of them matters most (in slot 1)

**Avoid**

- Fewer than 6 topics: do not pad, use NumberedColumns
- You cannot name one figure or table for a topic's detail: the detail page will be empty
- Used more than once in one deck

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards exactly 6; order is position: 1 large (left), 2–3 wide (top right), 4–6 small (bottom)
- Titles must be pre-broken and never wrap: slot 1 one line; slots 2–3 ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars; slots 4–6 one line ≤ 12 chars
- summary 1 line; lead ≤ 70 chars
- feature.stats exactly 3 items, value ≤ 7 characters

**Interaction**: Click or Tab + Enter to expand; Esc returns and gives focus back to the card; arrow keys do not change slides while a detail is open.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `cards` | `BentoCard[6]` | yes | id, title[], summary, lead, source, mini?, detail, aside?, figure? |
| `feature` | `BentoFeature` | yes | Slot 1 only: numeral, codename?, pill?, date?, stats[3] |
| `label` | `string` |  | Accessible name |
| `card.mini` | `ReactNode` |  | Small graphic on a card (slots 2–6), plain DOM/CSS; see decks/release/minis.tsx |
| `card.detail` | `ReactNode` | yes | Right column of the detail page: chart, code comparison, table; see decks/release/details.tsx |
| `card.figure` | `{ value, unit?, caption }` |  | Anchor figure at the lower left of the detail page |
| `card.aside` | `ReactNode` |  | Custom block instead of figure, for example one command |

**Example**

```tsx
<Bento
  cards={[
    { id: 'release', title: ['Lumen 13'], summary: '…', lead: '…', source: '…', detail: <ReleaseVisual /> },
    { id: 'apt', title: ['APT 源改用', 'deb822 格式'], summary: '…', lead: '…', source: '…',
      mini: <MiniCode />, detail: <AptVisual />, aside: <AptAside /> },
    // …共 6 张
  ]}
  feature={{ numeral: '13', codename: 'aurora', pill: 'Stable', date: '2025.08.09',
    stats: [{ label: '软件包总数', value: '69,830+' }, { label: '官方架构', value: '7' }, { label: '支持周期', value: '5 年' }] }}
/>
```

<a id="metric-bars"></a>

## 03 · Metric bars `<MetricBars />`

**Position**: Data · size and share; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `numbers`

Argument on the left; on the right 2–4 big numbers with share bars, plus an optional accent total row. A serif conclusion can sit at the lower left.

**Use when**

- A few quantities that add up or compare: added / updated / removed, revenue mix, user segments
- The audience should remember one proportion

**Avoid**

- The numbers are not comparable (different units): use SpotlightList
- More than 4 numbers: use a table or split the slide
- Data changes over time: use Timeline

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 2–4 (at most 5 with total)
- Values ≤ 6 digits (92u size)
- label ≤ 6 chars
