- pull.figure ≤ 6 characters, pull.text ≤ 24 chars

**Interaction**: Share bars grow in sequence; numbers use tabular figures and are right-aligned for vertical comparison.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `rows` | `{ label, value: number, suffix? }[]` | yes | suffix shows as a grey superscript, e.g. "+" for "over" |
| `total` | `MetricRow` |  | Accent total row; shares use it as denominator, otherwise the largest value |
| `pull` | `{ figure, text }` |  | Conclusion at the lower left, for example 1 / 5 |
| `shares` | `boolean` |  | Whether to show the ≈% column, default true; turn it off when rows are not parts of one whole |

**Example**

```tsx
<MetricBars
  kicker="软件包"
  title="数字里的 aurora"
  lead="与 Lumen 12 相比，超过 14,116 个新软件包进入仓库。"
  rows={[
    { label: '新增', value: 14116, suffix: '+' },
    { label: '更新', value: 44326, suffix: '+' },
  ]}
  total={{ label: '软件包总数', value: 69830, suffix: '+' }}
  pull={{ figure: '1 / 5', text: '每五个软件包里，约有一个是新增的。' }}
  source="Lumen 发布公告（示意） 2025-08-09"
/>
```

<a id="timeline"></a>

## 04 · Timeline `<Timeline />`

**Position**: Time · periods and roadmap; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `lifecycle`

Segment bars on a shared calendar axis; it labels each boundary date, draws today and hatches time already past; optionally 3 countdowns computed live from the viewer's clock.

**Use when**

- Support periods, contract terms, release roadmaps, project phases
- The audience should feel how much time is left

**Avoid**

- Only one date: put it in Cover's facts
- More than 3 rows: split across two slides
- Events are discrete points, not ranges: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 1–3; ≤ 2 when countdowns are used
- segments on each row are contiguous, 1–3 per row
- A segment label must be shorter than the segment: ≤ 6 chars for a one-year segment
- countdowns ≤ 3
- About half a year of blank space at each end of the axis

**Interaction**: Segments grow in sequence; today and the countdowns are computed from the current date on every open, so the deck never expires.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `axis` | `{ from: 'YYYY-MM-DD', to: 'YYYY-MM-DD' }` | yes | Visible range; year ticks are generated |
| `rows` | `TimelineRow[]` | yes | name, code?, segments[{ from, to, label?, tone }], planned? |
| `legend` | `{ ink, accent }` | yes | Legend text for the two tones |
| `today` | `boolean` |  | Whether to draw the today line and the hatching over past time, default true |
| `countdowns` | `{ label, until, note? }[]` |  | Months remaining below the chart |

**Example**

```tsx
<Timeline
  kicker="支持周期"
  title="birch 已转入 LTS"
  axis={{ from: '2023-01-01', to: '2031-01-01' }}
  legend={{ ink: '常规安全支持', accent: 'LTS 长期支持' }}
  rows={[{
    name: 'Lumen 12', code: 'birch',
    segments: [
      { from: '2023-06-10', to: '2026-07-12', label: '常规安全支持', tone: 'ink' },
      { from: '2026-07-12', to: '2028-06-30', label: 'LTS', tone: 'accent' },
    ],
  }]}
  countdowns={[{ label: 'birch 剩余维护', until: '2028-06-30' }]}
  source="wiki.example.org/LTS"
/>
```

<a id="spotlight-list"></a>

## 05 · Spotlight list `<SpotlightList />`

**Position**: Data · name + value; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `desktop`

The right column is a list of names with oversized values; the first item is spotlighted with accent colour and a larger size. The left column can carry a KeyValue detail table.

**Use when**

- A set of name → version / score / count, with one item that matters
- Rankings, component versions, regional data

**Avoid**

- Before and after must be shown: use CompareTable
- Values are quantities that add up: use MetricBars
- No item clearly stands out

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–6; the first item is the spotlight
- value ≤ 6 characters
- name ≤ 14 characters; tag ≤ 8 chars, first item only
- KeyValue 2–4 rows

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ name, value, tag? }[]` | yes | The first item is enlarged and set in the accent colour |
| `extra` | `string` |  | Grey supplementary line below the list |
| `detail` | `ReactNode` |  | Supplementary block at the lower left, usually <KeyValue rows={…} /> |

**Example**

```tsx
<SpotlightList
  kicker="桌面"
  title={['KDE Plasma 6', '首次进入 Lumen']}
  items={[
    { name: 'KDE Plasma', value: '6.3', tag: '全新大版本' },
    { name: 'GNOME', value: '48' },
    { name: 'Xfce', value: '4.20' },
  ]}
  detail={<KeyValue rows={[{ key: 'Qt 6', value: { from: '6.4.2', to: '6.8.2' } }]} />}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §2"
/>
```

<a id="compare-table"></a>

## 06 · Before and after table `<CompareTable />`

**Position**: Comparison · old → new; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `compare`

Row by row before / after; the column with the new state floats on a white panel, so the eye lands on the result first.

**Use when**

- Version upgrades, policy changes, before and after a redesign
- Every row is the old and new value of one dimension

**Avoid**

- Only 1–2 changes: put them in NumberedColumns
- Three or more sides to compare: split the slide or build it custom
- A cell needs a line break to explain

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 4–7; fewer than 4 leaves the bottom empty
- Every cell must fit on one line: item ≤ 12 chars, value ≤ 22 chars
- Set mono: true for commands and file names

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `columns` | `[before, after]` | yes | Column names for the two columns |
| `rows` | `{ item, before, after, mono? }[]` | yes | Comparison rows |
| `itemLabel` | `string` |  | First column name, default "item" |
| `source` | `string` |  | Shown right of the title (table fills the height) |

**Example**

```tsx
<CompareTable
  kicker="行为变化"
  title="升级之后，这些默认行为变了"
  columns={['Lumen 12 · birch', 'Lumen 13 · aurora']}
  rows={[
    { item: '仓库配置', before: 'sources.list 单行格式', after: 'lumen.sources（deb822）', mono: true },
    { item: '/tmp', before: '磁盘上的普通目录', after: 'tmpfs，最多占用 50% 内存' },
  ]}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §2 · §5"
/>
```

<a id="numbered-columns"></a>

## 07 · Numbered points `<NumberedColumns />`

**Position**: Key points · parallel features; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `hardening`

3–4 points of equal weight, each column with a monospace tag, title and body; a huge serif number sits at the column foot and lights up on hover.

**Use when**

- 3–4 equally important features, principles or findings
- Each point fits under one keyword tag

**Avoid**

- The points have an order: use StepTrack
- The points differ in weight: use SpotlightList or Bento
- More than 4: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items exactly 3 or 4
- title ≤ 10 chars (one line)
- body 24–40 chars: shorter leaves the column empty, longer pushes into the big number at the foot
- Put long English identifiers (component names, package names) in tag, not in body; they cannot break mid-word
- tag ≤ 20 characters
- Keep body lengths close across columns

**Interaction**: Hover a whole column: the number turns accent and lifts, and the tag takes the accent background.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ tag, title, body }[]` | yes | Points |

**Example**

```tsx
<NumberedColumns
  kicker="安全与可验证"
  title="更安全，也更容易验证"
  items={[
    { tag: 'PAC · BTI', title: 'arm64 硬件加固', body: '在硬件支持时自动启用 PAC 与 BTI。' },
    { tag: 'lumen-repro-status', title: '可复现构建', body: '新增 lumen-repro-status 软件包。' },
    { tag: 'UEFI · U-Boot', title: 'HTTP Boot', body: '安装器可以通过 HTTP 启动。' },
  ]}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §2"
/>
```

<a id="verdict-columns"></a>

## 08 · Grouped recommendation `<VerdictColumns />`

**Position**: Recommendation · apply / wait / skip; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `who`

Sorts cases into 2–3 verdict buckets: accent for recommended, ink for conditional, outline for not yet. A colour bar at the top states the stance.

**Use when**

- Advice of the form who should use it, who should wait and who should not
- Selection verdicts, risk grades

**Avoid**

- There is no clear direction in the verdict
- More than 3 items per group: trim or split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- groups 2–3, ordered from yes to no
- 2–3 items per group, counts within 1 of each other; a group with 1 item looks empty
- item.title ≤ 16 chars; item.body 14–26 chars (one line)
- label ≤ 6 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `groups` | `{ tone: 'go' \| 'plan' \| 'stop', label, items[] }[]` | yes | tone sets the colour and icon |

**Example**

```tsx
<VerdictColumns
  kicker="升级建议"
  title="该不该现在升级？"
  groups={[
    { tone: 'go', label: '优先升级', items: [{ title: 'RISC-V 设备', body: '首个正式支持 riscv64 的稳定版。' }] },
    { tone: 'plan', label: '验证后升级', items: [{ title: '生产服务器', body: 'LTS 维护到 2028-06-30。' }] },
    { tone: 'stop', label: '暂不升级', items: [{ title: 'i386 机器', body: '发行说明建议不要升级。' }] },
  ]}
/>
```

<a id="checklist"></a>

## 09 · Tickable checklist `<Checklist />`

**Position**: Procedure · preparation; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `prepare`

Two columns of clickable checks, each with a title, one command and a reason. A large counter in the top right updates live with each tick, good for reading through live.

**Use when**

- Pre-launch checks, pre-upgrade preparation, acceptance criteria
- Each item has one concrete command or action

**Avoid**

- Items have a strict order: use StepTrack
- You are only listing features: use NumberedColumns

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 6 or 8 (two equal columns); with 4 the lower half is empty
- title ≤ 18 chars
- cmd ≤ 56 characters; give a cmd for every item where possible, an item without one is shorter than its neighbour
- note ≤ 30 chars

**Interaction**: Click an item to tick it; the tick is drawn with a stroke animation. Ticking never changes slides (Space stays with the button).

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `items` | `{ title, cmd?, note }[]` | yes | Checks |
| `countLabel` | `string` |  | Text after the count, default "confirmed" |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Defaults to "Click an item to tick it off" |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<Checklist
  kicker="升级前"
  title="先回到“纯净”的 Lumen 12"
  items={[
    { title: '备份关键数据', cmd: "dpkg --get-selections '*' > ~/curr-pkgs.txt", note: '另存 /etc 与 /var/lib/dpkg' },
    { title: '检查软件包状态', cmd: 'dpkg --audit && apt-mark showhold', note: '解除会阻塞核心包的 hold' },
  ]}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §4.1 – §4.4"
/>
```

<a id="step-track"></a>

## 10 · Step track `<StepTrack />`

**Position**: Procedure · ordered flow; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `steps`

3–5 nodes on one track, the last step as the accent end point. Each step can carry one command (dark) or config line (light), with an optional bar of after commands below.

**Use when**

- A flow that must run in order: upgrade, deploy, onboarding
- Every step has one clear action

**Avoid**

- Steps have no order: use NumberedColumns
- More than 5 steps: merge steps or split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps 3–5
- title ≤ 8 chars; body ≤ 40 chars (within two lines)
- cmd ≤ 30 characters
- after.cmds 2–5

**Interaction**: The track draws left to right, nodes appear in order and the track ends at the last node.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `steps` | `{ title, body, cmd?, config? }[]` | yes | with config: true the cmd shows as a light config line without the # prompt |
| `after` | `{ label, cmds[] }` |  | Bar of closing commands after the flow |

**Example**

```tsx
<StepTrack
  kicker="升级步骤"
  title="四步完成升级"
  steps={[
    { title: '改写软件源', body: '把 birch 改为 aurora。', cmd: 'Suites: aurora aurora-updates', config: true },
    { title: '刷新索引', body: '确认没有旧源。', cmd: 'apt update' },
    { title: '完整升级', body: '完成后重启。', cmd: 'apt full-upgrade' },
  ]}
  after={{ label: '重启之后', cmds: ['apt autoremove'] }}
/>
```

<a id="teaser"></a>

## 11 · Next stop `<Teaser />`

**Position**: Preview · look ahead; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `forky`

A huge outlined numeral and a serif codename on the left; title, lead and 2–4 forward-looking notes on the right. Quieter than the cover.

**Use when**

- What happens next: next release, next quarter, roadmap points
- A chapter transition slide

**Avoid**

- A firm conclusion must land: use Closing
- There is no forward-looking information to cite: do not invent it

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- numeral 1–3 characters
- word ≤ 10 characters
- notes 2–4: key ≤ 16 characters, text ≤ 34 chars

**Interaction**: The outlined numeral drifts with the mouse and rises digit by digit.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `numeral` | `string` | yes | Outlined large numeral |
| `word` | `string` |  | Serif italic word, quoted automatically |
| `notes` | `{ key, text }[]` | yes | Forward-looking notes |

**Example**

```tsx
<Teaser
  kicker="下一站"
  title="Lumen 14 cedar"
  numeral="14"
  word="cedar"
  notes={[
    { key: 'armel', text: 'aurora 是最后一个支持 armel 的版本。' },
    { key: 'KDE Frameworks 5', text: '将在 cedar 周期中移除。' },
  ]}
/>
```

<a id="closing"></a>

## 12 · Conclusion `<Closing />`

**Position**: Closing · last slide; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `closing`

One large statement plus one grey call to action, with three numbered takeaways and references below. A small number in the top left echoes the cover.

**Use when**

- The last slide of a deck

**Avoid**

- The conclusion runs past one sentence: shorten it first
- With no references, do not fake links; omit refs

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- statement ≤ 20 chars; follow ≤ 28 chars
- takeaways exactly 3, each 20–46 chars; rewrite a sentence when its last line holds only a word or two
- refs 2–5 without a protocol prefix; refs are this slide's source, there is no separate source

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, usually "Conclusion" |
| `mark` | `string` |  | Small number echoing the cover; 1–4 digits only, text blanks the whole slide |
| `statement` | `string` | yes | One-sentence conclusion |
| `follow` | `string` |  | Grey second line: call to action |
| `takeaways` | `string[3]` | yes | Three takeaways, numbered automatically |
| `refs` | `string[]` |  | References |

**Example**

```tsx
<Closing
  kicker="结论"
  mark="13"
  statement="aurora 是当前的稳定版。"
  follow="birch 已转入 LTS，现在开始规划升级。"
  takeaways={['新装系统直接选 aurora。', '老机器按「检查 → 改源 → 两步升级」推进。', '留意 /tmp 与 deb822 源。']}
  refs={['example.org/releases/aurora/release-notes', 'wiki.example.org/LTS']}
/>
```

<a id="terminal"></a>

## 13 · Terminal session `<Terminal />`

**Position**: Procedure · the commands to type; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `habits`

A dark terminal window on the left where commands are typed in sequence, each numbered; the right side explains by number what each command shows.

**Use when**

- 3–4 independent commands that all have to be typed in a terminal
- The audience should see exactly what to type and why each command is there

**Avoid**

- The commands depend on each other and form a flow: use StepTrack
- Items must be ticked off: use Checklist
- There is no real command: do not invent output

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps 3–4; cmd ≤ 44 characters, ASCII only (non-ASCII breaks the typing animation)
- out 0–3 lines, ≤ 48 characters per line; only output or comments (# prefix) that appear in the docs, never invented
- note ≤ 34 chars
- window ≤ 24 characters, for example root@aurora: ~

**Interaction**: Commands type out character by character in order, output fades in after each, and the cursor blinks on the last line. Hovering a note on the right turns its number accent.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `window` | `string` | yes | Window title |
| `prompt` | `string` |  | Prompt, default # |
| `steps` | `{ cmd, out?, note }[]` | yes | Command, output and the explanation on the right |

**Example**

```tsx
<Terminal
  kicker="新命令"
  title="升级之后，换掉几个老习惯"
  window="root@aurora: ~"
  steps={[
    { cmd: 'apt install wtmpdb libpam-wtmpdb', note: 'last 由 wtmpdb 替代，装上 PAM 模块后才会记录登录。' },
    { cmd: 'lslogins --failed', note: '查看失败的登录尝试，作用与过去的 lastb 相近。' },
    { cmd: 'ssh-keygen -t ed25519', note: 'OpenSSH 9.8p1 起不再支持 DSA 密钥。' },
  ]}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §5.1.8 · §5.1.9"
/>
```

<a id="code-diff"></a>

## 14 · Config comparison `<CodeDiff />`

**Position**: Change · same config, old and new form; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `deb822`

Two code panes: the old form on the left with changed lines struck through, the new form on the right with changed lines in accent; 2–3 notes at the bottom.

**Use when**

- A config file or snippet moves from an old format to a new one
- The two forms are equivalent and the audience should compare them line by line

**Avoid**

- Only a few setting values changed: use CompareTable
- More than 8 lines of code: cut to the key part or split across two slides

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- before / after 2–8 lines each, ≤ 52 characters per line
- label ≤ 12 chars; file takes the full path
- mark is a zero-based line number
- notes 2–3: key ≤ 12 characters (monospace), text ≤ 30 chars

**Interaction**: The two panes slide in one after the other and code fades in line by line.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `before` | `{ label, file, lines, mark? }` | yes | Pane with the old form |
| `after` | `{ label, file, lines, mark? }` | yes | Pane with the new form |
| `notes` | `{ key, text }[]` |  | Notes at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<CodeDiff
  kicker="APT 源格式"
  title="一行源，变成一段源"
  before={{ label: '单行格式 · 已弃用', file: '/etc/apt/sources.list', lines: ['deb https://repo.example.org/lumen aurora contrib'], mark: [0] }}
  after={{ label: 'deb822 格式', file: '/etc/apt/sources.list.d/lumen.sources', lines: ['Types: deb', 'URIs: https://repo.example.org/lumen', 'Suites: aurora', 'Components: contrib'], mark: [0, 1, 2, 3] }}
  notes={[{ key: 'apt modernize-sources', text: '发行说明推荐用这条命令迁移。' }]}
  source="sources.list(5) · aurora 发行说明 §4.7"
/>
```

<a id="status-grid"></a>

## 15 · Status grid `<StatusGrid />`

**Position**: Status · state of each object in a set; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `arch`

6–10 small cards, each with a name and a status label (supported / new / partial / unsupported, renameable); a legend at the top counts each status.

**Use when**

- Support or readiness of a set of objects: architectures, platforms, functions, modules
- The audience should see at a glance how many are fine and how many are not

**Avoid**

- Each object must be compared on several dimensions: use Matrix
- Only two states and fewer than 4 objects: use VerdictColumns

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- tiles 6–10; 6 in 3 columns, 7–8 in 4, 9–10 in 5
- name ≤ 10 characters (monospace); sub ≤ 12 chars
- note ≤ 24 chars, only for partial or unsupported
- labels ≤ 4 chars each

**Interaction**: Cards fade in one by one and lift on hover.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `tiles` | `{ name, sub?, status: 'ok'\|'new'\|'partial'\|'off', note? }[]` | yes | Cards |
| `labels` | `Partial<Record<Status, string>>` |  | Rename the four states for this slide, e.g. { ok: "ready", off: "blocked" } |

**Example**

```tsx
<StatusGrid
  kicker="架构支持"
  title="七个官方架构，riscv64 首次加入"
  labels={{ ok: '官方支持', new: '首次支持', partial: '受限', off: '已移除' }}
  tiles={[
    { name: 'amd64', sub: '64 位 x86', status: 'ok' },
    { name: 'riscv64', sub: '64 位 RISC-V', status: 'new', note: '首个正式支持的版本' },
    { name: 'i386', sub: '32 位 x86', status: 'partial', note: '无官方内核和安装器' },
    { name: 'mipsel', sub: '32 位 MIPS', status: 'off', note: '不再受支持' },
  ]}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §2.1 · §5.1"
/>
```

<a id="spec-sheet"></a>

## 16 · Version table `<SpecSheet />`

**Position**: Change · old and new versions of a set of components; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `versions`

A grouped table of 2–3 columns, one component per row from old to new version; rows with a major version change are marked with accent colour and a dot.

**Use when**

- A batch of version numbers in a distro, dependency or platform upgrade
- The audience should spot which ones are major jumps

**Avoid**

- Fewer than 3 components: use KeyValue or CompareTable
- Values are behaviour descriptions, not versions: use CompareTable

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- groups 2–3, 3–6 rows each, row counts within 1 of each other
- name ≤ 12 characters; from / to ≤ 10 characters
- major marks a major version change only, never every row

**Interaction**: Groups fade in one by one; hovering a group lights its icon.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `columns` | `[string, string]` | yes | Column names for the two versions |
| `groups` | `{ label, icon?, rows: { name, from?, to, major? }[] }[]` | yes | Groups |

**Example**

```tsx
<SpecSheet
  kicker="软件栈"
  title="工具链、系统和服务一起换代"
  columns={['Lumen 12', 'Lumen 13']}
  groups={[
    { label: '工具链', icon: 'code', rows: [{ name: 'GCC', from: '12.2', to: '14.2', major: true }, { name: 'Python 3', from: '3.11', to: '3.13' }] },
    { label: '服务', icon: 'server', rows: [{ name: 'PostgreSQL', from: '15', to: '17', major: true }, { name: 'Nginx', from: '1.22', to: '1.26' }] },
  ]}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §2.2.10"
/>
```

<a id="cycle"></a>

## 17 · Cycle diagram `<Cycle />`

**Position**: Mechanism · stages that loop; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `cycle`

A ring on the left with 3–6 stages clockwise around it and a small accent dot travelling the ring; one number or word in the centre; each stage explained on the right.

**Use when**

- Stages repeat: development cycle, growth flywheel, review loop
- The last step feeding back into the first is the point

**Avoid**

- Stages run once and do not return to the start: use FlowDiagram or StepTrack
- More than 6 stages: merge similar ones

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- nodes 3–6, clockwise from the top
- title ≤ 8 characters (shown beside the ring); body ≤ 36 chars; tag ≤ 14 characters
- hub.value ≤ 6 characters; hub.caption ≤ 12 chars
- focus at most 1

**Interaction**: Stages pop in one by one and the accent dot keeps circling; hovering a stage turns its icon tile slightly.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `nodes` | `{ icon, title, body, tag? }[]` | yes | Stages |
| `hub` | `{ value, caption }` | yes | Ring centre |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Stage to emphasise |

**Example**

```tsx
<Cycle
  kicker="增长飞轮"
  title="AARRR：五个环节，首尾相接"
  hub={{ value: 'AARRR', caption: 'Dave McClure，2007' }}
  nodes={[
    { icon: 'search', title: '获客', tag: 'Acquisition', body: '客户从哪些渠道第一次接触到产品。' },
    { icon: 'bolt', title: '激活', tag: 'Activation', body: '第一次用就得到价值。' },
    { icon: 'refresh', title: '留存', tag: 'Retention', body: '客户持续回来使用。' },
  ]}
  focus={1}
  source="Dave McClure · Startup Metrics for Pirates（2007）"
/>
```

<a id="faq"></a>

## 18 · FAQ `<Faq />`

**Position**: Questions · questions the audience asks most; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `faq`

Two columns of questions and answers, each with a serif number Q1–Q6 on the left, the question in bold, one or two sentences of answer below and an optional monospace tag.

**Use when**

- The 4–6 questions that come up before an upgrade, a launch or a purchase
- Every question can be answered in one or two sentences

**Avoid**

- An answer needs a whole slide: give it its own slide
- You are really checking whether a claim is true: use ClaimCards

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 4 or 6
- q ≤ 24 chars; a ≤ 56 chars
- tag ≤ 24 characters: a command, section number or parameter

**Interaction**: Q&A pairs fade in one by one; hovering turns the number accent and moves it up.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ q, a, tag? }[]` | yes | Questions and answers |

**Example**

```tsx
<Faq
  kicker="常见问题"
  title="升级前常被问到的六件事"
  items={[
    { q: '能跳过版本直接升级吗？', a: '不能。只支持从 Lumen 12 升级。', tag: '§4.2.1' },
    { q: '/boot 要留多大？', a: '分区至少 768 MB，并留出约 300 MB 空闲空间。', tag: '§5.1.5' },
  ]}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §4 · §5"
/>
```

<a id="lanes"></a>

## 19 · Swimlane board `<Lanes />`

**Position**: Roadmap · now / next / later; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `sunset`

3–4 lanes, each with a time range and 2–4 cards below; one lane takes a pale accent background to mark what to do now.

**Use when**

- Roadmap: now, next, later
- Grouping by status: removed, to be removed, deprecated

**Avoid**

- There are exact dates and durations: use Gantt
- Projects depend on each other: use FlowDiagram

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- lanes 3 or 4
- label ≤ 6 chars; sub ≤ 12 characters
- 2–4 cards per lane, counts within 1 of each other
- card.title ≤ 14 chars; body ≤ 28 chars; tag ≤ 12 characters

**Interaction**: Lanes appear in order and cards drop in one by one; hovering a card lifts it.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `lanes` | `{ label, sub?, icon?, cards: { title, body?, tag? }[] }[]` | yes | Lanes |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Lane to emphasise, default 0 |

**Example**

```tsx
<Lanes
  kicker="正在退场"
  title="这些东西正在离开 Lumen"
  focus={1}
  lanes={[
    { label: '已移除', sub: 'Lumen 13', icon: 'close', cards: [{ title: 'mipsel 与 mips64el', body: '不再受支持' }] },
    { label: '将移除', sub: 'Lumen 14', icon: 'hourglass', cards: [{ title: 'armel 架构', body: 'aurora 是最后一个版本' }] },
    { label: '已弃用', icon: 'history', cards: [{ title: '单行 sources.list', body: '最早 2029 年后移除' }] },
  ]}
  source="aurora 发行说明 §5"
/>
```

<a id="line-chart"></a>

## 20 · Line chart `<LineChart />`

**Position**: Trend · change over time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/release` — `packages`

1–3 lines; one focus line in accent with an area fill and value labels and its last point enlarged; 1–2 annotations hang below the line and connect to data points with a dashed line; the right column can carry interpretation.

**Use when**

- Change across 4–12 points in time: releases, years, quarters, months
- A point in time needs a callout

**Avoid**

- Only 2–3 points: use MetricBars or DeltaBars
- Different objects at one moment are compared: use MetricBars
- Periods add and subtract into each other: use Waterfall

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- x 4–12 labels, ≤ 6 characters each
- series 1–3 with only one focus; write approximate values in labels as in the original, e.g. 43,000+
- annotations ≤ 2, text ≤ 16 chars, never on the last point
- yFrom defaults to 0; say so in the lead when the axis start is raised
- notes 2–3: key ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 30 chars

**Interaction**: The line draws from left to right, data points pop in one by one and the annotations fade in last.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `x` | `string[]` | yes | Horizontal axis labels |
| `series` | `{ name, values, labels?, focus? }[]` | yes | Data series; null in values means missing |
| `yFrom` | `number` |  | Vertical axis start, default 0 |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit, written below the horizontal axis |
| `digits` | `number` |  | Decimal places for automatic labels |
| `annotations` | `{ at, text }[]` |  | Annotations; at is the horizontal axis index |
| `notes` | `{ key, text }[]` |  | Interpretation in the right column |

**Example**

```tsx
<LineChart
  kicker="十年"
  title="十年，软件包多了约六成"
  x={['2015', '2017', '2019', '2021', '2023', '2025']}
  series={[{ name: '软件包总数', focus: true, values: [43000, 51000, 59000, 59551, 64419, 69830], labels: ['43,000+', '51,000+', '59,000+'] }]}
  unit="个"
  annotations={[{ at: 1, text: '8–10 公告只给约数' }]}
  source="Lumen 各版本发布公告"
/>
```

<a id="agenda"></a>

## 21 · Contents `<Agenda />`

**Position**: Navigation · after the cover; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `agenda`

Title and lead on the left; 3–6 chapter rows on the right with number, icon, chapter name, one line of description and the opening page. Every row is a jump button.

**Use when**

- A deck of more than 15 slides with several chapters
- The audience should see the shape first, or you need to jump back to a chapter mid-talk

**Avoid**

- A short deck under 10 slides: the contents alone takes a tenth of it
- Only 2 chapters: say so in the cover lead

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–6, in deck order
- title ≤ 10 chars; body one line, ≤ 26 chars
- page is the number of the chapter's first slide (from 1); update it after reordering
- meta ≤ 20 characters, e.g. "27 slides · 4 chapters"
- No source field: the contents states no facts

**Interaction**: Hover a row: the background brightens, the icon tile takes the accent background and turns slightly, the arrow moves right; click to jump to that chapter's first slide.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Usually "Agenda" |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Page title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead, ≤ 60 chars |
| `items` | `{ icon, title, body, page }[]` | yes | icon comes from the icon index |
| `meta` | `string` |  | Small text in the bottom left |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Defaults to "Click any section to jump to it" |

**Example**

```tsx
<Agenda
  kicker="目录"
  title={['四章，', '从原理到行动']}
  meta="27 页 · 4 章"
  items={[
    { icon: 'brain', title: '认识 GEO', body: 'GEO 是什么，AI 回答怎样拼出来', page: 3 },
    { icon: 'routing', title: '让 AI 读得到', body: '爬虫、渲染、摘要控制与结构化数据', page: 9 },
    { icon: 'map', title: '国内平台与合规', body: '各平台的信源偏好与监管时间线', page: 16 },
  ]}
/>
```

<a id="section-break"></a>

## 22 · Section break `<SectionBreak />`

**Position**: Section break · first slide of each chapter; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `ch2`

A large chapter name and one line of lead, with the chapter's slides listed below; on the right a large accent icon on a double tray and a serif chapter number. The icon drifts slightly with the mouse.

**Use when**

- Pairs with Agenda; one at the start of each chapter
- Where the talk needs a pause or a change of subject

**Avoid**

- A short deck without an Agenda
- Two section breaks in a row
- A chapter with one slide of content: no separate break needed

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title 1–2 lines, ≤ 6 chars per line (128u size)
- lead ≤ 44 chars
- contents 2–4 items, ≤ 12 chars each, the titles of the chapter's slides
- Pick an icon that stands for the whole chapter; it takes the accent colour
- No source field

**Interaction**: Concentric circles unfold in order and the chapter number rises digit by digit; the icon drifts with the mouse.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | For example "Chapter Two" |
| `index` | `number` | yes | Chapter number, shown as 01, 02… |
| `icon` | `IconName` | yes | Chapter icon |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Chapter name |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One sentence on what this chapter covers |
| `contents` | `string[]` | yes | Slides in this chapter |

**Example**

```tsx
<SectionBreak
  kicker="第二章"
  index={2}
  icon="routing"
  title={['让 AI', '读得到你']}
  lead="内容再好，爬虫进不来、读不到正文，也不会出现在回答里。"
  contents={['六层访问检查', 'AI 爬虫一览', 'JavaScript 渲染', '结构化数据']}
/>
```

<a id="quote-hero"></a>

## 23 · Definition and quotation `<QuoteHero />`

**Position**: Concept · define a term / quote a line; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `definition`

A definition or quotation set centred at 76u, with keywords highlighted in accent one by one; three notes with icons below add the source, the stance or a caveat.

**Use when**

- One definition of a term for the whole deck
- A weighty original line carries the argument

**Avoid**

- The sentence runs past 64 chars: take it apart in NumberedColumns
- A quotation without a reliable source

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- quote ≤ 64 chars; pass an array for line breaks, at most 3 lines × 22 chars
- marks 1–3, each an exact substring of quote
- cite ≤ 30 characters, the original source
- notes exactly 3: label ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 36 chars
- Quote the original word for word; do not paraphrase and add quotation marks

**Interaction**: Keyword highlights sweep in from the left one by one; hovering a note turns its icon secondary colour to accent.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `quote` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Definition or quotation |
| `marks` | `string[]` |  | Keywords to highlight |
| `cite` | `string` | yes | Original source; a dash is added before it |
| `notes` | `{ icon, label, text }[3]` |  | Three notes |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source in the note |

**Example**

```tsx
<QuoteHero
  kicker="定义"
  quote={['GEO 指提高内容在 AI 生成回答中', '被呈现和引用的频率与准确性。']}
  marks={['频率', '准确性']}
  cite="示例知识库 · 基础概念 / 生成式引擎优化"
  notes={[
    { icon: 'academic', label: '术语出处', text: '一篇 2023 年的论文。' },
    { icon: 'search', label: 'Seekr 的立场', text: '针对其生成式 AI 功能做优化，仍然属于 SEO。' },
    { icon: 'shield-warning', label: '研究的提醒', text: '多数优化方法基本无效。' },
  ]}
  source="示例知识库 · 基础概念 / GEO"
/>
```

<a id="flow-diagram"></a>

## 24 · Flow diagram `<FlowDiagram />`

**Position**: Mechanism · how it runs; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `answer`

3–5 icon nodes run left to right with dashed arrows that keep flowing; one node can be marked as the focus and an input band can sit at the bottom.

**Use when**

- Explaining a system or mechanism: how data flows, how a request is handled
- One step is within the audience's reach; point at it with focus and band

**Avoid**

- The audience will follow the steps: use StepTrack
- The flow branches or loops: split across two slides or draw a dedicated figure

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- nodes 3–5; title ≤ 8 chars; body ≤ 30 chars
- parts at most 3 per node, ≤ 12 chars each
- focus marks at most 1 node
- band.items 3–4: title ≤ 6 chars, body ≤ 14 chars; label ≤ 10 chars
- Say it is a simplified diagram in the lead or the source

**Interaction**: Arrows grow first and then keep flowing; the focus node gets an accent halo.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `nodes` | `{ icon, title, body, parts?, focus? }[]` | yes | Flow nodes |
| `band` | `{ label, items: { icon, title, body }[] }` |  | Input band at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<FlowDiagram
  kicker="AI 回答"
  title="AI 怎样拼出一个回答"
  lead="各助手只公开了流程的一部分，下图是简化示意。"
  nodes={[
    { icon: 'user-speak', title: '顾客提问', body: '顾客用自然语言提出一个问题。' },
    { icon: 'branch', title: '拆成子查询', body: '改写成多个子查询，分别检索。' },
    { icon: 'database', title: '检索候选', body: '从三类来源找候选内容。', parts: ['搜索索引', '平台自家内容', '实时抓取'], focus: true },
    { icon: 'chat-quote', title: '附链接的回答', body: '挑选内容写成回答，附上来源。' },
  ]}
  band={{ label: '你能影响的来源', items: [{ icon: 'home', title: '你的官网', body: '事实、价格、常见问题' }] }}
  source="示例知识库 · 图示「AI 助手如何拼出一个回答」"
/>
```

<a id="claim-cards"></a>

## 25 · Claim check `<ClaimCards />`

**Position**: Claim check · a verdict per claim; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `claims`

4 or 6 cards, each with a common claim, a verdict stamp and one line of evidence. A claim judged false is struck through after the stamp lands.

**Use when**

- Clearing up a misunderstanding that circulates in the field
- Giving each claim in a set a level of evidence

**Avoid**

- Only one or two: put them in another slide's lead
- A verdict has no source: the whole value of this slide is the evidence

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- claims exactly 4 or 6 (2 × 2 or 3 × 2)
- claim ≤ 22 chars, written the way people say it; the template adds quotation marks
- verdict one of five: false (wrong), unsupported (no evidence), disputed (contested), partial (partly backed), supported (backed)
- note ≤ 36 chars, the single most important piece of evidence
- tag ≤ 16 characters, an English topic word

**Interaction**: Stamps land one by one; a false claim is then struck through with an accent line. Hovering a card lifts it.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `claims` | `{ claim, verdict, note, tag? }[]` | yes | verdict sets the stamp colour and icon |

**Example**

```tsx
<ClaimCards
  kicker="说法核查"
  title="六条流行说法，都没有依据"
  claims={[
    { tag: 'llms.txt', claim: '加上 llms.txt 能提升 AI 可见度', verdict: 'unsupported', note: '已发布的文件 97% 一个月内从未被请求。' },
    { tag: 'duplicate', claim: '重复内容会让网站受罚', verdict: 'false', note: '重复页面被过滤，不是惩罚。' },
    // …共 4 或 6 条
  ]}
  source="示例知识库 · 说法核查"
/>
```

<a id="layer-stack"></a>

## 26 · Layer check `<LayerStack />`

**Position**: Troubleshooting · confirm layer by layer from outside in; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `layers`

4–6 layers stacked like steps; each layer has a number, icon, layer name and the item to check, and the bottom layer is the accent-coloured “outcome” layer. Hovering one layer fades the others.

**Use when**

- Any layer that fails blocks the rest of the chain: network, permissions, release pipeline
- The troubleshooting checklist has to be worked from the top down

**Avoid**

- The items have no order or dependency: use Checklist
- Each layer needs a command-line example: use the cmd of Checklist

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- layers 4–6, the outermost layer on top
- name ≤ 12 chars; check one or two lines, ≤ 30 chars
- outcome.text ≤ 16 chars

**Interaction**: Hover a layer: it turns white and shifts right, the others drop to 45% opacity.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `layers` | `{ icon, name, check }[]` | yes | From outside in |
| `outcome` | `{ icon, text }` |  | Result once every layer passes |

**Example**

```tsx
<LayerStack
  kicker="访问路径"
  title={['每一层都可能', '挡住下一层']}
  layers={[
    { icon: 'cpu', name: 'AI 爬虫发起请求', check: '在日志里核实真爬虫：反向 DNS 或官方 IP 段' },
    { icon: 'shield', name: 'CDN / WAF / 防火墙', check: '已验证的搜索爬虫没有被拦截或挑战' },
    { icon: 'document', name: 'robots.txt', check: '放行搜索类爬虫' },
  ]}
  outcome={{ icon: 'chat-quote', text: '出现在 AI 回答中' }}
  source="示例知识库 · 图示「访问路径」"
/>
```

<a id="matrix"></a>

## 27 · Matrix comparison `<Matrix />`

**Position**: Side-by-side · marks in a row × column grid; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `crawlers`

Rows are objects, columns are categories, and cells hold a monospace mark or “—”. One column can take an accent background as the focus of the slide.

**Use when**

- “Which name sits in which category”: vendor × use, platform × interface, version × feature
- Cells hold short marks rather than sentences

**Avoid**

- Cells need sentences: use CompareTable or split the slide
- Only an old and a new column: use CompareTable

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- columns 2–4: label ≤ 8 chars, note ≤ 14 chars
- rows 3–6: label ≤ 10 chars, sub ≤ 16 characters
- Cells ≤ 18 characters; write null when empty
- focus at most 1 column; footnote ≤ 60 chars
- A one-line title leaves the slide more room; with a two-line title keep at most 5 data rows

**Interaction**: Hover a row: the whole row takes a dark background; hover a cell: the mark turns white.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `columns` | `{ icon?, label, note? }[]` | yes | Column headers |
| `rows` | `{ label, sub?, cells: (string \| null)[] }[]` | yes | cells match columns one to one |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Index of the accent column |
| `footnote` | `string` |  | Note below the table |

**Example**

```tsx
<Matrix
  kicker="AI 爬虫"
  title="三类爬虫，三个独立的决定"
  columns={[
    { icon: 'search', label: '搜索收录', note: '封了就从回答中消失' },
    { icon: 'user', label: '用户发起访问' },
    { icon: 'cpu', label: '模型训练', note: '不影响搜索收录' },
  ]}
  rows={[
    { label: 'Orbit', sub: 'AskBot', cells: ['OB-SearchBot', 'AskBot-User', 'OrbitBot'] },
    { label: 'Pexo', cells: ['PexoBot', 'Pexo-User', null] },
  ]}
  focus={0}
  source="示例知识库 · 为 AI 爬虫配置 robots.txt"
/>
```

<a id="icon-grid"></a>

## 28 · Icon cards `<IconGrid />`

**Position**: Parallel · 6–8 objects; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `platforms`

A 3 × 2 or 4 × 2 grid of icon cards; each card has an icon, title, description and a tag in the top right. On hover the card lifts and the icon lights up.

**Use when**

- 6 or 8 parallel objects: platforms, products, roles, regions
- Each object takes one or two sentences

**Avoid**

- 3–4 objects: use NumberedColumns, each item gets more room
- Objects are compared by a number: use MetricBars or Matrix

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items exactly 6 or 8
- title ≤ 8 chars; body 18–40 chars, keep the cards close in length
- tag ≤ 12 characters; omit it when there is none, do not invent one
- Pick icons by meaning; do not repeat one on the same slide

**Interaction**: Hover a card: it lifts 6u, the shadow deepens and the icon tile takes the accent background.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ icon, title, body, tag? }[]` | yes | Cards |

**Example**

```tsx
<IconGrid
  kicker="国内平台"
  title="每个助手，偏爱自家生态"
  items={[
    { icon: 'video', title: '小禾', tag: '禾流集团', body: '偏向禾流号、禾视频和禾百科。' },
    { icon: 'chat', title: '青柚', tag: '柚子集团', body: '偏向柚子号和柚子新闻。' },
    // …共 6 或 8 个
  ]}
  source="示例知识库 · 国内 GEO 实战（行业观察）"
/>
```

<a id="big-stat"></a>

## 29 · Single big number `<BigStat />`

**Position**: Evidence · one number carries the slide; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `fanout`

A very large number on the left (rising digit by digit, drifting with the pointer) with its definition; on the right the title, lead and 2–3 supporting numbers.

**Use when**

- One number is the argument: 97%, 9.34%, 1/100
- A visual accent is needed between a run of text slides

**Avoid**

- Several numbers matter equally: use MetricBars
- The number only means something next to others: use DeltaBars or Dumbbell
- Used more than 3 times in one deck

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- figure ≤ 6 characters (unit included); the shorter it is, the larger the type
- caption ≤ 30 chars; say what the number counts
- support 2–3 items: value ≤ 8 characters, label ≤ 24 chars
- title 2 lines × ≤ 8 chars (right column 640u wide)

**Interaction**: Digits rise out of a mask one by one and drift slightly with the pointer.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `figure` | `string` | yes | The big number |
| `caption` | `string` | yes | Definition of the number |
| `support` | `{ value, label }[]` |  | Supporting numbers |

**Example**

```tsx
<BigStat
  kicker="查询扇出"
  title={['排在前面，', '不等于被引用']}
  figure="97%"
  caption="AskBot 检索到的页面里，不在 Seekr 前 50 名的比例"
  support={[{ value: '99%', label: '不在 Findo 前 30 名' }, { value: '93%', label: 'Zeno 引用的页面不在 Seekr 前 50 名' }]}
  source="示例知识库 · 查询扇出"
/>
```

<a id="delta-bars"></a>

## 30 · Positive and negative change `<DeltaBars />`

**Position**: Evidence · up or down; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `schema`

Diverging bars around a zero line: positive values to the right in the accent colour, negative to the left in ink. The left column holds the argument, an optional pull figure and the source.

**Use when**

- Change after a test or a change: citation rate, conversion rate, time taken
- The same treatment moves in different directions on different platforms

**Avoid**

- All values are positive totals: use MetricBars
- Both the before and the after value need showing: use Dumbbell

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 2–6: label ≤ 10 chars, sub ≤ 14 characters
- unit ≤ 4 characters; scale is the half-width of the axis, an integer slightly above the largest absolute value
- ends each ≤ 6 chars, for example “fewer citations / more citations”
- When the change is not significant, say so in the title or lead; do not let the bars imply an effect

**Interaction**: Bars grow out from the zero line to both sides.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `rows` | `{ label, sub?, value }[]` | yes | value carries its sign |
| `unit` | `string` |  | for example '%' |
| `scale` | `number` |  | Half-width of the axis |
| `ends` | `[string, string]` |  | Labels for the left and right ends |
| `pull` | `{ figure, text }` |  | Pull figure in the left column |

**Example**

```tsx
<DeltaBars
  kicker="结构化数据"
  title={['加了 schema，', '引用没有提升']}
  rows={[
    { label: 'AskBot', value: 2.2 },
    { label: 'AI 模式', sub: 'Seekr', value: 2.4 },
    { label: 'AI 概览', sub: 'Seekr', value: -4.6 },
  ]}
  unit="%"
  scale={6}
  ends={['引用减少', '引用增加']}
  source="示例知识库 · 结构化数据与 AI 搜索（某工具商）"
/>
```

<a id="dumbbell"></a>

## 31 · Two-point comparison `<Dumbbell />`

**Position**: Evidence · two ways to measure one metric; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `api-app`

Each row has two points (hollow ink a, solid accent b) joined by a gradient line; the length of the line is the gap, and the difference is listed on the right.

**Use when**

- One metric under two conditions: API and App, last year and this year, control and test
- The gap itself is the argument

**Avoid**

- Only one condition: use MetricBars
- More than 5 rows: split the slide or keep only the most telling rows

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 2–5: label ≤ 8 chars, sub ≤ 14 characters
- legend two items, each ≤ 8 chars
- max defaults to 100; unit defaults to %, and the gap is written as “percentage points”
- footnote ≤ 60 chars

**Interaction**: Point a appears first, the line grows from a to b, then point b pops in.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `legend` | `[string, string]` | yes | Names of a and b |
| `rows` | `{ label, sub?, a, b }[]` | yes | The two measurements |
| `max` | `number` |  | Upper end of the axis |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit of the values |
| `footnote` | `string` |  | Note below the chart |

**Example**

```tsx
<Dumbbell
  kicker="在哪里测"
  title={['API 测到的，', '不是用户看到的']}
  legend={['API 推荐率', 'App 命中率']}
  rows={[
    { label: '通义千帆', a: 78, b: 53 },
    { label: '深言', a: 69, b: 1 },
    { label: '青柚', a: 3, b: 99 },
  ]}
  source="示例知识库 · 用提示词抽样测量 AI 可见度"
/>
```

<a id="range-bars"></a>

## 32 · Range estimate `<RangeBars />`

**Position**: Method · how reliable the number is; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `sampling`

Each row has a point estimate and its error range; the narrower the range, the more reliable the number. One row can be highlighted, and the left column can hold an example block of “how to write the result”.

**Use when**

- Explaining sampling error, confidence intervals, forecast ranges
- Showing “how large the sample has to be”

**Avoid**

- The data has no error range: use MetricBars
- The range is your own estimate and is not marked as one: write the calculation in source

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 3–6: label ≤ 8 characters, note ≤ 8 characters
- axis.ticks 3–5; focusLabel ≤ 8 chars
- example.text ≤ 48 characters (two monospace lines)

**Interaction**: Ranges expand from the centre to both sides.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `rows` | `{ label, value, low, high, note? }[]` | yes | Point estimate and range |
| `axis` | `{ from, to, ticks }` | yes | Axis range and ticks |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit of the ticks |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Index of the highlighted row |
| `focusLabel` | `string` |  | Label on the highlighted row |
| `example` | `{ label, text }` |  | Example block in the left column |

**Example**

```tsx
<RangeBars
  kicker="抽样"
  title={['只问几次，', '测到的多是噪声']}
  rows={[
    { label: 'N = 5', value: 50, low: 28, high: 72, note: '±22' },
    { label: 'N = 30', value: 50, low: 41, high: 59, note: '±9' },
    { label: 'N = 100', value: 50, low: 45, high: 55, note: '±5' },
  ]}
  axis={{ from: 0, to: 100, ticks: [0, 25, 50, 75, 100] }}
  unit="%"
  focus={1}
  focusLabel="最低要求"
  source="标准误按 √(p(1−p)/N) 计算"
/>
```

<a id="gantt"></a>

## 33 · Gantt chart `<Gantt />`

**Position**: Plan · work laid out by week; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `plan`

A relative timeline in weeks, sprints or days, with optional month groups above, one work stream per row and deliverables that can be highlighted.

**Use when**

- A 30–90 day action plan or a pilot schedule
- Several work streams overlap and the order has to be clear

**Avoid**

- A long period with real dates (support window, contract term): use Timeline
- Only order, no duration: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- axis 6–16 units; groups 2–4 spans
- rows 3–7: label ≤ 12 chars; sort by start
- With a bar shorter than 2 units the “5–9” label inside it does not fit
- footnote ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: Bars grow from the left one after another; hovering a row lifts its bar.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `axis` | `{ from, to, unit }` | yes | Unit axis, both ends included |
| `groups` | `{ label, from, to }[]` |  | Groups above |
| `rows` | `{ label, from, to, focus? }[]` | yes | Work streams |
| `footnote` | `string` |  | Note below the chart |

**Example**

```tsx
<Gantt
  kicker="90 天"
  title="第一个 90 天，按周排开"
  axis={{ from: 1, to: 13, unit: '周' }}
  groups={[{ label: '第 1 个月', from: 1, to: 4 }, { label: '第 2 个月', from: 5, to: 8 }, { label: '第 3 个月', from: 9, to: 13 }]}
  rows={[
    { label: '基线测量', from: 2, to: 4 },
    { label: '改写 3–5 个核心页面', from: 5, to: 9 },
    { label: '复测并汇报', from: 10, to: 13, focus: true },
  ]}
  source="示例知识库 · 从零建立品牌"
/>
```

<a id="milestones"></a>

## 34 · Event timeline `<Milestones />`

**Position**: Background · events in date order; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `regulation`

4–7 events on a horizontal axis, alternating above and below it, each with a date, a two-line title and a type tag; one key event can be highlighted.

**Use when**

- A run of dated events: policy, standards, releases
- Showing how things got here step by step

**Avoid**

- Gaps must follow real time: this template spaces events evenly, use Timeline
- More than 7 events: keep only the key ones

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- events 4–7, sorted by date; spaced evenly, not to a time scale
- date ≤ 10 characters; title ≤ 24 chars (two lines)
- tag ≤ 8 chars; name the kind of event (regulation, standard, event)
- focus at most 1

**Interaction**: The axis grows from the left, event points pop in one by one, cards slide in from the axis.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `events` | `{ date, title, tag?, focus? }[]` | yes | Events |

**Example**

```tsx
<Milestones
  kicker="监管"
  title="监管针对投毒，不针对 GEO"
  events={[
    { date: '2023-08-15', title: '《生成式人工智能服务管理暂行办法》施行', tag: '部门规章' },
    { date: '2026-03', title: '媒体调查曝光 GEO 投毒', tag: '媒体调查' },
    { date: '2026-04', title: '专项行动把恶意 GEO 列为数据投毒', tag: '执法专项', focus: true },
    { date: '2026-09-14', title: '五项 GEO 团体标准公布', tag: '自愿性标准' },
  ]}
  source="示例知识库 · 中国 GEO 监管"
/>
```

<a id="quote-wall"></a>

## 35 · Quote wall `<QuoteWall />`

**Position**: Support · a few verbatim lines; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `quotes`

3 or 5 verbatim quotes: the first in a large dark card on the left, the rest in a grid; each has the speaker, the source and a topic tag below it.

**Use when**

- Exact wording from platforms, regulators or authoritative documents
- Exact wording from customer interviews, traceable to the transcript

**Avoid**

- One quote only: use QuoteHero
- It is your summary or paraphrase: put it in body text, without quotation marks
- The original cannot be found: do not use it

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- quotes 3 or 5; the first one is the large card
- First quote ≤ 50 chars, the rest ≤ 40 chars; copy verbatim, without quotation marks
- who ≤ 16 characters; where ≤ 24 characters; tag ≤ 12 characters
- source must name where the original text comes from

**Interaction**: Cards appear one after another; hovering a small card lifts it and draws an accent border.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `quotes` | `{ text, who, where?, tag? }[]` | yes | Quotes; the first one is the focus |

**Example**

```tsx
<QuoteWall
  kicker="官方原话"
  title="平台自己是怎么说的"
  quotes={[
    { text: 'Seekr 不接受付费来更频繁地抓取网站或提升其排名。', who: 'Seekr', where: 'Seekr 搜索的工作原理', tag: '付费排名' },
    { text: '在这些方面中，可信度最重要。', who: 'Seekr', where: '创建有用的内容', tag: 'E-E-A-T' },
    { text: '…', who: 'Findo', where: '网站管理员工具帮助' },
  ]}
  source="Seekr 站长文档 · Findo 站长帮助（虚构）"
/>
```

<a id="before-after"></a>

## 36 · Before and after `<BeforeAfter />`

**Position**: Proof · two states of the same view; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `csr`

Two screenshots of the same size stacked together with a draggable divider; one label in each corner and the reading in the right column. On entry the divider slides in from the right to its start position.

**Use when**

- Before and after a redesign or an improvement
- The same page under two conditions: JavaScript on and off, desktop and mobile, two versions

**Avoid**

- The two images are composed differently: show them apart or use Screenshot
- The difference needs words rather than eyes: use CompareTable

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- Both images must match in size and composition; set ratio to the real aspect ratio
- label ≤ 10 chars
- notes 2–3: key ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 30 chars
- Must be real screenshots; source says how they were taken

**Interaction**: Drag or click the frame to move the divider; with the handle focused ← → nudges it and does not change slide.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `before` | `{ label, src }` | yes | Left of the divider |
| `after` | `{ label, src }` | yes | Right of the divider |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Description for screen readers |
| `ratio` | `number` |  | Aspect ratio, default 16/9 |
| `start` | `number` |  | Divider start position 0–100, default 50 |
| `notes` | `{ key, text }[]` |  | Reading in the right column |

**Example**

```tsx
import jsOn from './assets/deck-js-on.webp'
import jsOff from './assets/deck-js-off.webp'

<BeforeAfter
  kicker="实测"
  title="同一页，关掉 JavaScript 之后"
  before={{ label: '浏览器 · 执行 JS', src: jsOn }}
  after={{ label: '爬虫 · 不执行 JS', src: jsOff }}
  alt="启用与禁用 JavaScript 时的同一页面"
  source="实测：Playwright 分别启用和禁用 JavaScript 截图"
/>
```

<a id="screenshot"></a>

## 37 · Annotated screenshot `<Screenshot />`

**Position**: Explanation · product interface; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/geo` — `entry`

A screenshot in a browser frame on the left with 3–5 numbered dots on it; the right column lists the notes under the same numbers. Hovering a dot or a note highlights both sides.

**Use when**

- Introducing a product interface, a site structure, how to read a report
- The audience has to find a place on the real interface

**Avoid**

- No real screenshot: do not pass a diagram off as one
- Two states need comparing: use BeforeAfter
- More than 5 points: split into two slides

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- callouts 3–5; x and y are percentages, dots at least 8 apart
- title ≤ 10 chars; body ≤ 36 chars
- Keep images in the deck folder and import them; prefer WebP, ≤ 200 KB each
- Set ratio to the real aspect ratio; url is optional and shows the browser address bar when set

**Interaction**: Dots pop in one by one with a breathing ring; hovering a dot or its note highlights both. In build steps the dot and its note appear together.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `src` | `string` | yes | The imported image |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Image description |
| `ratio` | `number` |  | Aspect ratio, default 16/10 |
| `url` | `string` |  | Text in the browser address bar |
| `callouts` | `{ x, y, title, body }[]` | yes | Numbered callouts |

**Example**

```tsx
import entryShot from './assets/shot-kit-entry.webp'

<Screenshot
  kicker="查证"
  title="查一个模板，先看这五处"
  src={entryShot}
  alt="组件库条目页面截图"
  ratio={1600 / 1000}
  url="#/kit"
  callouts={[
    { x: 14, y: 12, title: '切换器', body: '图标、强调色和风格都能切换。' },
    { x: 31, y: 14, title: '模板名称', body: '中文名和组件名并列。' },
    { x: 60, y: 60, title: '实时预览', body: '预览就是真实的一页。' },
  ]}
  source="本项目截图"
/>
```

<a id="funnel"></a>

## 38 · Conversion funnel `<Funnel />`

**Position**: Diagnosis · stage-by-stage conversion and the stage to fix first; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `funnel`

Centred bars that narrow stage by stage (evenly, not to scale) with counts on the right; the conversion rate between stages is worked out automatically and one chosen stage is marked in accent; the right column says why to fix that one first.

**Use when**

- A stage-by-stage flow such as sign-up, activation, payment
- Saying which stage to fix first, not just showing numbers

**Avoid**

- The stages do not nest (a stage is not a subset of the one before): use MetricBars
- Two stages only: use a BigStat

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- stages 4–6, values falling at every step
- label ≤ 8 chars; note ≤ 18 chars
- leak points at the step to fix first (from stage leak to the next); leakLabel ≤ 6 chars
- aside.title ≤ 14 chars; aside.body ≤ 60 chars
- Bar width only shows order; read the numbers and rates for data

**Interaction**: Bars expand from the centre, rate labels appear one by one; hovering a stage darkens its bar.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `stages` | `{ label, value, note? }[]` | yes | The stages |
| `leak` | `number` |  | The step to fix first |
| `leakLabel` | `string` |  | Label for that step, default “Priority fix” |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit of the values |
| `aside` | `{ title, body }` |  | Note in the right column |

**Example**

```tsx
<Funnel
  kicker="转化漏斗"
  title="先修最漏的那一级"
  unit="人"
  stages={[
    { label: '注册试用', value: 2400 },
    { label: '激活', note: '首次出图', value: 960 },
    { label: '预约演示', value: 230 },
    { label: '付费', value: 86 },
  ]}
  leak={1}
  aside={{ title: '激活后尽快跟进', body: '…' }}
  source="示意：示例数据"
/>
```

<a id="quadrant"></a>

## 39 · Positioning quadrant `<Quadrant />`

**Position**: Positioning · two-dimensional competitive map; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `map`

A 2 × 2 plane on the left with a label at each end of both axes and four quadrants that can be named; 3–7 points, the focus (usually you) with a pulsing ring; 2–3 notes on the right.

**Use when**

- Competitor positioning, product portfolio, priority (impact × effort)
- Showing which space is still empty

**Avoid**

- Point coordinates have no basis and are not marked illustrative
- Only one dimension is compared: use MetricBars or RangeBars

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- points 3–7, coordinates 0–100, points at least 8 apart so labels do not overlap
- point.label ≤ 8 chars
- each end of x and y ≤ 6 chars; each zone ≤ 8 chars
- notes 2–3: title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: Points pop in one after another and the focus keeps pulsing; hovering a point turns its label white.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `x` | `[string, string]` | yes | Both ends of the x axis |
| `y` | `[string, string]` | yes | Both ends of the y axis (low, high) |
| `zones` | `[string, string, string, string]` |  | Quadrant names: top left, top right, bottom left, bottom right |
| `points` | `{ label, x, y, focus? }[]` | yes | Points |
| `notes` | `{ title, body }[]` | yes | Reading in the right column |

**Example**

```tsx
<Quadrant
  kicker="竞争地图"
  title="找一块别人没占的位置"
  x={['上手难', '上手快']}
  y={['只看报表', '能做归因']}
  points={[
    { label: '通用 BI', x: 22, y: 58 },
    { label: '示例产品', x: 74, y: 76, focus: true },
  ]}
  notes={[{ title: '轴要来自客户', body: '…' }]}
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="persona-cards"></a>

## 40 · Buyer personas `<PersonaCards />`

**Position**: Customer · three roles in the buying group; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `personas`

Three cards side by side, one per role: icon, decision role, the need in their own words, two goals, two pains and the channels that reach them. The main buyer has an accent line on top.

**Use when**

- A B2B buying group: decision maker, user, gatekeeper
- Marketing, sales and product need to agree on who this is sold to

**Avoid**

- Comparing three market segments, not roles in one purchase: use IconGrid
- The persona rests on no interviews and is not marked illustrative

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- personas exactly 3
- role ≤ 10 chars; part ≤ 6 chars; job ≤ 30 chars
- goals and pains exactly 2 each, every one ≤ 18 chars
- channels 2–3, each ≤ 8 characters
- focus at most 1

**Interaction**: Cards rise one after another; on hover a card lifts and its icon tile turns and lights up.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `personas` | `{ icon, role, part, job, goals, pains, channels, focus? }[]` | yes | The three roles |

**Example**

```tsx
<PersonaCards
  kicker="买家画像"
  title="同一笔采购，三个角色"
  personas={[
    { icon: 'user-speak', role: '市场负责人', part: '决策者', job: '让我能说清每一笔预算带来了多少客户', goals: ['预算分配有依据', '汇报少花时间'], pains: ['渠道数据对不上', '归因靠手工'], channels: ['行业活动', '同行推荐'], focus: true },
    …
  ]}
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="pricing-tiers"></a>

## 41 · Tiered pricing `<PricingTiers />`

**Position**: Pricing · good / better / best; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `tiers`

3–4 price cards; the recommended tier is a dark card, slightly raised and with a badge; each tier has a price, who it fits and 3–5 features, with one line on the value metric at the bottom.

**Use when**

- Product tiers and a pricing plan
- Steering most customers to one tier

**Avoid**

- Comparing competitor prices: use Matrix
- Only one tier: use BigStat

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- tiers 3 or 4, from cheapest to most expensive
- name ≤ 6 characters; price ≤ 6 characters; unit ≤ 10 characters
- for ≤ 16 chars
- features 3–5, each ≤ 16 chars; the next tier starts with “includes everything in the tier above”
- focus exactly 1 tier; badge ≤ 6 chars
- footnote ≤ 50 chars

**Interaction**: Cards rise one after another; hover lifts a card.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `tiers` | `{ name, price, unit?, for, features, focus?, badge? }[]` | yes | The tiers |
| `footnote` | `string` |  | Note at the bottom: value metric and upgrade rule |

**Example**

```tsx
<PricingTiers
  kicker="分档定价"
  title="三档定价，把人引到中间那档"
  tiers={[
    { name: '入门', price: '¥0', unit: '/ 3 席位', for: '个人和小团队试用', features: ['3 个数据源', '标准看板模板'] },
    { name: '专业', price: '¥399', unit: '/ 席位 / 月', for: '中型企业', features: ['包含入门档全部功能', '多触点归因'], focus: true, badge: '推荐' },
    { name: '企业', price: '询价', for: '大型组织', features: ['包含专业档全部功能', '单点登录'] },
  ]}
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="kpi-board"></a>

## 42 · Metric board `<KpiBoard />`

**Position**: Measurement · north star and input metrics; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `kpi`

A large dark card on the left holds the north star metric (big number and trend line); 4 or 6 small cards on the right hold input metrics, each with a value, a change and either a trend line or a note on how it is counted.

**Use when**

- The core metrics of a weekly meeting, monthly report or review
