- Showing one outcome metric driven by a few input metrics

**Avoid**

- Only 1–3 numbers: use BigStat or MetricBars
- One metric across several objects: use MetricBars

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- star 1; items 4 or 6
- label ≤ 10 chars; value ≤ 7 characters
- delta ≤ 6 characters; good sets the colour (neutral when unset)
- trend 6–12 points; with no trend write note (≤ 18 chars), one of the two

**Interaction**: Cards fade in one after another and trend lines wipe from left to right; hovering a small card lifts it.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `star` | `Kpi` | yes | North star metric |
| `items` | `{ label, value, delta?, good?, trend?, note? }[]` | yes | Input metrics |

**Example**

```tsx
<KpiBoard
  kicker="指标看板"
  title="一个北极星，六个输入指标"
  star={{ label: '每周活跃看板数', value: '1,240', delta: '+18%', good: true, trend: [420, 560, 700, 910, 1240] }}
  items={[
    { label: 'CAC 回收期', value: '16 月', delta: '−2 月', good: true, note: '中型客户目标 < 18 个月' },
    …
  ]}
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="share-bar"></a>

## 43 · Composition bar `<ShareBar />`

**Position**: Allocation · how one whole is split; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `mix`

One 100% stacked bar per row with 2–5 segments and the percentage written inside; one segment can be set to the accent colour in every row; the legend sits on top.

**Use when**

- The make-up of a budget, of time, of channels, of traffic
- Comparing how the make-up shifts from row to row

**Avoid**

- The parts do not add up to 100%: use MetricBars
- One row only: use BigStat for the key segment

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- legend 2–5 segments, each ≤ 6 chars
- rows 3–6; values in each row match legend one to one and add up to 100
- Segments under 7% show no number
- label ≤ 10 chars; note ≤ 16 chars; footnote ≤ 50 chars

**Interaction**: Bars grow from the left; hovering a segment fades the others.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `legend` | `string[]` | yes | Segment names |
| `rows` | `{ label, values, note? }[]` | yes | The rows |
| `focus` | `number` |  | The accent segment |
| `footnote` | `string` |  | Note at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<ShareBar
  kicker="渠道组合"
  title="预算随上市阶段换重心"
  legend={['品牌', '内容与搜索', '付费投放', '销售拓展']}
  focus={0}
  rows={[
    { label: '上市前', values: [40, 40, 5, 15] },
    { label: '上市当月', values: [30, 15, 35, 20] },
  ]}
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="waterfall"></a>

## 44 · Revenue bridge `<Waterfall />`

**Position**: Breakdown · from opening to closing; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `bridge`

A waterfall: an opening total, 3–6 changes and a closing total worked out automatically; increases in the accent colour, decreases hatched, dashed lines joining the bars; the reading goes in the right column.

**Use when**

- Breaking down how ARR, profit, budget or headcount moved from opening to closing
- Showing what ate the growth

**Avoid**

- The items do not add or subtract: use MetricBars or DeltaBars
- More than 6 changes: merge the small ones

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps 3–6, a positive number adds, a negative one subtracts; the closing total is computed
- label ≤ 6 chars; end ≤ 6 chars
- notes 2–3: key ≤ 8 chars (one number allowed), text ≤ 30 chars
- Keep values within 5 digits and put the scale in unit, for example ten thousand yuan or thousand people

**Interaction**: Bars rise one after another (decreases drop from above).

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `start` | `{ label, value }` | yes | Opening |
| `steps` | `{ label, value }[]` | yes | Changes |
| `end` | `string` | yes | Closing label |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `digits` | `number` |  | Decimal places, default 0 |
| `notes` | `{ key, text }[]` |  | Reading in the right column |

**Example**

```tsx
<Waterfall
  kicker="收入桥"
  title="年度经常性收入从哪里来"
  start={{ label: '期初 ARR', value: 1200 }}
  steps={[{ label: '新签', value: 420 }, { label: '扩容', value: 180 }, { label: '流失', value: -140 }]}
  end="期末 ARR"
  unit="万元"
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="team"></a>

## 45 · Team cards `<Team />`

**Position**: Roles · who owns what; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `team`

4, 6 or 8 cards, each with an initials avatar, a name or role, a title, and one line at the bottom on what that person owns. The lead's avatar is in the accent colour.

**Use when**

- Introducing the team at a kick-off
- Roles in a launch team or a virtual team

**Avoid**

- Each task needs its owner and its decider spelled out: build a RACI in Matrix
- Only 2–3 people: put them in body text

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- members 4, 6 or 8
- name ≤ 8 chars; role ≤ 14 chars; owns ≤ 36 chars
- initials 1–3 characters; when empty, the first character of the name or the English initials are used
- No photo slot: real photos do not go into a deck

**Interaction**: Cards appear one after another; hover lifts a card.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `members` | `{ name, role, owns, initials?, focus? }[]` | yes | Members |

**Example**

```tsx
<Team
  kicker="上市小组"
  title="六个角色，各管一段"
  members={[
    { name: '上市负责人', initials: 'PMM', role: '产品营销 · 1 人', owns: '定位与文案，统筹上市节奏。', focus: true },
    { name: '销售', initials: 'AE', role: '销售 · 3 人', owns: '跟进合格线索、演示和成交。' },
  ]}
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="issue-tree"></a>

## 46 · Issue tree `<IssueTree />`

**Position**: Analysis · breaking the question apart; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `diagnose`

A dark card on the left holds the question to answer; 2–4 branches on the right, each followed by a row of 2–3 causes to check; the focus branch gets a light accent background.

**Use when**

- A review or diagnosis: why a metric moved
- The first slide of a consulting-style analysis: split the question into parts that do not overlap

**Avoid**

- The branches have an order: use FlowDiagram
- The answer is known: give it with BigStat or NumberedColumns

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- branches 2–4; leaves 2–3 each
- root.title ≤ 12 chars; note ≤ 16 chars
- branch.title ≤ 10 chars; note ≤ 16 chars; leaf ≤ 14 chars (≤ 10 chars with 4 branches)
- Branches must not overlap and together must cover the whole question

**Interaction**: Branches appear one after another, cause tags pop in after them; hovering a branch turns its card to a light accent.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `root` | `{ title, note? }` | yes | The question to answer |
| `branches` | `{ title, note?, leaves }[]` | yes | Branches |
| `focus` | `number` |  | The accent branch |

**Example**

```tsx
<IssueTree
  kicker="问题拆解"
  title="先拆问题，再找原因"
  root={{ title: '新客户增长为什么放缓？' }}
  branches={[
    { title: '线索变少', note: '看各渠道的线索量', leaves: ['付费渠道预算下调', '内容更新变慢'] },
    { title: '转化变低', note: '看漏斗每一级', leaves: ['首次响应变慢', '试用没有上手'] },
    { title: '成交变慢', leaves: ['参与决策的人变多', '安全评审排队'] },
  ]}
  focus={1}
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="heatmap"></a>

## 47 · Heat table `<Heatmap />`

**Position**: Distribution · values where two dimensions cross; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `cohort`

A row × column table of values; cells are shaded by value and numbers on dark cells turn white automatically; cells with no data keep a dashed frame; a colour scale sits below and the reading in the right column.

**Use when**

- Cohort retention (signing month × month number)
- Conversion by channel × funnel stage, usage by feature × customer group

**Avoid**

- One row or one column: use MetricBars
- Exact comparison rather than the overall pattern: use Matrix

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cols 3–8, each ≤ 6 characters
- rows 3–7; label ≤ 8 chars; sub ≤ 10 characters
- Values ≤ 4 digits (decimals included); put the unit in unit
- notes 2–3: key ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 30 chars

**Interaction**: Cells pop in along the diagonal; hovering a cell enlarges it, hovering a row turns its name to the accent colour.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `cols` | `string[]` | yes | Column labels |
| `rows` | `{ label, sub?, values }[]` | yes | Rows; null in values means no data yet |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit inside the cells |
| `digits` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `min / max` | `number` |  | Both ends of the colour scale; defaults to the smallest and largest values in the data |
| `scale` | `[string, string]` |  | Text at the two ends of the colour scale |
| `notes` | `{ key, text }[]` |  | Reading in the right column |

**Example**

```tsx
<Heatmap
  kicker="分批留存"
  title="按签约月份看留存"
  cols={['第 1 月', '第 2 月', '第 3 月']}
  rows={[
    { label: '1 月签约', values: [92, 86, 82] },
    { label: '2 月签约', values: [93, 87, null] },
  ]}
  unit="%"
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="roadmap"></a>

## 48 · Roadmap `<Roadmap />`

**Position**: Roadmap · direction by quarter; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `roadmap`

2–4 work streams × 3–5 periods; items can span periods and are marked done (grey with a tick), doing (accent, with a breathing dot) or planned (dashed frame); a vertical “today” line marks where you are.

**Use when**

- A product, marketing or sales roadmap by quarter or half year
- Direction and progress in one view

**Avoid**

- Scheduling by week: use Gantt
- No periods, only now / next / later: use Lanes

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- periods 3–5, each ≤ 8 characters
- tracks 2–4; label ≤ 6 chars
- items 4–12; title ≤ 8 chars within one period, ≤ 12 chars across periods
- At most two items per work stream per period

**Interaction**: Items rise one after another along each work stream; doing items carry a breathing dot.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `periods` | `string[]` | yes | Periods |
| `tracks` | `{ label, icon? }[]` | yes | Work streams |
| `items` | `{ track, from, to?, title, status }[]` | yes | Items; status is 'done' \| 'doing' \| 'next' |
| `now` | `number` |  | Position of “today” in periods, for example 1.4 |
| `labels` | `{ done?, doing?, next? }` |  | Legend text |

**Example**

```tsx
<Roadmap
  kicker="路线图"
  title="上市后的四个季度"
  periods={['第 1 季度', '第 2 季度', '第 3 季度', '第 4 季度']}
  tracks={[{ label: '产品', icon: 'widget' }, { label: '市场', icon: 'presentation' }]}
  items={[
    { track: 0, from: 0, title: '公开测试版', status: 'done' },
    { track: 0, from: 1, title: '团队协作', status: 'doing' },
    { track: 1, from: 1, to: 2, title: '行业案例系列', status: 'next' },
  ]}
  now={1.4}
  source="示意"
/>
```

<a id="statement"></a>

## 49 · One line `<Statement />`

**Position**: Rhythm · a pause between two dense passages; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gtm` — `price-point`

One sentence alone on the slide, 112u and centred, key words in the accent colour; a faint line of support can go below. With tone: "accent" the whole slide takes the accent colour.

**Use when**

- A data slide has just finished and the conclusion needs to stand on its own
- A pause is needed mid-chapter so one line sticks

**Avoid**

- One sentence does not fit: use QuoteHero or BigStat
- Two slides in a row: too many pauses stop being a pause

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- text ≤ 28 chars; an array breaks lines by hand, at most 2 lines × 14 chars
- marks 0–2, each a substring that appears verbatim in text
- sub ≤ 40 chars
- Numbers in the sentence need their source in source too
- At most 3 slides per deck

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small label above the sentence |
| `text` | `string \| string[]` | yes | The sentence |
| `marks` | `string[]` |  | Words in the accent colour |
| `sub` | `string` |  | One line of support |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<Statement
  kicker="价格杠杆"
  text={['价格提高 1%，', '利润提高 11.1%']}
  marks={['11.1%']}
  sub="所以定价放在上市之前定，不放在上市之后补。"
  source="Marn & Rosiello · HBR 1992"
/>
```

<a id="hanging-cover"></a>

## 50 · Hanging icon cover `<HangingCover />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1 of a product launch; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `cover`

A large centred title with coloured icon tiles hanging from thin threads at the top, swaying slowly; one word of the title takes the accent colour, with the lead and 1–3 pills below.

**Use when**

- A launch or introduction for a product, platform or tool
- A light, clean opening with a product feel

**Avoid**

- A serious research or policy briefing: use Cover
- No fitting icons: icons are decoration, do not force them

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 12 chars
- mark must be a substring that appears verbatim in title
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- icons 4–8, names from icon-set only
- pills 1–3, facts only (page count, date), no fake buttons

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `tag` | `{ label, text }` |  | Small label above the title |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `mark` | `string` |  | Word in the accent colour |
| `lead` | `string` | yes | Lead |
| `icons` | `IconName[]` | yes | Hanging icons, 4–8 |
| `pills` | `string[]` |  | Pills |

**Example**

```tsx
<HangingCover
  tag={{ label: '第 1 期', text: '风格引擎与面板模板' }}
  title={['同一份内容，', '换一种气质']}
  mark="气质"
  lead="风格只管外观，内容和上限都不动。"
  icons={['tuning', 'layers', 'chart', 'presentation']}
  pills={['13 页', '2026 年 10 月']}
/>
```

<a id="panel-cards"></a>

## 51 · Panel cards `<PanelCards />`

**Position**: Product · 3–4 capabilities, each with a small interface; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `panels`

A light grey rounded card: a small heading on top and a white mini interface below, as a list, big number, bars, progress bar, conversion or chips.

**Use when**

- Introduce 3–4 product capabilities, each with data you can draw
- Show several small data sets side by side

**Avoid**

- Text only and no data: use IconGrid
- More than 4 items: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 3 or 4
- eyebrow ≤ 12 chars, title ≤ 8 chars, body ≤ 24 chars
- list 3–5 rows; bars 3–12; progress 2–4 rows; chips 3–6
- Numbers in the mini interface also need a source

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; array breaks lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences of lead, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `cards` | `{ eyebrow?, title, body?, ui }[]` | yes | Cards; ui.kind = list / figure / bars / progress / convert / chips |

**Example**

```tsx
<PanelCards
  kicker="面板卡片"
  title="一张卡，一小块界面"
  cards={[
    { title: '打包体积', ui: { kind: 'figure', value: '5.9 MB', note: '单个 HTML 文件' } },
    { title: '参考演示', ui: { kind: 'bars', values: [25, 31, 30], value: '86 页' } },
    { title: '检查视口', ui: { kind: 'list', rows: [{ label: '1920 × 1080', value: '截图' }] } },
  ]}
  source="npm run check"
/>
```

<a id="ask-answer"></a>

## 52 · Q&A card `<AskAnswer />`

**Position**: Product · Show one question and its answer; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `ask`

A panel on the right: question bubble at the top right, a "found ..." line below it, then three result cards, each with one big number and a row of small bars. Title and notes on the left.

**Use when**

- Show what search, analysis or an AI assistant can answer
- Put the answer to the same question for three targets side by side

**Avoid**

- The answer is not a number: use QuoteWall or ClaimCards
- More than 3 results: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- question ≤ 30 chars
- found ≤ 20 chars
- results exactly 3: name ≤ 8, value ≤ 6 characters, bars 4–10
- notes 0–3, each ≤ 20 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; array breaks lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences of lead, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `question` | `string` | yes | The question |
| `found` | `string` | yes | Answer summary |
| `results` | `{ name, sub?, value, bars, note? }[3]` | yes | Three result cards |
| `notes` | `string[]` |  | Notes on the left |

**Example**

```tsx
<AskAnswer
  kicker="问答卡"
  title="把一个问题，变成三张卡"
  question="三份参考演示，每一章各有几页？"
  found="找到 3 份演示，共 86 页"
  results={[
    { name: 'Lumen', value: '25 页', bars: [7, 8, 5, 3] },
    { name: 'GEO', value: '31 页', bars: [8, 8, 5, 8] },
    { name: 'GTM', value: '30 页', bars: [5, 6, 6, 11] },
  ]}
/>
```

<a id="proof-split"></a>

## 53 · Proof split `<ProofSplit />`

**Position**: Argument · One capability plus two evidence numbers; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `proof`

A dark panel on the left with one large icon tile and a few setting rows; on the right a title, lead, two big numbers and check items.

**Use when**

- Explain how an automation is set up and what it does
- One claim with two hard numbers

**Avoid**

- More than 2 numbers: use KpiBoard
- No rule that can be listed as a setting

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 3–5: label ≤ 8, value ≤ 12 characters
- stats exactly 2: value ≤ 6 characters, label ≤ 12 chars
- checks 0–3, each ≤ 20 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; array breaks lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences of lead, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `icon` | `IconName` | yes | Large icon on the panel |
| `rows` | `{ label, value }[]` | yes | Settings |
| `stats` | `{ value, label }[2]` | yes | Two numbers |
| `checks` | `string[]` |  | Check items |

**Example**

```tsx
<ProofSplit
  kicker="检查"
  title="每次构建，都自己检查一遍"
  icon="shield"
  rows={[{ label: 'FONT', value: '≥ 16.5u' }, { label: 'VIEWPORTS', value: '4 种' }, { label: 'REMOTE', value: '禁止' }]}
  stats={[{ value: '16.5u', label: '最小字号' }, { value: '4', label: '检查的视口' }]}
  source="scripts/qa.mjs"
/>
```

<a id="index-stack"></a>

## 54 · Index stack `<IndexStack />`

**Position**: Structure · One slide for 3–5 items, one at a time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `stack`

A contents column on the left, cards stacked like folders on the right. With build: "focus", each → brings the next card to the front and highlights it in the contents.

**Use when**

- Walk through 3–5 modules on one slide
- Each module has a small data set

**Avoid**

- Modules need a side-by-side comparison: use PanelCards
- More than 5 items

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–5
- label ≤ 8 chars, title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 40 chars
- ui is the same as PanelCards
- Set build: "focus" on the SlideDef

**Interaction**: Press → to bring the next card to the front; on a direct jump all cards start stacked

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; array breaks lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences of lead, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `items` | `{ label, icon, title, body, ui }[]` | yes | Items |

**Example**

```tsx
<IndexStack
  kicker="目录叠卡"
  title="一页讲四件事，一次只看一件"
  items={[
    { label: '风格令牌', icon: 'tuning', title: '风格令牌', body: '…', ui: { kind: 'figure', value: '5 种' } },
    …
  ]}
/>
```

<a id="contact-panel"></a>

## 55 · Contact card `<ContactPanel />`

**Position**: Closing · How to reach you next; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `contact`

A large dark rounded card: title, lead and promise checks on the left, a white card with contact details or entry points on the right, a black action bar at the bottom.

**Use when**

- Last slide of a deck, with a clear next step
- Closing for sales, partnership or hiring

**Avoid**

- No real contact details: use Closing
- Need a QR code: use Thanks

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- checks 2–3, each ≤ 18 chars
- fields 2–4: label ≤ 8 chars, value ≤ 28 characters, real addresses only
- action ≤ 14 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `checks` | `string[]` |  | Promises |
| `fields` | `{ icon, label, value }[]` | yes | Contact details |
| `action` | `string` | yes | Action bar text |

**Example**

```tsx
<ContactPanel
  kicker="开始用"
  title={['挑一种风格，', '写下一份演示']}
  fields={[{ icon: 'gallery', label: '组件库', value: '#/kit' }]}
  action="打开组件库挑风格"
/>
```

<a id="orbit"></a>

## 56 · Orbit `<Orbit />`

**Position**: Structure · One core and what surrounds it; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `orbit`

Two orbits on the right with a dark icon in the center and 4–8 colored icons on the inner and outer ring; title, lead and points on the left.

**Use when**

- Ecosystem, integrations, tech stack, partners
- Show that all of these connect to one thing

**Avoid**

- Nodes have an order or a dependency: use FlowDiagram
- More than 8 nodes

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- nodes 4–8, label ≤ 6 chars
- points 0–3, each ≤ 22 chars
- center.label ≤ 8 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; array breaks lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences of lead, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `center` | `{ icon, label }` | yes | Center |
| `nodes` | `{ icon, label }[]` | yes | Nodes on the orbits |
| `points` | `string[]` |  | Points on the left |

**Example**

```tsx
<Orbit
  kicker="技术栈"
  title="一个文件，背后这几样"
  center={{ icon: 'presentation', label: 'PCafe' }}
  nodes={[{ icon: 'code', label: 'React 19' }, { icon: 'bolt', label: 'Vite 8' }]}
  source="package.json"
/>
```

<a id="number-opener"></a>

## 57 · Big number opener `<NumberOpener />`

**Position**: Rhythm · Open a chapter with one number; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `count`

A 400u accent number on the left, one explanatory line and a sub line on the right. More minimal than BigStat; use it as the opener or on the first slide of a chapter.

**Use when**

- Open with the single most important number
- Set the tone of a chapter with one number

**Avoid**

- Need several numbers: use BigStat or KpiBoard
- The number has no source

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- value ≤ 5 characters
- unit ≤ 4 characters
- text ≤ 2 lines × 14 chars
- sub ≤ 40 chars
- source required

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `value` | `string` | yes | Number |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `text` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Explanation |
| `sub` | `string` |  | Sub line |
| `source` | `string` | yes | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<NumberOpener
  kicker="这一期之后"
  value="67"
  unit="个"
  text={['固定模板，', '每个都写明内容上限']}
  source="src/kit/catalog.ts"
/>
```

<a id="chapter-progress"></a>

## 58 · Chapter progress `<ChapterProgress />`

**Position**: Rhythm · First slide of a chapter, shows where the talk is; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `ch2`

Chapter title and a large "02 / 04" on top, a progress line below: finished chapters ticked, the current one lit, later chapters faded.

**Use when**

- A long deck in chapters, on the first slide of each chapter
- The audience needs to know how much is left

**Avoid**

- Only one chapter
- Want icons and big cards on the section break: use SectionBreak

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- chapters 3–6, each ≤ 8 chars
- current starts at 0
- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Chapter title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `chapters` | `string[]` | yes | All chapters |
| `current` | `number` | yes | Current chapter, starting at 0 |

**Example**

```tsx
<ChapterProgress
  kicker="第二部分"
  title="面板模板"
  chapters={['风格引擎', '面板模板', '节奏页', '检查与放映']}
  current={1}
/>
```

<a id="question"></a>

## 59 · Question slide `<Question />`

**Position**: Rhythm · Lead into the next part with one question; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `why`

The whole slide is one question in large centered type, with a very faint giant question mark behind it.

**Use when**

- The next chapter answers one question
- Say the audience's doubt out loud first

**Avoid**

- The question already contains its answer: use Statement
- Used twice in a row

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- question ≤ 2 lines × 12 chars, ends with a question mark
- sub ≤ 36 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `question` | `string \| string[]` | yes | The question |
| `sub` | `string` |  | Sub line |

**Example**

```tsx
<Question kicker="为什么不现场设计" question={['为什么不让 AI', '每次现场设计？']} sub="现场设计上限高，但每次结果不同。" />
```

<a id="contrast"></a>

## 60 · Two-line contrast `<Contrast />`

**Position**: Rhythm · Past / now, misconception / fact; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `contrast`

Two large lines stacked: the first greyed out and struck through, the second in body color, labels in the accent color. With build, the second line appears on →.

**Use when**

- Say in one line what changed
- Correct a common misconception

**Avoid**

- Several items on each side: use VerdictColumns
- Need a data comparison: use Dumbbell

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- label ≤ 6 chars
- text ≤ 20 chars
- sub ≤ 40 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `before` | `{ label, text }` | yes | Old wording |
| `after` | `{ label, text }` | yes | New wording |
| `sub` | `string` |  | Sub line |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<Contrast
  kicker="换风格的成本"
  before={{ label: '过去', text: '换风格，等于重写每一页' }}
  after={{ label: '现在', text: '换风格，只改一行 style' }}
/>
```

<a id="recap"></a>

## 61 · Chapter recap `<Recap />`

**Position**: Rhythm · Review the points at the end of a chapter; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/launch` — `recap`

3–4 cards, each with a tick, a point title and one line; a black pill at the bottom previews the next chapter.

**Use when**

- End a chapter with the few points to remember
- Help the audience keep track in a long deck

**Avoid**

- Only one point: use Statement
- Points have an order: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- points 3–4: title ≤ 12 chars, body ≤ 36 chars
- next.label ≤ 6 chars, next.title ≤ 14 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; array breaks lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences of lead, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `points` | `{ title, body }[]` | yes | Points |
| `next` | `{ label, title }` |  | Next chapter preview |

**Example**

```tsx
<Recap
  kicker="小结"
  title="这一期留下的三件事"
  points={[{ title: '风格令牌', body: '…' }, { title: '18 个新模板', body: '…' }, { title: '样张先行', body: '…' }]}
  next={{ label: '下一期', title: '等宽黑底与贴纸风' }}
/>
```

<a id="hero-panel"></a>

## 62 · Dark hero cover `<HeroPanel />`

**Position**: Opening · Tool or platform launch; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/studio` — `cover`

The whole slide is one dark rounded card: tag, title, lead and pills on the left, a real screenshot tilted in perspective on the right; up to 3 laurels below the card.

**Use when**

- Launch of software, a platform or a design system
- There is a real screenshot that shows the product

**Avoid**

- No real screenshot: use HangingCover
- Laurel content has no source: drop laurels

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- pills 1–2
- src must be a real screenshot
- laurels 0–3: title ≤ 10 chars, sub ≤ 14 chars, needs a source

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `tag` | `{ label, text }` |  | Small tag |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` | yes | Lead |
| `pills` | `string[]` |  | Pills |
| `src` | `string` | yes | Screenshot |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Screenshot description |
| `laurels` | `{ title, sub? }[]` |  | Laurels |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<HeroPanel
  tag={{ label: '第 1 期', text: '工作室风格' }}
  title={['做演示，', '像搭积木']}
  lead="…"
  src={kit}
  alt="组件库首页截图"
  laurels={[{ title: '67 个模板', sub: '全部带内容上限' }]}
/>
```

<a id="tabbed-shot"></a>

## 63 · Tabbed screenshot `<TabbedShot />`

**Position**: Product · 2–4 screens of one product; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/studio` — `tabs`

Centered title and lead, a tab switcher below, then one large screenshot. Click a tab to switch the screenshot; the first one shows on open.

**Use when**

- A product has a few main screens
- The screenshot is the argument

**Avoid**

- No real screenshot
- Details in the screenshot need labels: use Screenshot

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- tabs 2–4, label ≤ 6 chars
- every src is a real screenshot
- title ≤ 22 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars

**Interaction**: Click a tab to switch the screenshot

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `tabs` | `{ label, icon?, src, alt }[]` | yes | Tabs and screenshots |

**Example**

```tsx
<TabbedShot
  kicker="三种视图"
  title="写、挑、放，各有一个入口"
  tabs={[{ label: '组件库', src: kitEntry, alt: '…' }, { label: '放映', src: slide, alt: '…' }]}
/>
```

<a id="brand-kit"></a>

## 64 · Design guidelines `<BrandKit />`

**Position**: Product · Type, color and size; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/studio` — `brand`

A card on the left lists 2–3 typefaces with large Aa samples, rows of color dots at the top right and 4 size tokens at the bottom right. Here color is content, so hex values are allowed.

**Use when**

- Introduce brand guidelines or a design system
- Hand visual rules to design or front-end

**Avoid**

- Only one color to explain: write it in the body text
- More than 3 typefaces

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- fonts 2–3: name ≤ 20 characters, role ≤ 10 chars
- palettes 2–3 rows, 4–10 per row
- tokens 0–4: label ≤ 8 chars, value ≤ 12 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `fonts` | `{ name, role, family, sample? }[]` | yes | Typefaces |
| `palettes` | `{ name, colors: { name, value }[] }[]` | yes | Color palettes |
| `tokens` | `{ label, value }[]` |  | Size tokens |

**Example**

```tsx
<BrandKit
  kicker="设计规范"
  title="字体、颜色和尺寸，都在一个文件里"
  fonts={[{ name: 'Geist', role: '正文', family: 'var(--f-sans)' }]}
  palettes={[{ name: '主题色', colors: [{ name: 'cobalt', value: '#2457f5' }] }]}
/>
```

<a id="laurels"></a>

## 65 · Laurels `<Laurels />`

**Position**: Argument · Awards, rankings or hard numbers; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/studio` — `laurels`

3–4 cards, each with a title and subtitle ringed by laurel and one line below.

**Use when**

- Awards, rankings and certifications you can source
- A few hard promises or limits

**Avoid**

- "Best" or "first" with no source
- More than 4 items

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–4: title ≤ 10 chars, sub ≤ 14 chars, note ≤ 36 chars
- every item must be backed in the source

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `items` | `{ title, sub?, note? }[]` | yes | Laurels |

**Example**

```tsx
<Laurels
  kicker="三条底线"
  title="每次构建都守住的三件事"
  items={[{ title: '0 个外部请求', sub: '断网也能打开' }]}
  source="vite.config.ts"
/>
```

<a id="full-bleed"></a>

## 66 · Full-page image `<FullBleed />`

**Position**: Rhythm · One image fills the slide; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/studio` — `bleed`

A real screenshot or photo fills the slide and darkens towards the bottom, with a white title and caption at the bottom left. Set tone: "dark" on the SlideDef.

**Use when**

- One image says it all
- A visual pause between chapters

**Avoid**

- The image is not a real screenshot or photo
- Details in the image need labels: use Screenshot

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- src is a real screenshot or photo, 16:9 or wider
- title ≤ 2 lines × 12 chars
- caption ≤ 36 chars
- source gives the image source
- must set tone: "dark"

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src` | `string` | yes | Image |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Image description |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `caption` | `string` |  | Caption |
| `focus` | `string` |  | Crop focus, CSS object-position |
| `source` | `string` | yes | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
{ id: 'bleed', tone: 'dark', label: '总览', render: () => (
  <FullBleed src={overview} alt="…" kicker="按 G" title={['一眼看完', '整份演示']} source="本项目截图" />
) }
```

<a id="thanks"></a>

## 67 · Thanks and questions `<Thanks />`

**Position**: Closing · Thanks + questions + contact; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/studio` — `thanks`

A very large thank you and an invitation to ask on the left, 2–4 contact cards on the right, plus an optional real QR code.

**Use when**

- The talk ends and Q&A starts
- Several contact details to leave behind

**Avoid**

- Want the next step spelled out: use ContactPanel
- No real contact details

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars
- prompt ≤ 40 chars
- contacts 2–4: label ≤ 6 chars, value ≤ 32 characters
- qr must be a real QR code

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `prompt` | `string` |  | Invitation to ask |
| `contacts` | `{ icon, label, value }[]` | yes | Contact details |
| `qr` | `{ src, label }` |  | QR code |

**Example**

```tsx
<Thanks
  kicker="谢谢"
  title="谢谢"
  prompt="关于模板、风格和检查规则，都可以提问。"
  contacts={[{ icon: 'gallery', label: '组件库', value: '#/kit' }]}
/>
```

<a id="split-word"></a>

## 68 · Split word cover `<SplitWord />`

**Position**: Opening · Creative or brand proposal; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `cover`

Two huge words sit at the top left and the bottom right, with a stack of 2–3 cards tilted between them: real screenshots, or color blocks with icons. The signature cover of the mono style.

**Use when**

- Cover for a creative, brand or portfolio proposal
- 2–3 real screenshots that represent the work

**Avoid**

- Formal report: use Cover
- Title longer than two words: use HangingCover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- words 2, each ≤ 6 chars or 10 Latin letters
- cards 2–3: label ≤ 8 chars; src must be a real screenshot
- lead ≤ 30 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small label |
| `words` | `[string, string]` | yes | Two huge words |
| `cards` | `{ label, src?, alt?, icon?, spot? }[]` | yes | Cards in the middle |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One-line caption at lower right |
| `source` | `string` |  | Screenshot source |

**Example**

```tsx
<SplitWord
  kicker="第 2 期 · 等宽黑"
  words={['DECK', 'KIT']}
  cards={[{ label: '组件库', src: kit, alt: '…' }, { label: '放映', src: slide, alt: '…' }, { label: '总览', src: overview, alt: '…' }]}
  lead="用数字看这套模板：多少、由什么组成、节奏怎么排。"
/>
```

<a id="pill-agenda"></a>

## 69 · Pill agenda section break `<PillAgenda />`

**Position**: Rhythm · Start of each chapter; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `ch1`

A row of pills on top lists every chapter, past ones struck through and the current one inverted; a viewfinder at the bottom left frames a very large chapter number, with the chapter title and lead on the right.

**Use when**

- Section break in the mono style
- Show how many chapters there are and where the talk is

**Avoid**

- Fewer than 3 chapters
- Other styles: use ChapterProgress or SectionBreak

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- chapters 3–6, each ≤ 8 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 60 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small label |
| `chapters` | `string[]` | yes | All chapter names |
| `current` | `number` | yes | Current chapter index, starting at 0 |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Section title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |

**Example**

```tsx
<PillAgenda kicker="第一部分" chapters={['规模', '构成', '节奏', '取舍']} current={0} title={['先看规模']} lead="…" />
```

<a id="stat-row"></a>

## 70 · Stat card row `<StatRow />`

**Position**: Data · 3–4 scale numbers side by side; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `stats`

A row of dark cards like phone widgets: three dots at the top right, a mono label, a big number and one line; the first number uses the accent color. A row of name pills can go below.

**Use when**

- 3–4 scale numbers of equal weight
- List the tools or partners involved, names only

**Avoid**

- One number is the argument: use BigStat
- Need a trend: use SparkCards

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- stats 3–4: value ≤ 6 characters, label ≤ 12 characters, note ≤ 28 chars
- names ≤ 8, text only, no logos

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `stats` | `{ value, label, note }[]` | yes | Stat cards |
| `names` | `string[]` |  | Name pills |

**Example**

```tsx
<StatRow
  kicker="规模"
  title="四个数说清这套模板"
  stats={[{ label: 'templates', value: '86', note: '…' }, …]}
  names={['React 19', 'Vite 8', 'motion']}
  source="src/kit/catalog.ts"
/>
```

<a id="count-compare"></a>

## 71 · Rolling number comparison `<CountCompare />`

**Position**: Data · One number before and after; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `growth`

A card on each side with one big number that counts from 0 to its value on entry; an arrow and a change pill in between. The right card is inverted and stands for now.

**Use when**

- Before and after of one metric, where the change is the point
- Launch, retro or quarter comparison

**Avoid**

- More than two numbers: use MetricBars
- Need the path: use LineChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- before / after: label ≤ 8 chars, note ≤ 20 chars, unit ≤ 4 chars
- change ≤ 8 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `before` | `{ label, value, decimals?, unit?, note? }` | yes | Before |
| `after` | `{ label, value, decimals?, unit?, note? }` | yes | After |
| `change` | `string` | yes | Change, e.g. +19 |

**Example**

```tsx
<CountCompare
  kicker="这一期"
  title="模板从 67 个增加到 86 个"
  before={{ label: '第 1 期结束', value: 67, unit: '个' }}
  after={{ label: '第 2 期结束', value: 86, unit: '个' }}
  change="+19 个"
  source="src/kit/catalog.ts"
/>
```

<a id="donut"></a>

## 72 · Donut chart `<Donut />`

**Position**: Data · 2–5 parts of one whole; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `bundle`

One large ring, the focus part in the accent color and the rest in greys; the total or the focus share sits in the middle; a legend on the right gives the value and percentage of each part.

**Use when**

- Composition: where budget, size or time goes
- Only one part is the focus

**Avoid**

- More than 5 parts: use ShareBar or MetricBars
- The parts do not add up to the whole

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- segments 2–5: label ≤ 8 chars, note ≤ 16 chars, sorted largest first
- center.value ≤ 6 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `segments` | `{ label, value, note? }[]` | yes | Parts |
| `center` | `{ value, label }` | yes | Ring center |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Legend value unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Emphasized part, default 0 |

**Example**

```tsx
<Donut
  kicker="打包构成"
  title="一个文件，六成是字体"
  center={{ value: '6.0', label: 'MB · 单个 HTML' }}
  segments={[{ label: '字体', value: 3.6 }, { label: '脚本', value: 1.8 }, { label: '截图', value: 0.4 }, { label: '样式', value: 0.2 }]}
  unit=" MB"
  decimals={1}
  source="dist/index.html"
/>
```

<a id="bar-chart"></a>

## 73 · Bar chart `<BarChart />`

**Position**: Data · 2–8 categories, grouped or stacked; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `tones`

Vertical bars with the value above each bar; with mode="stack" they stack and only the total is labelled. One focus category can be set and the rest fade. Series 1 is the accent color, the rest are greys.

**Use when**

- Compare counts across 2–8 categories
- Each category splits into 2–3 series, grouped or stacked

**Avoid**

- Only 2–4 numbers: use MetricBars
- Time trend: use LineChart
- Positive and negative: use DeltaBars

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- categories 2–8, each ≤ 6 chars
- series 1–3: name ≤ 8 chars
- note ≤ 36 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `categories` | `string[]` | yes | Categories |
| `series` | `{ name, values }[]` | yes | Series |
| `mode` | `'group' \| 'stack'` |  | Grouped or stacked, default group |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Value unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Focus category index |
| `note` | `string` |  | Conclusion below the chart |

**Example**

```tsx
<BarChart
  kicker="明暗"
  title="深色页集中在 GTM"
  categories={['Lumen', 'GEO', 'GTM', 'Launch', 'Studio']}
  series={[{ name: '浅色页', values: [24, 30, 23, 13, 6] }, { name: '深色页', values: [1, 1, 5, 0, 1] }]}
  mode="stack"
  unit=" 页"
  focus={2}
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="bubble"></a>

## 74 · Scatter / bubble chart `<Bubble />`

**Position**: Data · Position on two dimensions; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `variety`

Two axes and 3–10 points; give size and it becomes a bubble chart, where area stands for a third value. One focus point uses the accent color. When labels collide, use side to move a label up, left or right.

**Use when**

- Where targets sit on two continuous metrics
- Read a third value at the same time through bubble size

**Avoid**

- Positioning of only two categories: use Quadrant
- More than 10 points

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- points 3–10: label ≤ 8 chars
- x / y.label ≤ 10 chars, with unit
- sizeLabel ≤ 12 chars, note ≤ 30 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `points` | `{ label, x, y, size?, side? }[]` | yes | Points |
| `x` | `{ label, min, max }` | yes | Horizontal axis |
| `y` | `{ label, min, max }` | yes | Vertical axis |
| `sizeLabel` | `string` |  | What bubble size means |
| `focus` | `string` |  | Label of the focus point |
| `note` | `string` |  | Conclusion on the right |

**Example**

```tsx
<Bubble
  kicker="页数与模板"
  title="页数越多，用到的模板越多"
  points={[{ label: 'GEO', x: 31, y: 25, size: 4, side: 'right' }, { label: 'GTM', x: 30, y: 26, size: 10, side: 'left' }, …]}
  x={{ label: '页数', min: 0, max: 35 }}
  y={{ label: '模板种类', min: 0, max: 30 }}
  sizeLabel="气泡大小：逐条展开的页数"
  focus="GTM"
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="gauge"></a>

## 75 · Gauge `<Gauge />`

**Position**: Data · One value against its limit; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `budget`

A 240 degree dial filled to the current value in the accent color, with an optional target tick; the big number in the middle. 2–3 supporting numbers can go on the right.

**Use when**

- Completion, budget used, capacity taken
- There is a clear limit or target

**Avoid**

- A number with no limit: use BigStat
- Several metrics: use KpiBoard

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- label ≤ 12 chars, unit ≤ 4 characters
- target.label ≤ 10 chars
- points 0–3: label ≤ 10 chars, value ≤ 8 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `value` | `number` | yes | Current value |
| `max` | `number` | yes | Limit |
| `target` | `{ value, label }` |  | Target tick |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `label` | `string` | yes | Caption under the big number |
| `points` | `{ label, value }[]` |  | Supporting numbers |

**Example**

```tsx
<Gauge
  kicker="体积"
  title="离预估上限还有余量"
  value={6.0}
  max={8}
  decimals={1}
  unit=" MB"
  label="当前单文件体积"
  target={{ value: 7, label: '预估下限 7 MB' }}
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="service-list"></a>

## 76 · Big word service list `<ServiceList />`

**Position**: Content · 4–6 items, one big word per row; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `services`

A column of very large words, alternating ink and accent color row by row, a colored icon square on the left and one line of monospace note text on the right.

**Use when**

- What we do, which steps the process has
- Each item fits in one word

**Avoid**

- Each item needs two sentences or more: use NumberedColumns
- Strict order and detail: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 4–6: word ≤ 6 chars or 12 letters, note ≤ 24 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small label |
| `items` | `{ word, icon, note }[]` | yes | Each row |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<ServiceList
  kicker="怎么用"
  items={[{ word: '挑模板', icon: 'widget', note: '在 #/kit 挑' }, { word: '填内容', icon: 'pen', note: '…' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="quote-arc"></a>

## 77 · Arc quote cards `<QuoteArc />`

**Position**: Content · three quotes with sources; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `quotes`

Three quote cards in an arc: the middle one inverted and in front, the side ones turned inward. Each holds one quote, a name and a source.

**Use when**

- Three quotes you can cite word for word, each with a source
- Back a point with a known quote or a customer's own words

**Avoid**

- Only one quote: use QuoteHero
- The quote has no traceable source

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- quotes exactly 3: text ≤ 48 chars, name ≤ 12 characters, role ≤ 20 chars
- source required

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `quotes` | `[ArcQuote, ArcQuote, ArcQuote]` | yes | { text, name, role } |

**Example**

```tsx
<QuoteArc
  kicker="取舍"
  title="删到不能再删，再把数据摆出来"
  quotes={[{ text: '少，但更好。', name: 'Dieter Rams', role: '…' }, …]}
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="work-cards"></a>

## 78 · Color block work cards `<WorkCards />`

**Position**: Content · 3–4 projects or options; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `styles`

Each card is one large block of color: title on top, a big icon in the middle (or a real screenshot or decor- illustration) and an outlined pill tag at bottom left.

**Use when**

- Showing a portfolio, options or product lines side by side
- One sentence per item is enough

**Avoid**

- You need body text: use IconGrid
- You need to compare pros and cons: use VerdictColumns

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 3–4: title ≤ 12 chars, tag ≤ 8 chars
- src must be a real screenshot or a decor- illustration

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `cards` | `{ title, tag, spot?, icon?, src?, alt? }[]` | yes | { title, tag, spot?, icon?, src?, alt? } |

**Example**

```tsx
<WorkCards
  kicker="风格"
  title="两期新增的四种风格"
  cards={[{ title: '静谧', tag: 'calm', icon: 'widget', spot: 1 }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="repeat-closing"></a>

## 79 · Repeated word closing `<RepeatClosing />`

**Position**: Closing · last slide; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mono` — `closing`

A Latin-script phrase fills the whole slide background (outlined, slowly drifting); a big line of text with an accent underline sits in the middle, a monospace line below it, and an optional chat bubble at bottom right.

**Use when**

- A closing slide in the mono black style
- Thanks, questions, a preview of the next issue

**Avoid**

- You need to list contact details: use ContactPanel or Thanks

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- word ≤ 12 Latin characters
- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- sub ≤ 40 chars
- chat: name ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 16 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `word` | `string` | yes | The word repeated in the background |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Large centered text |
| `sub` | `string` |  | Small line below |
| `chat` | `{ name, text }` |  | Chat bubble |

**Example**

```tsx
<RepeatClosing word="THANK YOU" title={['谢谢']} sub="下一期：…" chat={{ name: 'PCafe', text: '有问题，现在问' }} />
```

<a id="sticker-cover"></a>

## 80 · Sticker cover `<StickerCover />`

**Position**: Opening · informal sharing or personal intro; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sticker` — `cover`

On grid paper, the last line of the title sits inside a design-tool selection box and a handwritten note points at the title with a curved arrow; 2–4 stickers with mouse cursors scatter around; on the right a Polaroid holds a real screenshot with a file-name label.

**Use when**

- Team sharing, personal intro, informal internal sessions
- You have a real screenshot of a product or a piece of work

**Avoid**

- A formal report: use Cover
- With no screenshot you can drop shot; the title then fills the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- stickers 2–4, each ≤ 8 chars
- note ≤ 12 chars
- pills 1–2

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` | yes | Lead |
| `stickers` | `{ text, spot? }[]` | yes | Stickers |
| `note` | `string` |  | Handwritten note |
| `shot` | `{ src, alt, caption?, file? }` |  | Polaroid screenshot |
| `pills` | `string[]` |  | Pills |

**Example**

```tsx
<StickerCover
  title={['写演示，', '像贴贴纸']}
  lead="…"
  note="这就是标题"
  stickers={[{ text: '86 个模板' }, { text: '7 种风格' }]}
  shot={{ src: kit, alt: '…', caption: '组件库 #/kit', file: 'KIT.WEBP' }}
/>
```

<a id="word-blocks"></a>

## 81 · Color word blocks `<WordBlocks />`

**Position**: Content · 4–10 keywords; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sticker` — `blocks`

Each word is a thick-bordered color block with a small shape in front (circle, star, square, flower, triangle, half circle), laid out slightly tilted like stickers.

**Use when**

- A list of skills, topics or abilities
- One word per item is enough

**Avoid**

- Each item needs an explanation: use IconGrid
- The items have an order: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- words 4–10, each ≤ 6 chars or 12 letters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `words` | `{ word, shape?, spot? }[]` | yes | { word, shape?, spot? } |

**Example**

```tsx
<WordBlocks kicker="能做什么" title="这些页面，都有现成的模板" words={[{ word: '封面' }, { word: '数据图表' }, …]} />
```

<a id="folder-cards"></a>

## 82 · Stacked folder cards `<FolderCards />`

**Position**: Content · 3–4 projects, one at a time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sticker` — `folders`

A few folders stacked with offset tabs; the front one shows a title, a body, points and a real screenshot. With build set to focus, each → brings the next folder to the front.

**Use when**

- 3–4 projects or modules to walk through one by one
- Each one has a screenshot

**Avoid**

- You need a side-by-side comparison: use PanelCards
- More than 4

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–4: tab ≤ 10 characters, title ≤ 14 chars, body ≤ 60 chars, points 0–3, each ≤ 16 chars
- src must be a real screenshot

**Interaction**: Set build to focus: the current folder moves to the front.

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `items` | `{ tab?, title, body, points?, src?, alt?, file? }[]` | yes | { tab?, title, body, points?, src?, alt?, file? } |

**Example**

```tsx
<FolderCards
  kicker="三种视图"
  title="一个文件夹一个入口"
  items={[{ tab: 'KIT', title: '组件库', body: '…', src: kitEntry, alt: '…', file: 'ENTRY.WEBP' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="spark-cards"></a>

## 83 · Mini trend cards `<SparkCards />`

**Position**: Data · 3–4 metrics, each with a trend; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sticker` — `density`

Each card has a big number, a change pill and a small line chart (with area and a dot at the end); the period is noted at the bottom.

**Use when**

- 3–4 metrics, each with a series of past values
- You need both the current value and the trend

**Avoid**

- Only one metric's trend: use LineChart
- No past values: use StatRow

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 3–4: label ≤ 10 chars, value ≤ 7 characters, series 4–32 points, note ≤ 12 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `cards` | `{ label, value, unit?, delta?, series, note? }[]` | yes | { label, value, unit?, delta?, series, note? } |

**Example**

```tsx
<SparkCards
  kicker="文字密度"
  title="每页写多少字，起伏比平均数重要"
  cards={[{ label: 'Lumen 解读', value: '101', unit: '字/页', series: [74, 115, 52, …], note: '25 页' }, …]}
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="pictogram"></a>

## 84 · Pictogram share `<Pictogram />`

**Position**: Data · “how many out of every N”; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sticker` — `share`

The conclusion and legend on the left; 10 large cells or a 10×10 grid on the right, filled in order from the first cell in proportion, in at most three colors.

**Use when**

- A share reads better as “how many out of every 10 / 100”
- One to three parts

**Avoid**

- A share precise to decimals: use ShareBar
- More than 3 parts: use Donut

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 12 chars
- parts 1–3: label ≤ 10 chars
- Sum of parts ≤ of
- rest ≤ 10 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Conclusion |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `of` | `10 \| 100` | yes | Number of cells |
| `parts` | `{ label, value }[]` | yes | Filled parts |
| `rest` | `string` |  | Name of the unfilled part |
| `icon` | `IconName` |  | Icon in each cell |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<Pictogram
  kicker="页面类型"
  title={['每 100 页里，', '23 页逐条展开']}
  of={100}
  parts={[{ label: '逐条展开', value: 23 }, { label: '深色或强调色整页', value: 9 }]}
  rest="静态浅色页"
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="slope-rank"></a>

## 85 · Rank change `<SlopeRank />`

**Position**: Data · ranks at two time points or under two measures; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sticker` — `rank`

Two columns of ranks with connecting lines: rising lines are darker, the focus line uses the accent color, and the change in places is marked on the right.

**Use when**

- How ranks change between two time points
- The same set ranked under two measures

**Avoid**

- You need the actual values: use Dumbbell
- More than 8 items

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- periods 2, each ≤ 8 characters
- items 4–8: name ≤ 10 chars
- note ≤ 40 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `periods` | `[string, string]` | yes | The two column headers |
| `items` | `{ name, from, to }[]` | yes | Ranks |
| `focus` | `string` |  | Focus item |
| `note` | `string` |  | Conclusion on the right |

**Example**

```tsx
<SlopeRank
  kicker="两种排名"
  title="按页数排第二，按模板种类排第一"
  periods={['按页数', '按模板种类']}
  items={[{ name: 'GEO 简报', from: 1, to: 2 }, { name: 'GTM 手册', from: 2, to: 1 }, …]}
  focus="GTM 手册"
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="pixel-closing"></a>

## 86 · Pixel text closing `<PixelClosing />`

**Position**: Closing · last slide; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sticker` — `closing`

Very large pixel Latin text (for example LET'S TALK), over a yellow and black diagonal-striped block that holds a button-style call to action; a mascot at the top (a decor- illustration, replaced by an icon square when there is none).

**Use when**

- A closing slide in the sticker style
- There is one clear next action

**Avoid**

- A formal setting: use Closing

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- big ≤ 10 Latin characters
- action ≤ 10 chars
- sub ≤ 40 chars
- contacts 0–3: label ≤ 6 chars, value ≤ 24 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `big` | `string` | yes | The big pixel text |
| `action` | `string` | yes | Button text |
| `sub` | `string` |  | Note |
| `contacts` | `{ label, value }[]` |  | Contact details |
| `mascot` | `{ src, alt }` |  | decor- mascot |
| `icon` | `IconName` |  | Icon that replaces the mascot |

**Example**

```tsx
<PixelClosing big="LET'S TALK" action="打开 #/kit 试试" sub="…" contacts={[{ label: '规则', value: 'docs/zh-CN/guide.md' }]} />
```

<a id="float-cover"></a>

## 87 · Floating geometry cover `<FloatCover />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1 of a brand or launch deck; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `cover`

A very large centered title with a number card below it; four CSS-drawn 3D shapes (sphere, cube, torus, capsule) float around it, and a big-word marquee drifts slowly behind. The signature cover of the geometric style.

**Use when**

- A cover for a launch or a brand intro
- There is a number that stands for the topic

**Avoid**

- A formal report: use Cover
- No suitable number: use HangingCover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 7 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- card.value ≤ 8 characters, card.note ≤ 24 chars
- marquee ≤ 10 characters, only repeats words already on the slide

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `marquee` | `string` | yes | Word repeated in the marquee |
| `card` | `{ label, value, note }` | yes | The number card in the middle |
| `shapes` | `ShapeSpec[]` |  | Override the positions of the four default shapes |

**Example**

```tsx
<FloatCover
  kicker="第 3 期 · 2026 年 10 月"
  title={['给演示加点', '立体的玩具']}
  marquee="PCAFE"
  card={{ label: 'TEMPLATES', value: '251', note: '模板总数' }}
/>
```

<a id="tile-wall"></a>

## 88 · Icon tile wall `<TileWall />`

**Position**: Parallel · 6–9 features or parts; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `parts`

Title and lead on the left; three columns of large icon tiles on the right, with a big 3D sphere showing behind them. Good for abilities, integrations or parts.

**Use when**

- 6 or 9 parallel features, integrations or parts
- Each item fits in one word

**Avoid**

- Each item needs a sentence: use IconGrid
- Fewer than 6 items

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- tiles 6 or 9, label ≤ 6 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `tiles` | `{ icon, label, spot? }[]` | yes | Tiles |
| `shape` | `'sphere' \| 'cube' \| 'torus' \| 'capsule'` |  | Shape behind the tiles, sphere by default |

**Example**

```tsx
<TileWall kicker="新零件" title={['九个零件，', '模板拿来就用']} tiles={[{ icon: 'planet', label: '球体' }, …]} />
```

<a id="service-index"></a>

## 89 · Big number service columns `<ServiceIndex />`

**Position**: Procedure · 3–4 steps or services; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `steps`

3–4 columns, each sitting on a very large pale number: a title plus a short body. A small cube sits in the top right.

**Use when**

- 3–4 steps, services or methods
- Each item needs a sentence or two

**Avoid**

- The steps have explicit order arrows: use StepTrack
- More than 4 items

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–4: title ≤ 10 chars, text ≤ 44 chars
- title ≤ 1 line × 14 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `items` | `{ title, text }[]` | yes | Columns |
| `shape` | `ShapeKind` |  | Shape in the top right, cube by default |

**Example**

```tsx
<ServiceIndex kicker="工作流" title="做好一页，按四步走" items={[{ title: '收集事实', text: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="glow-stack"></a>

## 90 · Glow stack cards `<GlowStack />`

**Position**: Preview · this one, then the next; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `next`

A clear card in front lists the current points, a slightly blurred card behind it is the next one, and a colored glow shows through underneath.

**Use when**

- This issue versus the next, the current version versus the next
- The current one has 2–4 points

**Avoid**

- Both items matter equally: use DuoCards
- More than two items: use Roadmap

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- front.title ≤ 14 chars, items 2–4, each ≤ 20 chars
- back.title ≤ 14 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `front` | `{ label, title, items }` | yes | The front card |
| `back` | `{ label, title }` | yes | The blurred card behind |
| `spot` | `Spot` |  | Glow color, 4 by default |

**Example**

```tsx
<GlowStack kicker="路线" title="…" front={{ label: 'PHASE 3', title: '几何与桌面', items: ['…'] }} back={{ label: 'PHASE 4', title: '…' }} />
```

<a id="duo-cards"></a>

## 91 · Two-color option cards `<DuoCards />`

**Position**: Comparison · two options or two uses; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `outputs`

Two large solid cards, orange and purple, side by side; each has a big number, one line of text and a list with green dots. It reads like two price tiers and also suits two ways of using something.

**Use when**

- Two options, two tiers, two ways of using something
- Each one has a number that represents it

**Avoid**

- Three tiers: use PricingTiers
- You need a row-by-row attribute comparison: use CompareTable

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards exactly 2: name ≤ 8 chars, value ≤ 6 characters, note ≤ 24 chars, items 3–5, each ≤ 16 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `cards` | `[DuoCard, DuoCard]` | yes | The two cards |
| `spots` | `[Spot, Spot]` |  | Card colors, 3 and 4 by default |

**Example**

```tsx
<DuoCards kicker="两种用法" title="…" cards={[{ name: '单文件', value: '6.0', unit: 'MB', note: '…', items: ['…'] }, { … }]} />
```

<a id="desktop"></a>

## 92 · Desktop cover `<Desktop />`

**Position**: Opening · informal product demo; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/wallpaper` — `desktop`

A computer desktop: a column of desktop icons on the left, a window in the middle holding the title and lead, a clock widget in the top right and a dock along the bottom.

**Use when**

- A cover or contents slide for a product demo or a personal talk
- A deck in the desktop style

**Avoid**

- A formal report: use Cover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 6 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- apps 4–8, label ≤ 4 chars
- dock 4–7
- clock.time ≤ 5 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `file` | `string` | yes | File name in the window title bar |
| `apps` | `{ icon, label, spot? }[]` | yes | Desktop icons |
| `dock` | `{ icon, label, spot? }[]` | yes | Dock icons |
| `clock` | `{ time, date }` |  | Clock widget |

**Example**

```tsx
<Desktop kicker="第 3 期" title={['把演示', '放进桌面']} file="phase-3.deck" apps={[…]} dock={[…]} />
```

<a id="window-stack"></a>

## 93 · Stacked windows `<WindowStack />`

**Position**: Procedure · 2–3 steps; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/wallpaper` — `windows`

2–3 system windows stacked with an offset; each has a small title and one or two lines, and they appear in order during the build step.

**Use when**

- 2–3 steps, each with its own file or tool
- A deck in the desktop style

**Avoid**

- More than 3 steps: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- windows 2–3: title ≤ 16 characters, heading ≤ 14 chars, lines 1–3, each ≤ 26 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `windows` | `{ title, heading, lines }[]` | yes | Windows, the last one in front |

**Example**

```tsx
<WindowStack kicker="一页怎么来" title="…" windows={[{ title: 'index.tsx', heading: '写内容', lines: ['…'] }, …]} />
```

<a id="toggle-card"></a>

## 94 · Toggle settings window `<ToggleCard />`

**Position**: Key points · which are on, which are off; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/wallpaper` — `toggles`

A settings window where each row has an icon, a name, a note and a toggle. The toggles can be clicked live, but it is only illustrative and controls nothing.

**Use when**

- Default settings, feature switches or options
- A deck in the desktop style

**Avoid**

- The point is whether a state is good or bad: use StatusGrid

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 3–5: label ≤ 10 chars, note ≤ 24 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars

**Interaction**: Clicking a toggle switches it; illustrative only

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `file` | `string` | yes | Window title |
| `rows` | `{ icon, label, note, on, spot? }[]` | yes | Setting rows |

**Example**

```tsx
<ToggleCard kicker="放映" title="…" file="放映设置" rows={[{ icon: 'monitor', label: '全屏', note: 'F 键', on: true }, …]} />
```

<a id="pyramid"></a>

## 95 · Pyramid `<Pyramid />`

**Position**: Structure · layers of dependent conditions; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `ship`

3–5 trapezoid layers stacked into a pyramid: the bottom layer is widest and palest, each layer has one line of text on its right, and they appear bottom-up during the build step.

**Use when**

- Preconditions, levels, layers of need
- The layer below is the basis for the one above

**Avoid**

- The layers do not depend on each other: use LayerStack
- You need to show a falling count: use Funnel

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- levels 3–5: label ≤ 8 chars, note ≤ 30 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `levels` | `{ label, note }[]` | yes | Write from the top layer down |

**Example**

```tsx
<Pyramid kicker="交付条件" title="…" levels={[{ label: '人工看图', note: '…' }, …, { label: '事实有出处', note: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="venn"></a>

## 96 · Venn diagram `<Venn />`

**Position**: Concept · the overlap of a few things; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `parts-of-deck`

2 or 3 translucent circles overlap; each circle holds a name and one line of text, and a dark pill in the middle holds their overlap.

**Use when**

- Several dimensions together decide one result
- A qualitative point with no numbers

**Avoid**

- You need to compare part sizes: use Donut
- More than 3 sets

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- sets 2–3: label ≤ 8 chars, note ≤ 10 chars
- overlap ≤ 8 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `sets` | `{ label, note, spot? }[]` | yes | Sets |
| `overlap` | `string` | yes | The overlap |

**Example**

```tsx
<Venn kicker="三个开关" title="…" sets={[{ label: '模板', note: '布局与内容上限' }, …]} overlap="一份演示" />
```

<a id="concentric"></a>

## 97 · Concentric circles `<Concentric />`

**Position**: Structure · from the core outward; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `rings`

3–4 concentric rings on the left with the darkest core; a list on the right explains each ring, with dot colors matching the rings.

**Use when**

- Core versus edge, reach of influence, layers of a contents list
- Each ring encloses the one inside it

**Avoid**

- The layers stack vertically: use ArchLayers
- One core plus parallel satellites: use Orbit

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rings 3–4: label ≤ 6 chars, note ≤ 30 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `rings` | `{ label, note }[]` | yes | From the inside out |

**Example**

```tsx
<Concentric kicker="项目结构" title="…" rings={[{ label: '模板', note: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="decision-tree"></a>

## 98 · Decision tree `<DecisionTree />`

**Position**: Analysis · which one to pick when; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/toy3d` — `chart-pick`

A yes / no decision tree read from the top down, with at most two levels of questions and four results; the results are boxed in the accent color.

**Use when**

- Choosing an option or a tool from conditions
