- Every question can be answered yes / no

**Avoid**

- More than two levels of questions: split it over two slides
- You need to break down causes: use IssueTree

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- q ≤ 14 chars, leaf ≤ 8 chars, note ≤ 14 chars
- At most two levels of questions, 4 results

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `root` | `TreeNode` | yes | { q, yes, no } or { leaf, note? } |

**Example**

```tsx
<DecisionTree kicker="选图表" title="想比的是什么？" root={{ q: '数据跟时间有关吗？', yes: { leaf: 'LineChart' }, no: { leaf: 'BarChart' } }} />
```

<a id="fishbone"></a>

## 99 · Fishbone diagram `<Fishbone />`

**Position**: Analysis · causes of one problem; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/wallpaper` — `fishbone`

The fish head on the right holds the problem; 4 or 6 bones run in from above and below, each with a cause category and 1–3 specific causes. The bones slant toward the head and meet the spine.

**Use when**

- Troubleshooting a problem, reviewing an incident
- The causes fall into 4–6 categories

**Avoid**

- The causes have cause chains: use IssueTree
- Fewer than 4 categories

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- problem ≤ 10 chars
- bones 4 or 6: label ≤ 6 chars, causes 1–3, each ≤ 12 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `problem` | `string` | yes | The fish head |
| `bones` | `{ label, causes }[]` | yes | Alternate above and below |

**Example**

```tsx
<Fishbone kicker="排查" title="…" problem="页面看起来乱" bones={[{ label: '文字', causes: ['…'] }, …]} />
```

<a id="swimlane"></a>

## 100 · Swimlane flowchart `<Swimlane />`

**Position**: Roles · who does which step in a process; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/wallpaper` — `swimlane`

Each role gets a horizontal lane, the steps take one column in order and arrows connect them; a step handed to another role gets an accent outline.

**Use when**

- A process across roles, a handoff
- 4–8 steps

**Avoid**

- Only one role: use StepTrack
- You need long-term role division: use Lanes

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- lanes 2–4, each ≤ 6 chars
- steps 4–8, text ≤ 10 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `lanes` | `string[]` | yes | Roles |
| `steps` | `{ lane, text }[]` | yes | In order |

**Example**

```tsx
<Swimlane kicker="分工" title="…" lanes={['作者', 'AI 助手', '检查脚本']} steps={[{ lane: 0, text: '给出事实和出处' }, …]} />
```

<a id="arch-layers"></a>

## 101 · Architecture layers `<ArchLayers />`

**Position**: Mechanism · which layers make up the system; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/wallpaper` — `layers`

3–5 horizontal layers; the layer name and a mono tag sit on the left, a row of component blocks on the right. One layer can be highlighted.

**Use when**

- Software architecture, org levels, tech stack
- Each layer has 2–5 parts

**Avoid**

- Layers contain one another: use Concentric
- Explaining how data flows: use FlowDiagram

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- layers 3–5: name ≤ 8 chars, tag ≤ 16 characters, items 2–5 each ≤ 10 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `layers` | `{ name, tag?, items }[]` | yes | Start from the layer closest to the user |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Which layer is highlighted (from 0) |

**Example**

```tsx
<ArchLayers kicker="架构" title="…" focus={0} layers={[{ name: '演示', tag: 'src/decks', items: ['index.tsx'] }, …]} />
```

<a id="balance"></a>

## 102 · Pros and cons scale `<Balance />`

**Position**: Argument · weighing both sides; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/wallpaper` — `balance`

A balance with a set of reasons on each pan; the heavier pan sinks and the pans always hang vertically. The tilt angle comes from weight, so mark the tilt as illustrative in source when there is no measurement.

**Use when**

- Pros and cons, cost and benefit, trade-offs
- Showing which side weighs more

**Avoid**

- Comparing attributes item by item: use CompareTable
- The two sides have no basis and you do not want to mark the tilt illustrative

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- left / right: label ≤ 6 chars, items 1–3 each ≤ 14 chars
- weight only sets the tilt, 9° at most

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `left` | `{ label, items, weight }` | yes | Left pan |
| `right` | `{ label, items, weight }` | yes | Right pan |

**Example**

```tsx
<Balance kicker="取舍" title="…" left={{ label: '代价', items: ['…'], weight: 1 }} right={{ label: '好处', items: ['…'], weight: 2 }} source="…；天平倾斜是示意" />
```

<a id="double-funnel"></a>

## 103 · Double funnel comparison `<DoubleFunnel />`

**Position**: Comparison · two groups going through the same stages; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/wallpaper` — `funnel`

Two funnels back to back, the stage names in a middle column, each side's bars scaled by value, with the percentage relative to the first stage.

**Use when**

- Conversion comparison of two groups or two versions
- 3–5 identical stages

**Avoid**

- Only one group: use Funnel
- Different stages: split into two slides

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- stages 3–5 each ≤ 8 chars
- values match stages one to one and never increase
- name ≤ 8 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `stages` | `string[]` | yes | Stage names |
| `left` | `{ name, values }` | yes | Left side |
| `right` | `{ name, values }` | yes | Right side |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |

**Example**

```tsx
<DoubleFunnel kicker="对比" title="…" unit="页" stages={['全部页面', '写了出处', '出处 + 逐条展开']} left={{ name: 'GEO 演示', values: [31, 24, 4] }} right={{ name: 'GTM 演示', values: [30, 23, 10] }} />
```

<a id="key-reveal"></a>

## 104 · Gradient title reveal `<KeyReveal />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1 of a launch, or a one-line reveal; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/keynote` — `reveal`

One oversized, tightly tracked title in the centre of a pure black canvas, the keywords filled with a gradient, one line of small grey text below and up to 3 small numbers at the bottom.

**Use when**

- Launch openings, new version reveals
- One sentence is the whole theme

**Avoid**

- Details need explaining: use Cover
- The sentence is longer than 16 chars: use Statement

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars
- marks 0–2, must be substrings of title
- sub ≤ 36 chars
- facts 0–3: value ≤ 6 characters, label ≤ 10 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `marks` | `string[]` |  | Words filled with the gradient |
| `sub` | `string` |  | One extra line |
| `facts` | `{ value, label }[]` |  | Small numbers at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<KeyReveal kicker="PCafe · 第 4 期" title={['十种新风格，', '一个文件。']} marks={['十种新风格']} />
```

<a id="key-spec"></a>

## 105 · Giant spec number `<KeySpec />`

**Position**: Evidence · one spec number; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/keynote` — `size`

A huge gradient number and unit filling half the slide, one point above it, one caption and up to 3 small specs below.

**Use when**

- Product specs, key metrics
- One number is the argument

**Avoid**

- Comparing several numbers: use StatRow
- Showing change: use CountCompare

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 1 line × 16 chars
- value ≤ 5 characters, unit ≤ 4 characters
- caption ≤ 30 chars
- specs 0–3: value ≤ 7 characters, label ≤ 12 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `value` | `string` | yes | The big number |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `caption` | `string` | yes | What the number measures |
| `specs` | `{ value, label }[]` |  | Small specs at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<KeySpec kicker="单文件" title="整份演示，就是一个 HTML" value="6.1" unit="MB" caption="…" />
```

<a id="orb-cover"></a>

## 106 · Gradient orb cover `<OrbCover />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1 for creative or brand decks; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gradient` — `cover`

Large title and lead on the left, a diffuse gradient orb on the right, with 2–3 frosted-glass number cards floating over the orb.

**Use when**

- Covers for brand, creative or feature launches
- There are 2–3 representative numbers

**Avoid**

- Formal report: use Cover
- Only one number: use FloatCover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 3 lines × 6 chars
- lead ≤ 48 chars
- chips 2–3: value ≤ 6 characters, label ≤ 10 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `chips` | `{ value, label }[]` | yes | Frosted-glass number card |

**Example**

```tsx
<OrbCover kicker="配色 × 风格" title={['三个开关，', '换一身', '新衣服']} chips={[{ value: '5', label: '套配色' }, …]} />
```

<a id="gradient-cards"></a>

## 107 · Gradient feature cards `<GradientCards />`

**Position**: Parallel · 3 features or options; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/gradient` — `switches`

Three large rounded frosted-glass cards; the top of each is a gradient panel in a different hue with a glass icon disc, and below it a tag, title and body.

**Use when**

- 3 parallel features, switches or options
- One or two sentences for each item

**Avoid**

- Order matters: use StepTrack
- More than 3 items: use IconGrid

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items exactly 3: title ≤ 10 chars, body 24–48 chars, tag ≤ 18 characters
- title ≤ 2 lines × 9 chars
- lead ≤ 60 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `items` | `{ icon, tag?, title, body }[]` | yes | Three cards |

**Example**

```tsx
<GradientCards kicker="三个开关" title="…" items={[{ icon: 'stars', tag: 'DeckDef.theme', title: '配色', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="block-stats"></a>

## 108 · Colour block numbers `<BlockStats />`

**Position**: Data · 3–4 hard metrics; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/pop` — `numbers`

3–4 saturated blocks with a thick black outline, hard shadow and slight tilt; each block has a large condensed number, a label and one note. The first block uses the theme colour.

**Use when**

- 3–4 numbers of equal importance
- Marketing, rule walkthroughs

**Avoid**

- Comparing sizes: use MetricBars
- Only one number: use BigStat

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- stats 3–4: value ≤ 5 characters, unit ≤ 3 characters, label ≤ 8 chars, note ≤ 24 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `stats` | `{ value, unit?, label, note }[]` | yes | Blocks |

**Example**

```tsx
<BlockStats kicker="硬指标" title="…" stats={[{ value: '17', unit: 'u', label: '最小字号', note: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="pop-poster"></a>

## 109 · Pop slogan poster `<PopPoster />`

**Position**: Opening / pause · one slogan; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/pop` — `poster`

Two or three lines of oversized bold slogan, one word inside a tilted colour block, a round sticker number in the top right corner and a row of outlined tags at the bottom.

**Use when**

- Cover, slogan, rule walkthrough
- One keyword in the sentence

**Avoid**

- You need to argue: use Statement
- The sentence is longer than 14 chars

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title 2–3 lines × 7 chars
- mark must be a substring of title
- badge.value ≤ 4 characters, label ≤ 6 chars
- tags 0–4, each ≤ 12 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `mark` | `string` |  | Word inside the colour block |
| `badge` | `{ value, label }` |  | Round sticker |
| `tags` | `string[]` |  | Tags at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<PopPoster kicker="交付规则" title={['不出 PASS，', '就不交付']} mark="PASS" badge={{ value: '4', label: '种窗口' }} />
```

<a id="bento-board"></a>

## 110 · Grid overview `<BentoBoard />`

**Position**: Overview · the whole picture on one slide; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/bento` — `board`

Six white blocks of different sizes: a large block top left for the title and lead, two number blocks and one vertical text block on the right, a horizontal icon block at the bottom and an accent-colour number block bottom right. Unlike the expandable Bento, it is a static one-slide overview.

**Use when**

- A one-slide overview of a product or project
- There are 3 numbers, one rule and 4–6 feature points

**Avoid**

- Details open on click: use Bento
- Only one number: use BigStat

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars, lead ≤ 40 chars
- stats exactly 3: label ≤ 14 characters, value ≤ 6 characters, unit ≤ 4 characters, note ≤ 20 chars
- note.text ≤ 70 chars
- chips.items 4–6, label ≤ 6 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines manually with an array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `stats` | `[BoardStat × 3]` | yes | Three number blocks, the last one in the accent colour |
| `note` | `{ label, text }` | yes | Vertical text block |
| `chips` | `{ label, items: { icon, label }[] }` | yes | Horizontal icon block |

**Example**

```tsx
<BentoBoard kicker="全貌" title={['一个文件，', '装下整份演示']} stats={[{ label: 'TEMPLATES', value: '251', unit: '个', note: '…' }, …]} note={{ label: 'RULE', text: '…' }} chips={{ label: 'KEYS', items: [{ icon: 'monitor', label: '全屏 F' }, …] }} />
```

<a id="bento-spotlight"></a>

## 111 · Bento spotlight `<BentoSpotlight />`

**Position**: Note · one feature explained in full; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/bento` — `single-file`

A set of blocks under the title: a tall block on the left with icon, title and text, two number blocks top right and a wide block bottom right with mono key-value rows.

**Use when**

- Introducing one feature, one file, one command
- Two key numbers and 3–4 key-value rows

**Avoid**

- Several parallel features: use IconGrid
- The numbers are the point: use StatRow

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- hero.title ≤ 10 chars, hero.text ≤ 60 chars
- cells exactly 2: value ≤ 8 characters, note ≤ 18 chars
- rows.items 3–4 rows: k ≤ 16 characters, v ≤ 22 chars
- title ≤ 1 line × 14 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `hero` | `{ icon, label, title, text }` | yes | Tall block on the left |
| `cells` | `[cell, cell]` | yes | Two number blocks, the first in the accent colour |
| `rows` | `{ label, items: { k, v }[] }` | yes | Key-value rows |

**Example**

```tsx
<BentoSpotlight kicker="构建" title="断网也能放映" hero={{ icon: 'file-check', label: 'dist/index.html', title: '一个离线文件', text: '…' }} cells={[…]} rows={{ label: 'NPM SCRIPTS', items: [{ k: 'npm run check', v: '…' }] }} />
```

<a id="slot-picker"></a>

## 112 · Calendar time slots `<SlotPicker />`

**Position**: Schedule · date and time slot; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/soft-saas` — `booking`

Title on the left, a booking card on the right: a month calendar marks the available dates and the right column lists that day's time slots. Both dates and time slots switch on click.

**Use when**

- Scheduling a review, training or Q&A session
- A small product-style interface in the soft style

**Avoid**

- A real long-term schedule: use Gantt or Timeline
- The dates are not real and are not marked illustrative

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- card.title ≤ 12 chars, meta ≤ 10 characters
- month ≤ 10 characters, days 28–31, start is the weekday of the 1st (Monday = 0)
- slots 3–5: time ≤ 8 characters, note ≤ 8 chars
- when the schedule is not real, write illustrative in source

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `card` | `{ title, meta }` | yes | Card title and duration |
| `month` | `string` | yes | Month |
| `start` | `number` | yes | Weekday of the 1st |
| `days` | `number` | yes | Number of days |
| `open` | `number[]` | yes | Available dates |
| `day` | `number` | yes | Selected date |
| `slots` | `{ time, note? }[]` | yes | Time slots |
| `pick` | `number` |  | Selected time slot, default 0 |

**Example**

```tsx
<SlotPicker kicker="评审" title={['约个时间，', '一起逐页看截图']} card={{ title: '截图评审', meta: '30 分钟' }} month="2026 年 11 月" start={6} days={30} open={[3, 4, 5]} day={4} slots={[{ time: '10:00', note: '上午' }, …]} source="示意" />
```

<a id="ui-steps"></a>

## 113 · UI step cards `<UiSteps />`

**Position**: Procedure · getting started in three steps; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/soft-saas` — `steps`

Three rounded step cards; the top half of each is a small interface (input field, option pills or a done message) and the bottom half holds the step number, title and text.

**Use when**

- Three-step flows such as setup, onboarding or creating something
- Each step can be drawn as a simple interface action

**Avoid**

- Steps carry commands and there are more than 3: use StepTrack
- The steps have no order: use NumberedColumns

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps exactly 3: title ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 36 chars
- ui: field (label ≤ 10 chars, value ≤ 24 characters) / options (2–4, each ≤ 8 characters) / done (≤ 14 chars)

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `steps` | `[UiStep × 3]` | yes | Steps and their small interfaces |

**Example**

```tsx
<UiSteps kicker="新建演示" title="三步，从空目录到 PASS" steps={[{ title: '建目录', text: '…', ui: { kind: 'field', label: '演示文件', value: 'src/decks/demo/index.tsx' } }, …]} />
```

<a id="float-hero"></a>

## 114 · Floating tag cover `<FloatHero />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1 for a product or feature; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/float-ui` — `hero`

A quiet centred title and lead, a small card with a waveform below, and 4–6 slightly tilted icon tags floating around.

**Use when**

- Openings for AI, voice or tool products
- 4–6 features each describable in one word

**Avoid**

- Formal report: use Cover
- Features need explaining: use IconGrid

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 12 chars, lead ≤ 48 chars
- chips 4–6, label ≤ 10 characters
- card.label ≤ 12 characters, card.text ≤ 22 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `chips` | `{ icon, label }[]` | yes | Floating tags |
| `card` | `{ label, text }` | yes | Small card with the waveform |

**Example**

```tsx
<FloatHero kicker="第 4 期 · 浮窗风格" title={['讲的时候，', '只管往下讲']} lead="…" chips={[{ icon: 'monitor', label: '全屏 F' }, …]} card={{ label: 'AUTOPLAY', text: '每 8 秒前进一步' }} />
```

<a id="transcript-card"></a>

## 115 · Transcript card `<TranscriptCard />`

**Position**: Citation · a stretch of dialogue or narration; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/float-ui` — `narrate`

Title on the left, a transcript card with a waveform on the right, 3–4 spoken lines with one highlighted, and 2–3 small tags floating beside the card.

**Use when**

- Quoting a recording, interview or narration
- You want listeners to notice one line

**Avoid**

- Only one quotation: use QuoteHero
- The transcript is made up and not marked illustrative

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- lines 3–4: who ≤ 6 chars, time ≤ 6 characters, text ≤ 34 chars
- file ≤ 16 characters, meta ≤ 8 characters
- tags 2–3, label ≤ 8 chars
- when the recording is not real, write illustrative in source

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `file` | `string` | yes | Card title |
| `meta` | `string` | yes | Duration |
| `lines` | `{ who, time, text }[]` | yes | Transcript lines |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Highlighted line, default 0 |
| `tags` | `{ icon, label }[]` | yes | Floating tags |

**Example**

```tsx
<TranscriptCard kicker="逐条展开" title={['讲到哪一条，', '就亮到哪一条']} file="讲稿 · 第 5 页" meta="00:24" focus={1} lines={[{ who: '讲者', time: '00:00', text: '…' }, …]} tags={[{ icon: 'keyboard', label: '→ / Space' }]} source="讲稿为示意" />
```

<a id="retro-tabs"></a>

## 116 · Retro tab window `<RetroTabs />`

**Position**: Grouping · 2–4 parallel lists; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/desktop` — `tabs`

Title on the left, an old-style window with a thick black border on the right, clickable folder tabs along the top and 3–4 icon rows inside each tab.

**Use when**

- Lists grouped by role, stage or scenario
- Clicking through the tabs live, group by group

**Avoid**

- Only one group: use IconGrid
- A screenshot shows only the first tab, so put the key content in the first group

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- tabs 2–4, label ≤ 6 chars
- rows 3–4 per group: title ≤ 10 chars, note ≤ 26 chars
- file ≤ 20 characters
- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `file` | `string` | yes | Window title |
| `tabs` | `{ label, rows: { icon, title, note }[] }[]` | yes | Tabs and rows |
| `open` | `number` |  | Tab open by default |

**Example**

```tsx
<RetroTabs kicker="分工" title={['点开标签，', '看每一步做什么']} file="WORKFLOW.EXE" tabs={[{ label: '写演示', rows: [{ icon: 'folder', title: '新建目录', note: '…' }, …] }, …]} />
```

<a id="retro-log"></a>

## 117 · Retro changelog `<RetroLog />`

**Position**: Recap · version or stage changes; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/desktop` — `log`

A changelog inside an old-style window: version, tag and one line each, with the newest tag in the accent colour; a small sticky note window sits at an angle beside it.

**Use when**

- Version updates, stage recaps
- One sentence per entry

**Avoid**

- Needs a true time scale: use Timeline
- Several paragraphs per entry: use Milestones

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- entries 4–6, newest first: ver ≤ 10 characters, tag ≤ 6 chars, text ≤ 30 chars
- note.title ≤ 10 characters, lines 2–3 each ≤ 16 chars
- title ≤ 1 line × 16 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `file` | `string` | yes | Window title |
| `entries` | `{ ver, tag, text }[]` | yes | Log entries |
| `note` | `{ title, lines }` | yes | Sticky note window |

**Example**

```tsx
<RetroLog kicker="更新日志" title="四期下来，模板从 67 到 103" file="CHANGELOG.txt" entries={[{ ver: '第 4 期', tag: '风格', text: '…' }, …]} note={{ title: 'NOTE.txt', lines: ['风格只换外观', '…'] }} />
```

<a id="glow-metric"></a>

## 118 · Glowing big number `<GlowMetric />`

**Position**: Data · one core number and three supporting numbers; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/warm-dark` — `size`

Title and lead on the left; on the right a huge serif italic number floating on a warm glow, with a hairline-separated row of three supporting numbers below. The signature data slide of the warm dark style.

**Use when**

- One number you most want people to remember
- 3 more numbers that support it

**Avoid**

- Numbers need comparing: use CountCompare
- No supporting numbers: use BigStat

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- figure ≤ 5 characters, unit ≤ 4 characters
- caption ≤ 30 chars
- subs exactly 3: value ≤ 8 characters, label ≤ 16 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `figure` | `string` | yes | The big number |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `caption` | `string` | yes | What the number measures |
| `subs` | `{ value, label }[]` | yes | Three supporting numbers at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<GlowMetric kicker="交付物" title={['整份演示，', '只有一个文件']} figure="6.1" unit="MB" caption="单文件构建的大小" subs={[{ value: '3.6 MB', label: '其中是内嵌字体' }, …]} />
```

<a id="hairline-grid"></a>

## 119 · Hairline feature grid `<HairlineGrid />`

**Position**: Parallel · 4 or 6 features; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/warm-dark` — `switches`

1px hairlines under the title split the area into 2×2 or 3×2 cells; each cell has an icon, a mono tag, a title and one note, and warm light glows from the bottom of the focused cell.

**Use when**

- 4 or 6 parallel features, fields or capabilities
- One of them matters most

**Avoid**

- One word per item is enough: use TileWall
- Order matters: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 4 or 6: title ≤ 10 chars, body 18–40 chars, tag ≤ 16 characters
- title ≤ 1 line × 14 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `items` | `{ icon, title, body, tag? }[]` | yes | Cells |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Glowing cell, default 0 |

**Example**

```tsx
<HairlineGrid kicker="六个开关" title="一份演示，六个字段定风格" items={[{ icon: 'tuning', title: '风格', tag: 'DeckDef.style', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="ribbon-cover"></a>

## 120 · Ribbon cover `<RibbonCover />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1 for events and training; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/ribbon` — `cover`

An oversized rounded title top left; two thick ribbons, one blue and one black, cross at an angle in the lower right and repeat one word, plus a thin lime ribbon.

**Use when**

- Covers for training, events and external introductions
- A lively, friendly opening

**Avoid**

- Formal report: use Cover
- Numbers to show: use FloatCover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- band, band2 ≤ 12 characters, only repeat words already on the slide

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `band` | `string` | yes | Word repeated on the main ribbon |
| `band2` | `string` |  | Word on the black ribbon, defaults to band |

**Example**

```tsx
<RibbonCover kicker="第 4 期 · 彩带风格" title={['做演示，', '先守住规则']} band="PCAFE" band2="PASS" />
```

<a id="ribbon-section"></a>

## 121 · Ribbon section break `<RibbonSection />`

**Position**: Rhythm · first slide of each chapter; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/ribbon` — `ch1`

A wide accent-colour ribbon runs across the slide at an angle, holding the chapter number badge and the chapter title; below it the lead on the left and this chapter's slide titles on the right.

**Use when**

- Chaptered decks, at the start of each chapter
- Stands in for SectionBreak in the ribbon style

**Avoid**

- Short decks without chapters
- A dark slide is needed: use SectionBreak

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title one line ≤ 8 chars
- lead ≤ 44 chars
- contents 2–4 items, each ≤ 12 chars
- no source: a section break states no facts

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Chapter title on the ribbon; the slide title also accepts an array to break lines |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `index` | `number` | yes | Chapter number |
| `contents` | `string[]` | yes | Titles of this chapter's slides |

**Example**

```tsx
<RibbonSection kicker="第一章" index={1} title="按顺序做" contents={['四个关键步骤', '六条文案规则']} />
```

<a id="chat-thread"></a>

## 122 · Chat Q&A `<ChatThread />`

**Position**: Q&A · 3–6 messages; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/playful` — `chat`

Title, lead and a hand-drawn wavy arrow on the left, a column of chat bubbles on the right: questions on the left in white, answers on the right in the accent colour. Each bubble is one build step.

**Use when**

- Beginner questions, user questions
- Making Q&A read like a conversation

**Avoid**

- More than 3 questions that need sources: use Faq
- The answers are long

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- messages 3–6: question ≤ 24 chars, answer ≤ 44 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 7 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- names each ≤ 6 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `messages` | `{ from: 'q' \| 'a', text }[]` | yes | The conversation |
| `names` | `{ q, a }` |  | Names for the two sides, default Question / Answer |

**Example**

```tsx
<ChatThread kicker="新手问答" title={['第一次上手，', '先问这三句']} messages={[{ from: 'q', text: '新演示放在哪里？' }, { from: 'a', text: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="note-cards"></a>

## 123 · Tilted sticky notes `<NoteCards />`

**Position**: Parallel · 3–4 rules or tips; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/playful` — `rules`

3–4 candy-coloured sticky notes, each slightly tilted with a strip of tape on top; each has an icon, a title and one line of text.

**Use when**

- 3–4 rules, tips or cautions
- A relaxed share-out session

**Avoid**

- Formal setting: use NumberedColumns
- More than 4 items: use IconGrid

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- notes 3–4: title ≤ 8 chars, body 20–40 chars
- title ≤ 1 line × 14 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `notes` | `{ icon, title, body }[]` | yes | Sticky notes |

**Example**

```tsx
<NoteCards kicker="视觉规则" title="模板做好的事，别去破坏" notes={[{ icon: 'code', title: '不写 CSS', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="reveal-statement"></a>

## 124 · Word-by-word statement `<RevealStatement />`

**Position**: Pause · one line to be remembered; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/keynote` — `reveal`

One large line in the centre of the slide; on entry each character rises from below one by one (Latin words appear whole), then a small line fades in. Screen readers read the whole sentence.

**Use when**

- The one line of the deck you most want remembered
- A pause slide between chapters

**Avoid**

- More than twice in one deck
- The sentence needs emphasis or an icon: use Statement

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- text ≤ 24 chars; when split, ≤ 2 lines × 12 chars
- sub ≤ 36 chars
- kicker 2–8 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `text` | `string \| string[]` | yes | The sentence that appears character by character, an array splits the lines |
| `sub` | `string` |  | A small line that fades in after the sentence |

**Example**

```tsx
<RevealStatement
  kicker="一句话"
  text={['把演示做成', '一个单文件 HTML']}
  sub="离线打开，不需要网络。"
/>
```

<a id="sketch-cover"></a>

## 125 · Drawing board cover `<SketchCover />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1 for a product home page or portfolio; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-nuance` — `cover`

A large iridescent rounded panel with a two-line serif title in the centre, the keyword of the first line pressed into a tilted orange tag; below it a dotted drawing board with one line circled in orange pen, and a row of red dot pills at the bottom of the panel.

**Use when**

- Openings for a product home page, tool introduction or portfolio
- There is a command or slogan that can be written on the board

**Avoid**

- Formal report: use Cover
- No word in the title can sit alone in the tag: use HangingCover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- pre ≤ 6 chars, tag ≤ 4 chars, line ≤ 12 chars
- lead ≤ 48 chars
- canvas ≤ 30 characters, one line
- chips 3–5, each ≤ 8 chars
- write source when there are facts

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–12 chars |
| `pre` | `string` | yes | Text before the tag on the first line |
| `tag` | `string` | yes | Word pressed into the orange tag |
| `line` | `string` | yes | Second line |
| `lead` | `string` | yes | Lead |
| `canvas` | `string` | yes | The circled line on the drawing board |
| `chips` | `string[]` | yes | Red dot pills |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |

**Example**

```tsx
<SketchCover kicker="开源 · CPAL-1.0" pre="让 AI 做" tag="演示" line="每一页都整整齐齐" lead="…" canvas="npx @pcafe/pcafe init my-talk" chips={['251 个模板', '33 种风格', '自动 QA']} source="…" />
```

<a id="arc-cards"></a>

## 126 · Arc dark cards `<ArcCards />`

**Position**: Parallel · five equally important mechanisms or services; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-nuance` — `how`

A centred serif title, with five dark cards along a shallow arc below, tilted inward at both ends. Each card holds an icon in an orange corner frame, a title and one line of text. With a build step, they appear one by one.

**Use when**

- Exactly 5 parallel mechanisms, services or features
- Each one fits in a single sentence

**Avoid**

- 3–4 items: use NumberedColumns
- 6–8 items: use IconGrid
- The items have an order: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards exactly 5: title ≤ 8 chars, text 16–34 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 16 chars
- Icons do not repeat on one slide

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `cards` | `[{ icon, title, text } × 5]` | yes | The cards |

**Example**

```tsx
<ArcCards kicker="怎么做到" title="五条规则，管住每一页" cards={[{ icon: 'layers', title: '固定模板', text: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="case-card"></a>

## 127 · Case card `<CaseCard />`

**Position**: Evidence · one project or feature with a real screenshot; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-nuance` — `gallery`

One large white rounded card: a serif title, a paragraph and small grey tags at the bottom left; a real screenshot on the right, with a dark ticker under it carrying the project keywords.

**Use when**

- Show one feature, project or result with a real screenshot
- Portfolio, case study

**Avoid**

- The screenshot needs callouts: use Screenshot
- Switching between several views: use TabbedShot
- You have no real screenshot

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 3 lines × 9 chars
- lead ≤ 70 chars
- tags 2–3, each ≤ 10 chars
- ticker 2–3, each ≤ 14 characters
- image must be a real screenshot

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One explanatory paragraph |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |
| `image` | `string` | yes | Imported real screenshot |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Screenshot description |
| `tags` | `string[]` | yes | Small grey tags |
| `ticker` | `string[]` | yes | Ticker keywords |

**Example**

```tsx
<CaseCard kicker="模板库" title={['251 个模板，', '先看再选']} lead="…" image={shotKit} alt="组件库页面截图" tags={['#/kit', '33 种风格']} ticker={['TEMPLATES', 'STYLES']} />
```

<a id="agency-hero"></a>

## 128 · Growth site hero `<AgencyHero />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1 for a product or proposal; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-northbound` — `hero`

A small neon-green badge on top, two or three tight oversized black lines on the left that may embed coloured icon squares, a black status pill at bottom left, a lead with pill buttons at bottom right, and a row of light names along the bottom.

**Use when**

- A product-site style opening
- Cover for a marketing or growth proposal

**Avoid**

- Formal report: use Cover
- Large numbers to show: use NumberOpener

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- lines 2–3, each ≤ 7 chars (or 12 letters), at most 2 lines with an icon
- badge ≤ 24 chars; tag ≤ 16 chars
- lead ≤ 64 chars
- actions 1–2, each ≤ 10 characters
- names 0–6, text only; namesLabel ≤ 16 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `badge` | `string` | yes | Neon-green badge at the top |
| `lines` | `{ text, icon? }[]` | yes | Lines of the big title |
| `tag` | `string` | yes | Black pill at bottom left |
| `lead` | `string` | yes | Lead in the bottom right |
| `actions` | `string[]` | yes | Pill button labels, the first one solid |
| `names` | `string[]` |  | Names along the bottom |
| `namesLabel` | `string` |  | Small text above the names |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |

**Example**

```tsx
<AgencyHero badge="开源 · CPAL-1.0" lines={[{ text: '让 AI 做的', icon: 'magic' }, { text: '幻灯片' }]} tag="HTML 演示引擎" lead="…" actions={['开始使用', 'GitHub']} />
```

<a id="agency-rows"></a>

## 129 · Numbered service rows `<AgencyRows />`

**Position**: Content · 4–5 items, one per row; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-northbound` — `how`

A big title with an outlined pill on the right, then numbered rows divided by hairlines: number, bold title, short grey note, coloured icon square and a round plus.

**Use when**

- What we do, or which practices we follow
- Each item is one title plus one short note

**Avoid**

- Each item needs two or more sentences: use NumberedColumns
- There are steps in order: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 12 chars
- more ≤ 10 characters
- items 4–5 rows: title ≤ 14 chars, note ≤ 22 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; pass an array to break lines manually |
| `more` | `string` |  | Outlined pill on the right |
| `items` | `{ title, note?, icon }[]` | yes | The rows |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |

**Example**

```tsx
<AgencyRows kicker="01 做法" title={['模板管排版，', 'AI 只管内容']} items={[{ title: '只组合现成模板', note: '…', icon: 'widget' }, …]} />
```

<a id="agency-manifesto"></a>

## 130 · Point-of-view paragraph `<AgencyManifesto />`

**Position**: Rhythm · one paragraph of a position; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-northbound` — `problem`

A small grey pill label, then a full block of tight oversized bold text, indented on the left, with 0–2 words in the accent colour.

**Use when**

- State a problem or a position that one line cannot cover
- A pause between two dense blocks

**Avoid**

- Only one short line: use Statement
- A sourced quotation: use QuoteHero

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- text ≤ 64 chars
- label ≤ 8 chars
- marks 0–2, each must appear verbatim in text

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `label` | `string` | yes | Small pill label |
| `icon` | `IconName` |  | Icon in the label |
| `text` | `string` | yes | The paragraph of the position |
| `marks` | `string[]` |  | Words in the accent colour |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts |

**Example**

```tsx
<AgencyManifesto label="我们的看法" text="…" marks={['固定的版式']} />
```

<a id="halo-hero"></a>

## 131 · Halo display cover `<HaloHero />`

**Position**: Opening / closing · first or last slide in a product- or brand-home style; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-nutshell` — `cover`

Two or three centred light display lines; one word in line 2 turns italic serif; a striped pill sits at the end of line 1 and line 3 fades out; a soft multi-colour halo behind. Optionally a command pill and a link for the closing.

**Use when**

- Product-home style opening with one value statement
- Closing slide with a command to start and the repo address

**Avoid**

- Formal report: use Cover
- Key numbers to show: use OrbCover or Cover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- lines 2–3, each ≤ 10 chars; with 3 lines the last one fades out
- serif must appear verbatim in line 2, ≤ 4 chars
- kicker ≤ 16 characters
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- command ≤ 32 characters, ASCII only; link ≤ 40 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small caps line above the title |
| `lines` | `string[]` | yes | 2–3 display lines |
| `serif` | `string` |  | Word in line 2 set in italic serif |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One-sentence lead |
| `command` | `string` |  | Dark command pill |
| `link` | `string` |  | Link text next to the command |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<HaloHero kicker="你好，这是 PCafe" lines={['让 AI 做演示', '每一页都整齐', '还能直接放映。']} serif="整齐" lead="…" />
```

<a id="halo-rows"></a>

## 132 · Hairline item list `<HaloRows />`

**Position**: Parallel · 3–6 features, rules or shortcuts; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-nutshell` — `present`

A small label, title and lead on the left; a column of hairline-separated items on the right: round outlined icon, title, one short line of detail and a plus.

**Use when**

- 3–6 parallel features, rules or shortcuts
- Each item is one short title plus one short line

**Avoid**

- Each item needs two or more sentences: use NumberedColumns
- There is an order: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–6: title ≤ 12 chars, detail ≤ 22 chars
- title ≤ 2 lines × 9 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines by hand in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two sentences of lead |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |
| `items` | `{ icon, title, detail }[]` | yes | The items |

**Example**

```tsx
<HaloRows kicker="放映" title={['讲的时候，', '工具都在键盘上']} items={[{ icon: 'presentation', title: '演讲者视图', detail: 'S：备注、计时、下一页' }, …]} />
```

<a id="halo-marquee"></a>

## 133 · Marquee showcase `<HaloMarquee />`

**Position**: Showcase · two screenshots or pieces of work; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-nutshell` — `showcase`

One oversized line scrolling sideways in a loop, with two large image cards below, each with a title and small grey tags.

**Use when**

- Show two real screenshots or pieces of work
- A transition slide that should keep moving

**Avoid**

- The screenshot needs callouts: use Screenshot
- More than two items: use WorkCards

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- band ≤ 6 chars
- items exactly 2: title ≤ 16 chars, tags 1–3 each ≤ 8 characters
- src must be a real screenshot or a decor- illustration

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `band` | `string` | yes | The scrolling display line |
| `items` | `{ src, alt, title, tags }[]` | yes | The two image cards |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<HaloMarquee kicker="模板与风格" band="挑一个模板" items={[{ src: shotKit, alt: '…', title: '…', tags: ['#/kit'] }, …]} />
```

<a id="fan-cover"></a>

## 134 · Fan screenshot cover `<FanCover />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1, product-site style; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-cobalt` — `cover`

A full page of saturated blue with tone: accent, a centred pill badge, a tight big title and lead, two pill buttons with round arrows, and a row of fan-tilted screenshot cards rising from the bottom edge.

**Use when**

- Cover for a product, tool or open-source project
- You have 3–5 real screenshots to show

**Avoid**

- No real screenshots: use OrbCover or Cover
- Formal report: use Cover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- badge.text ≤ 20 chars, icons 1–3
- title 1–2 lines × 12 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- actions 1–2, each ≤ 28 characters
- cards 3–5 real screenshots, aspect ratio about 16:10
- Use with tone: accent

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `badge` | `{ icons, text }` | yes | Pill badge above the title |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines by hand in the array |
| `lead` | `string` | yes | One-sentence lead |
| `actions` | `string[]` | yes | Pill button labels, the first one solid white |
| `cards` | `{ src, alt }[]` | yes | Imported screenshots |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<FanCover badge={{ icons: ['code', 'stars'], text: '开源 · CPAL-1.0' }} title={['让 AI 做的演示，', '每一页都整齐']} lead="…" actions={['npx @pcafe/pcafe init my-talk', 'GitHub 源码']} cards={[{ src: shot, alt: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="pill-sentence"></a>

## 135 · Sentence with icon pills `<PillSentence />`

**Position**: Point of view · one big line that states the problem; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-cobalt` — `problem`

One full oversized left-aligned sentence with 2–3 coloured icon pills inside it; two columns at the bottom: a small note on the left and a line of text on the right.

**Use when**

- Problem or position slide after the opening
- One line holds 2–3 keywords that icons can carry

**Avoid**

- The line is short and should sit centred as a pause: use Statement
- Numbers to compare: use a chart template

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- parts ≤ 44 chars in total, 2–3 icon pills, each with 1–2 icons
- note.label ≤ 12 chars, note.body ≤ 24 chars
- aside ≤ 64 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `parts` | `(string \| { icons })[]` | yes | Sentence fragments and icon pills, in order |
| `note` | `{ label, body }` |  | Small note at bottom left |
| `aside` | `string` |  | Text at bottom right |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<PillSentence kicker="问题" parts={['常见的结果是', { icons: ['text'] }, '字号乱跳、', …]} note={{ label: '为什么', body: '…' }} aside="…" />
```

<a id="open-list"></a>

## 136 · Expanding list `<OpenList />`

**Position**: Checklist · 5–7 features or questions; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/home-cobalt` — `present`

Title, lead and one pill button on the left; a hairline-separated list on the right where each row may carry a keycap and a plus, and one row opens to show its answer.

**Use when**

- Shortcut, command or feature lists
- A FAQ where only one answer needs to open

**Avoid**

- Every item needs an answer: use Faq
- More than 7 items: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 6 chars; lead ≤ 40 chars; action ≤ 16 chars
- rows 5–7: key ≤ 8 characters, q ≤ 16 chars, a in the open row ≤ 44 chars
- open expands one row only

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines by hand in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `action` | `string` |  | Pill button label |
| `rows` | `{ key?, q, a }[]` | yes | The list rows |
| `open` | `number` |  | Row to expand, default 0 |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<OpenList kicker="放映" title={['讲的时候，', '一个键就够']} rows={[{ key: 'S', q: '演讲者视图', a: '…' }, { key: 'L', q: '激光笔', a: '' }, …]} />
```

<a id="photo-cover"></a>

## 137 · Photo cover `<PhotoCover />`

**Position**: Opening · one image sets the tone; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `cover`

One image fills the page, with a vignette and a gradient darkening the bottom left, where a white title, subtitle and one line of meta sit. The image pushes in slowly.

**Use when**

- You have an atmosphere image that carries the topic: landscape, scene, object
- First slide of a talk, a science explainer or a brand story

**Avoid**

- No suitable image: use Cover
- Numbers and key facts on the cover: use Cover or NumberOpener

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- sub ≤ 40 chars
- meta 0–3, each ≤ 12 chars
- image.credit ≤ 20 chars
- Must use tone: 'dark'

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `image` | `{ src, alt, credit?, focus? }` | yes | Imported decor- illustration or screenshot; credit shows at bottom right |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Main title |
| `sub` | `string` |  | Subtitle |
| `meta` | `string[]` |  | Small text at the bottom: duration, occasion |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<PhotoCover image={{ src: reef, alt: '…', credit: '插图：AI 生成（示意）' }} kicker="海洋科普" title={['珊瑚礁：', '海里的城市']} sub="…" meta={['12 分钟']} />
```

<a id="photo-split"></a>

## 138 · Half image, half text `<PhotoSplit />`

**Position**: Explanation · one image with one block of text; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `animal`

Half the page is a full-bleed image against the edge, left or right; the other half holds the title, lead and 0–3 numbered points that appear one by one.

**Use when**

- Introduce an object or concept where an image helps
- A chapter needs a slide that breathes

**Avoid**

- The image is a screenshot to read closely: use Screenshot or DeviceFrame
- More than 3 points: split the slide or use NumberedColumns

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 3 lines × 9 chars
- lead ≤ 60 chars
- points 0–3: title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 36 chars
- With a lead, use ≤ 3 points; when the bodies are full, prefer ≤ 2

**Interaction**: build: points appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `image` | `{ src, alt, credit?, focus? }` | yes | A portrait or square image works better |
| `side` | `'left' \| 'right'` |  | Which side the image is on, default left |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `points` | `{ title, body }[]` |  | Numbered points, one build step each |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<PhotoSplit image={{ src: coral, alt: '…' }} side="left" kicker="先认识它" title={['珊瑚不是石头，', '是动物']} lead="…" points={[{ title: '水螅体', body: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="photo-mosaic"></a>

## 139 · Photo mosaic `<PhotoMosaic />`

**Position**: Showcase · 3–5 images seen together; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `residents`

An asymmetric image grid below the title; the first image takes the large cell and the rest stagger around it, each with an optional pill caption, appearing one at a time.

**Use when**

- Show a set of scenes, species or work on one theme
- Set the mood for a number with images

**Avoid**

- Every image needs its own text: use PolaroidWall or PhotoStack
- The images are interface screenshots: use WindowStack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 1 line × 18 chars or 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- tiles 3–5, caption ≤ 12 chars
- The first tile holds the most important image and is the largest

**Interaction**: build: images appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker / title / lead / source` | `SlideBase` |  | Base header fields: see the shared notes below |
| `tiles` | `{ image, caption? }[]` | yes | 3–5 images, each one build step |

**Example**

```tsx
<PhotoMosaic kicker="生物多样性" title="不到 1% 的海底，约 25% 的物种" tiles={[{ image: { src: fish, alt: '…' }, caption: '鱼群' }, …]} />
```

<a id="polaroid-wall"></a>

## 140 · Polaroid wall `<PolaroidWall />`

**Position**: Showcase · a few images each with one line; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `value`

3–5 square instant photos in a row, each slightly rotated with tape at the top and one line of serif italic handwriting below, stuck up one at a time.

**Use when**

- List 3–5 uses, places or memories, one image and one line each
- A light, storytelling tone

**Avoid**

- A serious data report
- Each item needs more than 10 chars: use PhotoStack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 1 line × 18 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- items 3–5, note ≤ 10 chars

**Interaction**: build: the photos appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker / title / lead / source` | `SlideBase` |  | Base header fields: see the shared notes below |
| `items` | `{ image, note }[]` | yes | Instant photos, each one build step |

**Example**

```tsx
<PolaroidWall kicker="价值" title="…" items={[{ image: { src: fish, alt: '…' }, note: '渔获与食物' }, …]} />
```

<a id="film-strip"></a>

## 141 · Film strip `<FilmStrip />`

**Position**: Timeline · 3–5 points with images; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `events`

A black film strip runs across the page with sprocket holes above and below; each frame holds an image with the year printed in its corner, and a numbered caption sits below. Frames not yet shown look undeveloped.

**Use when**

- 3–5 points in time or step order, each with an image
- Recap, history, evolution

**Avoid**

- More than 5 points: use Timeline
- No images: use Milestones

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 1 line × 18 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- frames 3–5: label ≤ 8 chars, mark ≤ 6 characters

**Interaction**: build: each frame develops and shows its caption

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker / title / lead / source` | `SlideBase` |  | Base header fields: see the shared notes below |
| `frames` | `{ image, label, mark? }[]` | yes | Film frames; image and caption appear in the same step |

**Example**

```tsx
<FilmStrip kicker="时间线" title="四次全球白化" frames={[{ image: { src: a, alt: '…' }, label: '约 21% 的礁区', mark: '1998' }, …]} />
```

<a id="magazine-spread"></a>

## 142 · Magazine spread `<MagazineSpread />`

**Position**: Explanation · one block of text to read; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `bleaching`

A full image on the left half with a shadow at the gutter; the right half holds the big title, a standfirst over a thick rule, body text with a drop cap and a pull quote, with a folio and source at the bottom.

**Use when**

- Explain a concept or tell a story that needs two paragraphs of body text
- Editorial style, explainers and features

**Avoid**

- The points can be listed: use PhotoSplit
- Body over 150 chars: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars
- standfirst ≤ 40 chars
- body 1–2 paragraphs, ≤ 150 chars in total
- pull ≤ 26 chars
- folio ≤ 16 chars

**Interaction**: build: the pull quote appears last

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `image` | `{ src, alt, credit?, focus? }` | yes | Image on the left half |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Column name |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Large title |
| `standfirst` | `string` |  | Lead paragraph |
| `body` | `string[]` | yes | Body paragraphs, the first one with a drop cap |
| `pull` | `string` |  | Pull quote in the right column, one build step |
| `folio` | `string` |  | Footer, for example "Feature · page 6" |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<MagazineSpread image={{ src: polyp, alt: '…' }} kicker="专题" title={['珊瑚为什么', '会变白']} standfirst="…" body={['…', '…']} pull="…" />
```

<a id="case-study"></a>

## 143 · Case study card `<CaseStudy />`

**Position**: Evidence · one case with three numbers; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `gbr`

A large rounded hero image on the left; on the right a title, tags and a short intro, with three metric cards at the bottom appearing one by one.

**Use when**

- Introduce one case, place or project with three key numbers
- Customer case with a real screenshot as the hero image

**Avoid**

- Only one number: use BigStat
- Several cases side by side: use WorkCards

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- tags 0–4, each ≤ 6 chars
- body ≤ 70 chars
- metrics exactly 3: value ≤ 7 characters, label ≤ 12 chars

**Interaction**: build: the metrics appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `image` | `{ src, alt, credit?, focus? }` | yes | Hero image |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `tags` | `string[]` |  | Tags |
| `body` | `string` | yes | Short intro |
| `metrics` | `{ value, label }[]` | yes | Three metrics, one build step each |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<CaseStudy image={{ src: aerial, alt: '…' }} kicker="案例" title="…" tags={['世界遗产']} body="…" metrics={[{ value: '34.8万', label: '平方公里面积' }, …]} />
```

<a id="device-frame"></a>

## 144 · Device frame `<DeviceFrame />`

**Position**: Evidence · a product or site screenshot; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `watch`

A real screenshot inside a drawn laptop, tablet or phone frame, with 0–3 hairline callouts on the right appearing one by one.

**Use when**

- Show the real interface of a site, app or tool
- Explain where the data comes from

**Avoid**

- A detail in the screenshot needs enlarging: use Screenshot or TabbedShot
- Only atmosphere images, no screenshot: use PhotoSplit

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 1 line × 16 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- points 0–3: title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 30 chars
- Screenshot ratio: laptop 16:10, tablet 4:3, phone 9:19.5
- image must be a real screenshot

**Interaction**: build: the callouts appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker / title / lead / source` | `SlideBase` |  | Base header fields: see the shared notes below |
| `image` | `{ src, alt, credit?, focus? }` | yes | Real screenshot; credit joins the source line |
| `device` | `'laptop' \| 'tablet' \| 'phone'` |  | Frame, default laptop |
| `points` | `{ title, body }[]` |  | Callouts, one build step each |

**Example**

```tsx
<DeviceFrame kicker="怎么知道的" title="卫星每天测一次海温" image={{ src: shot, alt: '…', credit: '截图：…' }} points={[{ title: '5 公里分辨率', body: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="compare-slider"></a>

## 145 · Before-and-after slider `<CompareSlider />`

**Position**: Comparison · before and after in the same view; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `compare`

Two identically framed images stacked, with a divider and handle between them. Each press of → moves the divider to the next stop and lights the matching note below.

**Use when**

- Same-position comparisons: redesign, restoration, seasons
- The audience should pull the curtain back to see the change

**Avoid**

- The two images are framed differently: use BeforeAfter or DuoCards
- Comparing numbers: use a chart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 1 line × 16 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- before.label / after.label ≤ 8 chars
- stops 1–3: at is 0–100, note ≤ 24 chars
- The two images must match in size and framing; generated images must say "illustrative" in note or credit

**Interaction**: build: the divider slides step by step and the notes light up in turn

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker / title / lead / source` | `SlideBase` |  | Base header fields: see the shared notes below |
| `before` | `{ image, label }` | yes | Left side, shown left of the divider |
| `after` | `{ image, label }` | yes | Right side |
| `start` | `number` |  | Divider position at step 0, default 50 |
| `stops` | `{ at, note }[]` | yes | Position and note per step; without steps it rests at the last position |

**Example**

```tsx
<CompareSlider kicker="对照" title="同一片礁，白化前后" before={{ image: a, label: '健康' }} after={{ image: b, label: '白化' }} stops={[{ at: 82, note: '…' }, { at: 18, note: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="photo-stack"></a>

## 146 · Photo stack `<PhotoStack />`

**Position**: List · 3–4 items revealed one at a time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p1` — `regions`

A pile of white-bordered photos on the right; each press of → drops a new photo at an angle onto the top, and the matching numbered text appears on the left.

**Use when**

- 3–4 parallel places, cases or findings, each with an image and one line
- A one-after-another rhythm

**Avoid**

- All images should be compared at once: use PhotoMosaic
- More than 4 items: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars
- photos 3–4: title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 32 chars

**Interaction**: build: each photo drops onto the top in turn

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `photos` | `{ image, title, body }[]` | yes | Photo and text appear in the same step |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<PhotoStack kicker="区域差异" title={['十年损失 14%，', '各地差得很远']} photos={[{ image: { src: a, alt: '…' }, title: '全球：-14%', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="flip-cards"></a>

## 147 · Flip cards `<FlipCards />`

**Position**: Reveal · question first, answer after; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `myths`

2–4 cards with a claim or question on the front; each press of → flips one card in 3D to show the answer on the back.

**Use when**

- Correct a misconception: the common claim on the front, the fact on the back
- Pose a question for the audience to guess, then flip to reveal

**Avoid**

- No question-then-answer suspense: use PanelCards
- More than 4 cards: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 2–4
- tag ≤ 6 chars; front ≤ 3 lines × 9 chars
- title ≤ 12 chars; body ≤ 48 chars
- backTag ≤ 6 chars
- The flip needs build: reveal

**Interaction**: build: reveal flips one card per step; without steps or in print every card shows its back

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `cards` | `{ tag, front, title, body }[]` | yes | Front tag and front text, back title and body |
| `backTag` | `string` |  | Small label on the back, default "answer" |

**Example**

```tsx
<FlipCards kicker="先翻三张牌" title="关于纸，常见的三个说法" backTag="其实" cards={[{ tag: '说法一', front: '纸是蔡伦从零发明的', title: '更早就已有纸', body: '…' }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="card-stack"></a>

## 148 · Card stack deal `<CardStack />`

**Position**: Sequence · 3–4 stages; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `machine`

The cards start as one pile on the right; each press of → deals the next card out of the pile into its place in the row.

**Use when**

- 3–4 stages or years that happen in order
- Each item should be drawn from the pile

**Avoid**

- The stages have no order: use PanelCards
- More than 4 items: use Timeline

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 3–4
- mark ≤ 6 characters
- title ≤ 10 chars; body ≤ 44 chars

**Interaction**: build: reveal starts as one pile at step 0, then deals one card per step

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `cards` | `{ mark, title, body }[]` | yes | Ordered by when they appear |

**Example**

```tsx
<CardStack kicker="一台机器" title="…" cards={[{ mark: '1799', title: '连续造纸机', body: '…' }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="fan-carousel"></a>

## 149 · Fan cards `<FanCarousel />`

**Position**: Parallel · 4–6 points taken one by one; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `a4`

The cards spread along an arc like a hand of cards; each press of → turns the fan, and the next card stands up in the centre.

**Use when**

- 4–6 parallel small facts or numbers
- The slide should feel like a prop rather than a table

**Avoid**

- All items should be compared at once: use StatRow or PanelCards
- Each item runs past 30 chars

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 4–6
- mark ≤ 6 characters; title ≤ 8 chars; body ≤ 30 chars
- icon uses an icon-set name

**Interaction**: build: reveal starts with the fan closed at step 0, then turns to the next card each step; the final state rests on the last card

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `cards` | `{ icon, mark, title, body }[]` | yes | The cards on the fan |

**Example**

```tsx
<FanCarousel kicker="尺寸" title="一张 A4 背后的五个数字" cards={[{ icon: 'letter', mark: '1786', title: '最早的设想', body: '…' }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="depth-carousel"></a>

## 150 · Depth carousel `<DepthCarousel />`

**Position**: Route · 3–6 stops in order; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `route`

The cards sit on a 3D track that recedes into the distance; each press of → brings the next stop to the front and pushes the rest back and dimmer. A row of small marks at the bottom shows the position.

**Use when**

- 3–6 ordered items such as route, stops or versions
- Each item has one large number (year, index)

**Avoid**

- Everything should be visible at once: use Timeline
- More than 6 items

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–6
- mark ≤ 6 characters; title ≤ 8 chars; body ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: build: reveal moves to the next stop each step; the final state rests on the last stop

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ mark, title, body }[]` | yes | In order |

**Example**

```tsx
<DepthCarousel kicker="西传" title="纸走过的五站" items={[{ mark: '105', title: '东汉洛阳', body: '…' }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="envelope"></a>

## 151 · Envelope `<Envelope />`

**Position**: Reveal · a letter or an announcement; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `stamp`

Title and lead on the left, a CSS-drawn envelope on the right: step 1 opens the flap, step 2 pulls the letter out. Optional paper texture and stamp.

**Use when**

- Quoting a letter, a notice or an announcement
- You want the reveal feel of opening something up

**Avoid**

- The letter text is over 72 chars: use QuoteHero
- The content is invented and unmarked: mark it illustrative in sign or source

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 3 lines × 8 chars; lead ≤ 60 chars
- greeting ≤ 12 chars; body ≤ 72 chars; sign ≤ 14 chars
- stamp ≤ 4 characters
- texture only takes a decor- paper texture

**Interaction**: build: on reveal, step 1 opens the flap and step 2 pulls out the letter

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `greeting` | `string` | yes | Salutation of the letter |
| `body` | `string` | yes | Body of the letter |
| `sign` | `string` | yes | Signature |
| `stamp` | `string` |  | Text on the stamp |
| `texture` | `string` |  | Imported decor- paper texture |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<Envelope kicker="一封信" title={['一枚邮票，', '让信变便宜']} greeting="致每一位寄信人：" body="…" sign="示意信件" stamp="1d" texture={paper} source="…" />
```

<a id="flip-book"></a>

## 152 · Flip book `<FlipBook />`

**Position**: Narrative · 2–4 spreads; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `bible`

An open book; each page holds one big number, a title and a note. Each → press turns the right page around the spine in 3D to show the next spread.

**Use when**

- Telling a book, an archive or a piece of history
- 4–8 numbers told two at a time

**Avoid**

- Only 1 spread: use DuoCards
- Numbers must be compared: use a chart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- spreads 2–4 spreads
- label ≤ 8 chars; value ≤ 7 characters
- title ≤ 12 chars; body ≤ 56 chars
- texture only takes a decor- paper texture

**Interaction**: build: each step turns one page; the final state shows the last spread

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `spreads` | `{ left, right }[]` | yes | Each page { label?, value?, title, body? } |
| `texture` | `string` |  | Imported decor- paper texture |

**Example**

```tsx
<FlipBook kicker="一本书" title="翻开古腾堡圣经" texture={paper} spreads={[{ left: { value: '1455', title: '…' }, right: { value: '42', title: '每页 42 行' } }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="lanyard"></a>

## 153 · Lanyard badge `<Lanyard />`

**Position**: People · introduces the speaker or main character; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `lanyard`

A badge on a fabric strap on the right, swinging gently on entry then settling; the badge shows the event name, letter monogram, name and role. 2–4 points on the left appear one by one.

**Use when**

- Introducing the speaker
- Introducing a historical figure or story protagonist (no real photo)

**Avoid**

- Introducing 2 or more people: use Team
- A real photo is needed: this template only uses a letter monogram

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- badge.event ≤ 12 chars; initials 1–2 characters; name ≤ 6 chars; role ≤ 14 chars; code ≤ 10 characters
- points 2–4: label ≤ 6 chars, text ≤ 36 chars
- strap ≤ 16 characters

**Interaction**: Swings on entry (no swing with reduced motion); build: points appear one by one on reveal

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `badge` | `{ event, initials, name, role, code? }` | yes | Badge content |
| `points` | `{ label, text }[]` | yes | Left points, one build step each |
| `strap` | `string` |  | Text on the strap |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<Lanyard kicker="今天的向导" title="…" badge={{ event: '纸的旅程 · 人物卡', initials: '蔡', name: '蔡伦', role: '东汉 · 尚方令' }} points={[{ label: '身份', text: '…' }, …]} strap="PAPER ROUTE" source="…" />
```

<a id="progress-cards"></a>

## 154 · Progress cards `<ProgressCards />`

**Position**: Data · 2–4 percentages; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `recycle`

Each card has a ring, a big percentage, a label, a bar and one note; each → press fills one card's ring and bar to its value.

**Use when**

- 2–4 ratios from 0–100: completion, recovery rate, share
- Each number needs one line of explanation

**Avoid**

- Values are not percentages or go over 100: use StatRow
- Many items must be compared: use BarChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 2–4
- label ≤ 10 chars; display ≤ 6 characters; note ≤ 30 chars
- value 0–100

**Interaction**: build: each step fills one card

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `cards` | `{ label, value, display?, note }[]` | yes | value sets the ring and the bar |

**Example**

```tsx
<ProgressCards kicker="回收" title="美国的纸，回收了多少" cards={[{ label: '纸和纸板总体', value: 68.2, note: '…' }, …]} source="US EPA（2018）" />
```

<a id="process-accordion"></a>

## 155 · Process accordion `<ProcessAccordion />`

**Position**: Process · 4–6 steps opened one at a time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `craft`
