A row of vertical panels; collapsed panels show only the number and a vertical title. Each → press expands the next step into a wide panel (accent background) with its note and folds the previous one back.

**Use when**

- A 4–6 step craft or process, one note per step
- You want the other steps folded away while you talk about one

**Avoid**

- All notes must be visible at once: use StepTrack
- Step names over 6 chars (vertical text gets too tall)

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps 4–6
- title ≤ 6 chars; sub ≤ 16 chars; body ≤ 60 chars
- open is the panel expanded when not stepped, default the last one

**Interaction**: build: each step expands one item

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `steps` | `{ title, sub?, body }[]` | yes | Process steps |
| `open` | `number` |  | Index of the panel open in the final state |

**Example**

```tsx
<ProcessAccordion kicker="古法" title="竹纸的五道工序" steps={[{ title: '斩竹漂塘', sub: '取料', body: '…' }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="notebook-cover"></a>

## 156 · Notebook section `<NotebookCover />`

**Position**: Chapter · divider page; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p3` — `ch1`

A slightly tilted hardcover notebook on the right (accent cover, stitching, elastic band and paper label) with the chapter name on the label; on the left a very large chapter number and 2–4 items for this chapter.

**Use when**

- A chapter divider page
- You want the divider to feel handmade and note-like

**Avoid**

- A formal report: use SectionBreak
- A short deck with no chapter structure

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- number ≤ 3 characters
- title ≤ 2 lines × 7 chars; sub ≤ 16 chars
- items 0–4, each ≤ 14 chars
- texture only takes a decor- leather texture

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `number` | `string` | yes | Chapter number |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Chapter name on the tab |
| `sub` | `string` |  | Subtitle on the label |
| `items` | `string[]` |  | Items in this chapter |
| `texture` | `string` |  | Imported decor- leather texture |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<NotebookCover number="01" kicker="第一章" title={['纸从哪里', '出发']} sub="…" items={['…', '…']} texture={leather} />
```

<a id="halftone"></a>

## 157 · Halftone image `<Halftone />`

**Position**: Atmosphere · photo turned into dots or dither; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p5` — `what`

Title, lead and 0–3 small numbers on the left; on the right one image reprinted by an SVG filter as newspaper dots in the theme color (dots) or 1-bit dither (dither); the image itself needs no extra work.

**Use when**

- You want image texture without the photo taking the attention
- The decorative image should match the theme color

**Avoid**

- The image is evidence and detail must show: use Screenshot
- The image is dark or flat: the dots blur into one mass

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- kicker 2–8 chars
- title 1–3 lines × ≤ 8 chars
- lead ≤ 60 chars
- facts 0–3: value ≤ 6 characters, label ≤ 12 chars
- cell 8–24, default 14
- with facts, add build: reveal to show them one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title; break lines by hand in the array |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `image` | `string` | yes | Imported decor- image or screenshot |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Image description |
| `mode` | `'dots' \| 'dither'` |  | dots or dither, default dots |
| `cell` | `number` |  | Dot grid size |
| `facts` | `{ value, label }[]` |  | Small numbers |
| `credit` | `string` |  | Image credit, e.g. “Image: AI-generated decoration” |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<Halftone kicker="什么是白化" title={['颜色来自藻，', '白色是骨骼']} lead="…" image={bleached} alt="…" mode="dots" facts={[{ value: '共生藻', label: '珊瑚颜色的来源' }]} credit="图：AI 生成装饰图" />
```

<a id="progressive-blur"></a>

## 158 · Progressive blur image `<ProgressiveBlur />`

**Position**: Atmosphere · full-page image with a title; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p5` — `reef`

A full-page image; the lower half is blurred and darkened in 6 growing layers; title and lead sit on the blurred area, with 0–3 tags and the source at the bottom right. Use tone: dark.

**Use when**

- A chapter opening or a scene lead-in
- The upper half of the image holds the subject

**Avoid**

- The subject sits in the lower half: it gets blurred out
- Several points must be made: use a content template

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title 1–2 lines × ≤ 14 chars
- lead ≤ 56 chars
- tags 0–3, each ≤ 8 chars
- use tone: dark and set backdrop to none
- blur is simplified in PDF

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead, shown as one build step |
| `tags` | `string[]` |  | Bottom-right tags |
| `image` | `string` | yes | Imported image |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Image description |
| `credit` | `string` |  | Image credit |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<ProgressiveBlur kicker="先认识珊瑚礁" title={['不到 1% 的海底，', '养着约四分之一的海洋生物']} lead="…" tags={['动物', '共生藻']} image={coral} alt="…" credit="图：AI 生成装饰图" />
```

<a id="mask-reveal"></a>

## 159 · Mask reveal `<MaskReveal />`

**Position**: Journey · uncover the image as you talk; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p5` — `stages`

2–4 numbered points on the left; on the right a greyed image; each step opens the mask a little more (a circle growing from the center, or blinds opening group by group) and the image shows its original colors.

**Use when**

- Telling a process in stages and showing the whole at the end
- One atmosphere image should appear gradually with the talk

**Avoid**

- More than 4 points: split the slide or use StepTrack
- The image itself is evidence: use Screenshot

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title 1–2 lines × ≤ 8 chars
- steps 2–4: label ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 36 chars
- shape: circle (default) or blinds
- use build: reveal

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `steps` | `{ label, text }[]` | yes | Points, one step each |
| `image` | `string` | yes | Imported image |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Image description |
| `shape` | `'circle' \| 'blinds'` |  | Mask shape |
| `credit` | `string` |  | Image credit |
| `source` | `string` |  | Fact source |

**Example**

```tsx
<MaskReveal kicker="三个阶段" title={['从受热，', '到恢复或死去']} steps={[{ label: '热应激', text: '…' }, …]} image={coral} alt="…" shape="circle" />
```

<a id="logo-wall"></a>

## 160 · Logo wall `<LogoWall />`

**Position**: Trust · partners or participants; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p5` — `network`

Below the title, a thin-line grid wall; each cell holds a placeholder geometric mark (12 built in, no real brands) with a name and role; each row appears as one step, and a line below states that the marks are illustrative.

**Use when**

- Listing participant types or a partner network
- You need the visual of many parties involved

**Avoid**

- Real brand marks must be shown: not supported
- Every party needs a note: use IconGrid

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 6–12: name ≤ 8 chars, role ≤ 8 chars; ≤ 6 items gives 3 per row, otherwise 4
- note ≤ 30 chars and must say the marks are illustrative
- title ≤ 16 chars, lead ≤ 60 chars
- use build: reveal to show it row by row

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `items` | `{ name, role? }[]` | yes | Parties |
| `note` | `string` |  | Illustrative note |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source, or “illustration” |

**Example**

```tsx
<LogoWall kicker="谁在监测" title="一张多方合作的观测网" items={[{ name: '卫星海温', role: '大范围预警' }, …]} note="标识为占位图形，均为示意。" />
```

<a id="photo-closing"></a>

## 161 · Photo closing `<PhotoClosing />`

**Position**: Ending · full-page image closing; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p5` — `end`

A full-page image pushes in slowly; a black gradient darkens the left; one large closing line, a lead and 0–3 numbered next steps. Use tone: dark.

**Use when**

- The last slide of a talk, leaving one image
- There is a clear next step

**Avoid**

- No suitable image: use Closing
- Points must be recapped: use Recap

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title 1–2 lines × ≤ 10 chars
- lead ≤ 44 chars
- actions 0–3, each ≤ 20 chars
- the subject sits on the right; use tone: dark and set backdrop to none

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Closing line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `actions` | `string[]` |  | Next steps, appearing one by one |
| `image` | `string` | yes | Imported image |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Image description |
| `credit` | `string` |  | Image credit |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<PhotoClosing kicker="最后" title={['白色还不是', '终点']} lead="…" actions={['…', '…']} image={dusk} alt="…" credit="图：AI 生成装饰图" />
```

<a id="ranking-race"></a>

## 162 · Ranking race `<RankingRace />`

**Position**: Data · ranks changing over time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `race`

Horizontal bar ranking; each → press moves to the next period, the bars re-sort and change length with their values, and the current year shows at the bottom right.

**Use when**

- The same set of objects ranked at 3–8 points in time
- You want the audience to see “who passed whom” step by step

**Avoid**

- Only two points in time: use SlopeRank
- Exact trends matter: use LineChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- periods 3–8, each ≤ 6 characters
- items 5–12, name ≤ 8 chars; show 5–8 rows
- notes one per period, ≤ 28 chars
- needs build: 'reveal'; without steps it shows the last period

**Interaction**: → advances one period

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `periods` | `string[]` | yes | Time points |
| `items` | `{ name, values: (number \| null)[] }[]` | yes | One value per item per period |
| `show` | `number` |  | Rows to show, default 6 |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Value unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `focus` | `string` |  | Item in the accent color |
| `notes` | `string[]` |  | One reading per period |

**Example**

```tsx
<RankingRace kicker="排名" title="谁发的电最多" periods={['2015', '2020', '2025']} items={[{ name: '中国', values: [39, 261, 1175] }, …]} focus="中国" />
```

<a id="bubble-cluster"></a>

## 163 · Bubble cluster `<BubbleCluster />`

**Position**: Data · compare size; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `bubble`

A tight cluster of circles whose area is proportional to the value; large circles show the name and value; 1–3 readings can sit on the right.

**Use when**

- 5–14 objects compared by size, far apart but with no axes
- You want to stress where one item sits in the whole

**Avoid**

- Exact values must be compared: use BarChart
- Two dimensions: use Scatter

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 5–14, name ≤ 6 chars; smallest value ≥ 1/16 of the largest, or the small circles cannot hold the name
- unit ≤ 4 characters
- notes 1–3: key ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 30 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ name, value }[]` | yes | Objects and values |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `focus` | `string` |  | Item in the accent color, default the largest |
| `notes` | `{ key, text }[]` |  | Readings on the right, opened one by one |

**Example**

```tsx
<BubbleCluster kicker="体量" title="各类电源有多大" items={[{ name: '煤电', value: 10472 }, …]} unit="TWh" focus="太阳能" />
```

<a id="node-graph"></a>

## 164 · Node graph `<NodeGraph />`

**Position**: Structure · what connects to what; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `graph`

One center node and the nodes around it, joined by lines that can carry a relation; when stepped, each node appears with its lines.

**Use when**

- Relations between 4–10 objects: dependency, flow, cooperation
- Explaining what a system is made of and how the parts affect each other

**Avoid**

- There is a sequence of steps: use FlowDiagram
- Quantities flow: use Sankey

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- nodes 4–10: label ≤ 6 chars, sub ≤ 10 chars
- edges ≤ 14, label ≤ 6 chars
- note ≤ 60 chars
- the first node is centered by default; when every node has x/y (0–100) the manual positions are used

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `nodes` | `{ id, label, sub?, x?, y? }[]` | yes | Nodes |
| `edges` | `{ from, to, label?, strong? }[]` | yes | Lines; strong uses the accent color |
| `note` | `string` |  | Reading on the right |

**Example**

```tsx
<NodeGraph kicker="系统" title="…" nodes={[{ id: 'pv', label: '光伏发电' }, { id: 'store', label: '储能' }, …]} edges={[{ from: 'pv', to: 'store', label: '充电' }]} />
```

<a id="sankey"></a>

## 165 · Sankey diagram `<Sankey />`

**Position**: Data · how a total splits; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `sankey`

2–4 columns of nodes joined by flow bands whose width is proportional to the value; when stepped, the flow runs column by column.

**Use when**

- What a total is made of and where it goes
- Where a budget, energy or traffic goes

**Avoid**

- Only one layer of parts: use Donut or Treemap
- Relations without quantities: use NodeGraph

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- nodes 4–14, label ≤ 6 chars; ≤ 8 per column
- columns 2–4, derived from the links
- value label with unit ≤ 10 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `nodes` | `{ id, label }[]` | yes | Nodes |
| `links` | `{ from, to, value }[]` | yes | Flows |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit, may start with a space |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `focus` | `string` |  | Emphasize the flows of this node |

**Example**

```tsx
<Sankey kicker="流向" title="…" nodes={[{ id: 'coal', label: '煤电' }, …]} links={[{ from: 'coal', to: 'fossil', value: 10472 }, …]} focus="solar" />
```

<a id="treemap"></a>

## 166 · Treemap `<Treemap />`

**Position**: Data · composition of a whole; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `treemap`

One rectangle is cut into blocks by value; a bigger block means a bigger value, and large blocks show name, value and share.

**Use when**

- 6–12 parts making up a whole
- Shares differ a lot and the largest should stand out at a glance

**Avoid**

- Only 2–5 parts: use Donut
- Change over time must be compared: use StackedArea

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 4–12, name ≤ 6 chars
- blocks too small carry no text: the smallest item needs ≥ 1.5% of the total to get a label
- unit ≤ 4 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ name, value }[]` | yes | Parts |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `focus` | `string` |  | Item in the accent color |
| `shares` | `boolean` |  | Large blocks show their share, default true |

**Example**

```tsx
<Treemap kicker="分布" title="…" items={[{ name: '中国', value: 1175 }, …]} focus="中国" />
```

<a id="radar"></a>

## 167 · Radar chart `<Radar />`

**Position**: Data · compare profiles across dimensions; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `radar`

A web chart with 5–8 axes; the first series is filled with the accent color, the second is a grey dashed line and appears after the first when stepped.

**Use when**

- Profiles of two objects over 5–8 dimensions
- All dimensions share a unit (for example percent or a score)

**Avoid**

- Dimensions have different units: use CompareTable
- More than two objects: use grouped BarChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- axes 5–8, each ≤ 6 chars
- series 1–2, name ≤ 8 chars
- notes 1–3: key ≤ 8 chars, text ≤ 30 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `axes` | `string[]` | yes | Dimensions |
| `series` | `{ name, values }[]` | yes | 1–2 series |
| `max` | `number` |  | Value of the outer ring |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `notes` | `{ key, text }[]` |  | Reading on the right |

**Example**

```tsx
<Radar kicker="对比" title="…" axes={['煤电', '油气', '核电', '水电', '风电', '太阳能']} series={[{ name: '中国', values: […] }, { name: '德国', values: […] }]} max={60} unit="%" />
```

<a id="scatter"></a>

## 168 · Scatter quadrants `<Scatter />`

**Position**: Data · distribution on two metrics; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `scatter`

A scatter plot split into four quadrants by two dashed lines; the quadrant names appear one by one; a few points carry names and key points use the accent color.

**Use when**

- Where 6–60 objects sit on two metrics
- You want to group them by “high/low × high/low”

**Avoid**

- Fewer than 6 points: use Quadrant with cards
- Change over time matters: use LineChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- points 6–60, named points ≤ 8, name ≤ 6 chars
- xLabel / yLabel ≤ 14 chars
- quadrants 4, each ≤ 8 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `points` | `{ name, x, y, label?, focus? }[]` | yes | Points |
| `xLabel` | `string` | yes | X axis name and unit |
| `yLabel` | `string` | yes | Y axis name and unit |
| `split` | `{ x, y }` |  | Quadrant split values |
| `quadrants` | `[top-left, top-right, bottom-left, bottom-right]` |  | Quadrant names |
| `xDecimals` | `number` |  | X axis decimal places |
| `yDecimals` | `number` |  | Y axis decimal places |

**Example**

```tsx
<Scatter kicker="追赶" title="…" points={[{ name: '中国', x: 0.7, y: 11.1, label: true }, …]} xLabel="2015 年占比（%）" yLabel="2025 年占比（%）" split={{ x: 1.1, y: 8.7 }} quadrants={['后来居上', '一直领先', '仍在起步', '被平均追上']} />
```

<a id="stacked-area"></a>

## 169 · Stacked area chart `<StackedArea />`

**Position**: Data · composition over time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `stack`

2–6 layers stack into the total (or 100%); the right end labels each layer with its name and latest value; when stepped, layers add from the bottom up.

**Use when**

- How the composition of a total changes over time
- One layer is getting thicker or thinner

**Avoid**

- Only one or two trends: use LineChart
- Only one point in time: use Treemap or Donut

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- x 4–30 points, labels ≤ 6 characters; above 12, use ticks to label only some
- series 2–6 layers, name ≤ 6 chars
- unit ≤ 10 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `x` | `string[]` | yes | Time points |
| `series` | `{ name, values }[]` | yes | Bottom to top |
| `mode` | `'value' \| 'share'` |  | share stacks to 100% |
| `ticks` | `number[]` |  | X indices to label |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit note |
| `focus` | `string` |  | Layer in the accent color |

**Example**

```tsx
<StackedArea kicker="结构" title="…" x={years} series={[{ name: '煤电', values: […] }, …]} mode="share" focus="太阳能" />
```

<a id="dot-map"></a>

## 170 · Dot map `<DotMap />`

**Position**: Data · where things are; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `map`

An offline dot-matrix world map (Natural Earth land, public domain) with numbered dots placed by longitude and latitude and sized by value, plus a matching list on the right.

**Use when**

- Location and value of 1–8 countries or cities
- You want the audience to see where things are spread first

**Avoid**

- Comparison by colored region: use Heatmap or BarChart
- Places sit inside one city: the dot grid is too coarse

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- markers 1–8, name ≤ 6 chars
- listTitle ≤ 10 chars
- value with unit ≤ 10 characters
- the map is 2° precise, so country-level locations only

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `markers` | `{ name, lon, lat, value? }[]` | yes | Places |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `listTitle` | `string` |  | List title |

**Example**

```tsx
<DotMap kicker="地图" title="…" markers={[{ name: '中国', lon: 104, lat: 35, value: 1175 }, …]} unit=" TWh" />
```

<a id="glow-metrics"></a>

## 171 · Glow metric cards `<GlowMetrics />`

**Position**: Data · key numbers in the opening; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p4` — `glow`

2–4 dark cards; the big number carries an accent glow and counts up from 0, with an optional small line chart and change note; made for dark styles.

**Use when**

- The 2–4 numbers the whole deck rests on
- A data opening in a dark style (studio-dark and others)

**Avoid**

- More than 4 numbers: use KpiBoard
- A light formal report: use StatRow

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- metrics 2–4
- value with unit ≤ 8 characters; unit ≤ 4 characters
- label ≤ 10 chars, delta ≤ 12 characters, note ≤ 24 chars
- trend 4–16 numbers

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `metrics` | `{ value, decimals?, unit?, label, note?, delta?, trend? }[]` | yes | Metrics, one step per card |

**Example**

```tsx
<GlowMetrics kicker="2025 年" title="先记住三个数" metrics={[{ value: 2779, unit: 'TWh', label: '全球太阳能发电量', delta: '+30%' }, …]} />
```

<a id="typewriter"></a>

## 172 · Typewriter `<Typewriter />`

**Position**: Quotation · one quote typed out letter by letter; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `landed`

Monospace large text types out 1–3 lines of a quote letter by letter with a blinking cursor; one line below holds the translation or source, and 0–3 label and value pairs sit at the bottom. Each line is one build step.

**Use when**

- A quote from a telegram, radio or command line
- A short line the audience should read one character at a time

**Avoid**

- A long quotation: use Quote
- The sentence does not feel like it is being typed: use Statement

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- lines 1–3, each ≤ 32 Latin characters or ≤ 16 chars
- caption ≤ 40 chars
- meta 0–3: label ≤ 6 chars, value ≤ 12 chars

**Interaction**: in build mode each → press types one line; entering the slide directly starts typing and ends on the full text

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `lines` | `string[]` | yes | Lines typed letter by letter |
| `caption` | `string` |  | Translation or note |
| `meta` | `{ label, value }[]` |  | Bottom information |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<Typewriter kicker="第一句话" lines={['Houston, Tranquility Base here.', 'The Eagle has landed.']} caption="…" meta={[{ label: '着陆地点', value: '静海' }]} />
```

<a id="rotating-word"></a>

## 173 · Rotating word `<RotatingWord />`

**Position**: Point of view · swap 2–4 words in one sentence; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `stack`

The headline leaves one word slot; each step swaps in the next word and lights the matching card below; a card can carry one number and one note.

**Use when**

- Parallel answers like “it comes down to X / Y / Z”
- One sentence frame takes 2–4 objects in turn

**Avoid**

- The words share no sentence frame: use IconGrid or cards
- Values must be compared: use a chart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- before ≤ 8 chars, after ≤ 6 chars
- words 2–4: word ≤ 4 chars, value ≤ 8 characters, note ≤ 30 chars

**Interaction**: in build mode each → press moves to the next word and lights its card; the final state shows the last word

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `before` | `string` | yes | Text before the word slot |
| `words` | `{ word, note, value? }[]` | yes | Rotating words and cards |
| `after` | `string` |  | Text after the word slot |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<RotatingWord kicker="三样东西" before="登月靠的是" words={[{ word: '火箭', value: '110.6 m', note: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="morph-headline"></a>

## 174 · Morph headline `<MorphHeadline />`

**Position**: Turn · one line becomes another; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `step`

Two very large lines in the same place: the first blurs apart letter by letter, the second condenses in place; two columns at the bottom note the before and after lines.

**Use when**

- A famous line or slogan shown before and after
- A stance turning “from A to B”

**Avoid**

- Both lines must be read at once: use a Compare template
- The sentence is over 12 chars

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- from and to ≤ 12 chars each
- notes exactly 2: label ≤ 6 chars, body ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: in build mode → morphs; entering the slide directly morphs automatically after about 1.6 s, and print shows only the second line

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `from` | `string` | yes | First line |
| `to` | `string` | yes | Second line it becomes |
| `notes` | `[{ label, body }, { label, body }]` |  | Notes for the before and after lines |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<MorphHeadline kicker="那句话" from="一个人的一小步" to="人类的一大步" notes={[{ label: '前半句', body: '…' }, { label: '后半句', body: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="text-arc"></a>

## 175 · Circular text `<TextArc />`

**Position**: Atmosphere · one line bent into a ring; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `desolation`

Title, lead and 0–3 points on the left; on the right one line set along a ring (real SVG text) that rotates into place; the center holds one large word or number and can sit on a decorative image.

**Use when**

- A phrase or slogan worth repeating
- An editorial section break or impression slide

**Avoid**

- The ring text must be read closely: put it in the body
- No word or number fits the center

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars; lead ≤ 60 chars; points 0–3, each ≤ 24 chars
- ring 16–44 units (a Latin character counts 1, a Chinese character counts 2), padded to a full circle with repeated “ · ”; Latin uppercase works best, Chinese characters turn sideways along the arc
- center ≤ 5 characters, centerLabel ≤ 8 chars

**Interaction**: the ring is one build step; it rotates into place on appear, then holds still

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `points` | `string[]` |  | Points |
| `ring` | `string` | yes | Text on the ring |
| `center` | `string` | yes | Large word at the center |
| `centerLabel` | `string` |  | Center label |
| `image` | `string (import)` |  | Decorative image inside the ring, decor- or a screenshot |
| `alt` | `string` |  | Image description |
| `credit` | `string` |  | Image credit; AI-generated images must be marked |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<TextArc kicker="月面印象" title="壮丽的荒凉" ring="MAGNIFICENT DESOLATION · MAGNIFICENT DESOLATION · " center="静海" centerLabel="着陆地点" image={disc} alt="…" />
```

<a id="odometer"></a>

## 176 · Rolling digits `<Odometer />`

**Position**: Data · 1–3 big numbers; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `numbers`

1–3 big numbers, each digit rolling to its final value like an odometer; each number has a label and one line of note below. The DOM always holds the real final text; the rolling digits are decoration layered on top.

**Use when**

- A few big numbers that need to be counted out
- Magnitude numbers such as distance, headcount or duration

**Avoid**

- Numbers must be compared in size: use BarChart
- More than 3 numbers: use StatRow

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 18 chars
- stats 1–3: value ≤ 7 characters, unit ≤ 4 characters, label ≤ 12 chars, note ≤ 28 chars

**Interaction**: Each number is a build step; it rolls to its final value when it appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `stats` | `{ value, unit?, label, note? }[]` | yes | numbers |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<Odometer kicker="三个数字" title="远、久、看的人多" stats={[{ value: '384,400', unit: 'km', label: '地月平均距离' }, …]} />
```

<a id="marker-sweep"></a>

## 177 · Highlighter `<MarkerSweep />`

**Position**: Interpretation · mark key points in a passage; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `eva`

One large-type passage; a highlighter sweeps over 2–4 key phrases in turn, each numbered, with a matching margin note at the bottom.

**Use when**

- 2–4 points to make inside one passage
- Walk through a quoted passage or explanation sentence by sentence

**Avoid**

- Body longer than 110 chars: split the slide
- The points form a parallel list: use a list template

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 16 chars
- body 50–110 chars
- marks 2–4, must be text from body; note ≤ 22 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode each press → highlight the next spot and show its margin note

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `body` | `string` | yes | body text |
| `marks` | `{ text, note? }[]` | yes | Highlighted phrases and margin notes |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<MarkerSweep kicker="分工" title="两人在月面，一人在轨道" body="…" marks={[{ text: '21 小时 36 分', note: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="text-mask"></a>

## 178 · Cut-out word `<TextMask />`

**Position**: Opening · cover or section break; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `cover`

One huge word acts as a cut-out; the image shows only through the letterforms and slowly settles; below are a title, one subtitle and 0–3 numbers.

**Use when**

- Cover or section break where the topic fits one short word
- There is an image with strong texture

**Avoid**

- The image is evidence or must be read closely: use an image layout
- The word is longer than 5 Latin letters

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- word 2–5 Latin capitals or 1–2 Chinese characters
- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars; sub ≤ 36 chars
- facts 0–3: value ≤ 8 characters, label ≤ 10 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `word` | `string` | yes | Cut-out word |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `sub` | `string` |  | Subtitle |
| `facts` | `{ value, label }[]` |  | Numbers at the bottom |
| `image` | `string (import)` | yes | Image showing through, decor- or screenshot |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Image description |
| `credit` | `string` |  | Image credit; state it for AI-generated images |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<TextMask kicker="阿波罗 11 号 · 1969" word="MOON" title={['八天往返，', '人第一次站上月球']} image={surface} alt="…" />
```

<a id="split-flap"></a>

## 179 · Split-flap board `<SplitFlap />`

**Position**: Timeline · 3–6 moments; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `board`

An airport split-flap board: each row has a date, an optional time, an event and a status, and the flaps turn character by character to their final value; each row is a build step.

**Use when**

- A schedule or task timeline with exact times
- Point-in-time events such as departure, release or go-live

**Avoid**

- Events need long descriptions: use Timeline
- More than 6 rows: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 16 chars
- rows 3–6: time and at are each exactly 5 characters, label ≤ 8 chars or ≤ 14 Latin characters, status ≤ 4 chars
- heads each ≤ 6 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode each press → flip in one row

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `heads` | `string[]` |  | Column names |
| `rows` | `{ time, at?, label, status }[]` | yes | Rows |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<SplitFlap kicker="时刻表" title="…" heads={['日期', '时间 UTC', '事件', '状态']} rows={[{ time: '07-16', at: '13:32', label: '土星五号发射', status: '起飞' }, …]} />
```

<a id="news-ticker"></a>

## 180 · News ticker `<NewsTicker />`

**Position**: Event · front-page headline; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `home`

A front-page headline, dateline and lead; a news ticker at the bottom drives in from the right and stops (no loop), showing 2–4 items.

**Use when**

- Announce one event or result
- One main message plus a few secondary ones

**Avoid**

- Items need separate sources and close reading: use a list
- There is no single main headline

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- dateline ≤ 16 characters
- headline ≤ 2 lines × 14 chars; dek ≤ 56 chars
- label ≤ 4 chars; items 2–4, each ≤ 18 chars, total ≤ 50 chars

**Interaction**: The ticker is a build step; it drives in and stops

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `dateline` | `string` |  | Dateline |
| `headline` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Headline |
| `dek` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `label` | `string` | yes | Ticker label |
| `items` | `string[]` | yes | News items |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<NewsTicker kicker="返回" dateline="1969-07-24" headline={['哥伦布号溅落，', '三人平安返回']} label="快讯" items={['…', '…']} />
```

<a id="karaoke-lines"></a>

## 181 · Script highlighter `<KaraokeLines />`

**Position**: Quotation · read a script line by line; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v2-p2` — `speech`

3–6 script lines; unread lines are light grey, each step lights the next line (accent colour and a sweeping underline), and read lines return to body colour; the right column holds the original text and the source.

**Use when**

- A speech, oath or lyric-like passage
- You want the audience to read line by line with the rhythm

**Avoid**

- Only one sentence: use Quote or Statement
- Any line longer than 14 chars

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- lines 3–6, each ≤ 14 chars
- aside ≤ 120 Latin characters or ≤ 60 chars
- by ≤ 24 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode each press → light the next line; in the complete state the last line stays in the accent colour

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label, 2–8 chars |
| `lines` | `string[]` | yes | Script lines |
| `aside` | `string` |  | Original text or comment |
| `by` | `string` |  | Speaker, occasion |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<KaraokeLines kicker="我们选择登月" lines={['我们选择在这十年里登月，', …]} by="肯尼迪 · 1962" />
```

<a id="paradigm-cards"></a>

## 182 · Model comparison cards `<ParadigmCards />`

**Position**: Comparison · structure of 2–3 approaches; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `models`

2–3 cards side by side, each with a small structure sketch at the top (directory tree / blocks / flat) and aligned attribute rows below; each press → one card appears, and one card can be emphasised.

**Use when**

- Compare 2–3 models for organising data or doing the work
- Every model is described with the same few dimensions

**Avoid**

- More than 4 dimensions or scores are needed: use CompareTable
- The structure sketch is unrelated to the content: use PanelCards

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 2–3
- name ≤ 8 chars; tagline ≤ 16 chars
- rows 2–4: label ≤ 4 chars, value ≤ 16 chars; the label order is the same on every card
- shape is only tree / blocks / flat

**Interaction**: build: reveal — each step shows one card

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `cards` | `{ shape, name, tagline, rows: { label, value }[] }[]` | yes | One card per model |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Index of the emphasised card |

**Example**

```tsx
<ParadigmCards kicker="先分清三种" title="块、文件、对象" focus={2} cards={[{ shape: 'blocks', name: '块存储', tagline: '…', rows: [{ label: '组织', value: '…' }] }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="anatomy-card"></a>

## 183 · Anatomy card `<AnatomyCard />`

**Position**: Structure · what one thing is made of; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `anatomy`

A large card on the left is cut into 2–4 strips (example values in monospace); matching notes sit on the right; each press → light one strip and its note.

**Use when**

- Break down the parts of an object, request, message or record
- Every part has a concrete example value

**Avoid**

- Parts depend on each other top-down: use LayerStack or ArchLayers
- No example values: use NumberedColumns

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- label ≤ 24 Latin characters
- parts 2–4
- name ≤ 8 chars; sample ≤ 3 lines × 36 Latin characters; note ≤ 40 chars
- weight 1–3

**Interaction**: build: reveal — each step lights one strip; in the complete state all are lit

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `label` | `string` | yes | Label in the card's top-left corner |
| `parts` | `{ name, sample: string[], note, weight? }[]` | yes | Top to bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<AnatomyCard kicker="拆开一个对象" title="…" label="my-bucket" parts={[{ name: '键', sample: ['photos/puppy.jpg'], note: '…' }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="explorer-flip"></a>

## 184 · Browser flip `<ExplorerFlip />`

**Position**: Reveal · how it looks vs what it is; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `flat`

A file browser window first shows the familiar folder view; one press → every row flips to its full key or real path, and a note appears on the right.

**Use when**

- Explain a data structure that looks like A but is B (prefixes, aliases, mappings)
- There are 3–6 concrete entries

**Avoid**

- Only showing a file list: use Screenshot or Terminal
- More than 6 entries

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- path ≤ 32 Latin characters
- rows 3–6: name ≤ 24 Latin characters (a trailing / draws a folder), key ≤ 40 Latin characters, size ≤ 10 characters
- views each ≤ 6 chars
- note ≤ 60 chars

**Interaction**: build: reveal — step 1 flips to the keys, step 2 shows the note; the complete state shows the keys

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `path` | `string` | yes | Window address bar |
| `rows` | `{ name, key, size? }[]` | yes | Rows |
| `views` | `[string, string]` |  | Two view labels |
| `note` | `string` |  | Note shown after the flip |

**Example**

```tsx
<ExplorerFlip kicker="扁平命名空间" title="…" path="s3://admin-created" rows={[{ name: 'Finance/', key: 'Finance/statement1.pdf', size: '1 个对象' }, …]} note="…" />
```

<a id="verb-cards"></a>

## 185 · Request cards `<VerbCards />`

**Position**: Interface · 3–4 operations; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `api`

3–4 cards, each with a method badge (PUT/GET…), a request line and one line of note; each press → one card prints its response line at the bottom like a receipt.

**Use when**

- Introduce a few core operations of one API
- Every operation has a request → response pair

**Avoid**

- Full code must be shown: use Terminal or CodeDiff
- More than 4 operations: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 3–4
- verb ≤ 6 Latin characters; path ≤ 28 Latin characters
- body ≤ 30 chars; reply ≤ 28 Latin characters

**Interaction**: build: reveal — each step prints one card's response

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `cards` | `{ verb, path, body, reply }[]` | yes | One card per operation |

**Example**

```tsx
<VerbCards kicker="S3 API" title="…" cards={[{ verb: 'PUT', path: 'PUT /photos/a.jpg', body: '…', reply: '200 OK' }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="nines-dial"></a>

## 186 · Nines dial `<NinesDial />`

**Position**: Number · explain how many nines; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `nines`

A row of digit cells spells out 99.9…%; each press → light the next group of nines and show that group's note below.

**Use when**

- Explain durability or availability figures counted in nines
- Compare how many nines each tier has

**Avoid**

- An ordinary percentage: use BigStat or Gauge
- The digits are not all 9s: use Odometer

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- nines 2–11
- caption ≤ 12 chars
- marks 2–4 steps, upto rises and the last step = nines; label ≤ 10 chars, note ≤ 36 chars

**Interaction**: build: reveal — each step lights one group of nines and its note

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `nines` | `number` | yes | Number of 9s |
| `caption` | `string` | yes | Metric name |
| `marks` | `{ upto, label, note }[]` | yes | Grouped highlighting |

**Example**

```tsx
<NinesDial kicker="耐久度" title="…" nines={11} caption="设计耐久度" marks={[{ upto: 4, label: '四个九', note: '…' }, { upto: 11, label: '十一个九', note: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="tier-ladder"></a>

## 187 · Tier ladder `<TierLadder />`

**Position**: Rating · 3–5 tiers from hot to cold; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `tiers`

3–5 cards step down to the right, colour fading from the accent colour to grey; each press → drop the next tier.

**Use when**

- Ordered tiers such as storage classes, service levels or price bands
- Each tier has the same 2–3 parameters

**Avoid**

- The tiers have no order: use PanelCards
- Which tier to buy: use PricingTiers

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps 3–5
- name ≤ 14 Latin characters or ≤ 8 chars; tag ≤ 6 chars
- specs 2–3: label ≤ 4 chars, value ≤ 8 chars
- ends each ≤ 6 chars

**Interaction**: build: reveal — each step drops the next tier

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `steps` | `{ name, tag?, specs: { label, value }[] }[]` | yes | Top to bottom |
| `ends` | `[string, string]` |  | Labels at both ends |

**Example**

```tsx
<TierLadder kicker="存储类" title="…" ends={['常用', '归档']} steps={[{ name: 'Standard', tag: '默认', specs: [{ label: '取回', value: '毫秒级' }] }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="lifecycle-rail"></a>

## 188 · Lifecycle rail `<LifecycleRail />`

**Position**: Process · one thing changes position over time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `lifecycle`

A ticked rail holds 3–5 stops; a marker block moves to the next stop each step while that stop's card drops in.

**Use when**

- Data, orders or documents move through a few states over time
- Each stop has a clear point in time

**Avoid**

- Several parallel lines: use Swimlane
- Only historical years: use Timeline

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- stops 3–5
- at ≤ 8 characters; title ≤ 12 Latin characters or ≤ 8 chars; body ≤ 32 chars
- marker ≤ 10 Latin characters

**Interaction**: build: reveal — the first stop shows first, then each step moves to the next stop; in the complete state the marker rests on the last stop

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `stops` | `{ at, title, body }[]` | yes | Left to right |
| `marker` | `string` | yes | Text on the moving marker block |

**Example**

```tsx
<LifecycleRail kicker="生命周期" title="…" marker="report.csv" stops={[{ at: '第 0 天', title: 'Standard', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="meter-cards"></a>

## 189 · Metering cards `<MeterCards />`

**Position**: Composition · what a bill is made of; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `pricing`

3–5 cards, each naming one billing or metering dimension; the unit sits large in a dial, with one line of note below.

**Use when**

- Make clear what a bill consists of
- Every item has a clear unit of measure

**Avoid**

- Amounts and shares must be given: use Waterfall or ShareBar
- Plans must be compared: use PricingTiers

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- cards 3–5
- name ≤ 8 chars; unit ≤ 8 characters; note ≤ 40 chars
- unitLabel ≤ 4 chars

**Interaction**: build: reveal — each step shows one card

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `cards` | `{ icon, name, unit, note }[]` | yes | One card per dimension |
| `unitLabel` | `string` |  | Small text above the unit, default “Metering” |

**Example**

```tsx
<MeterCards kicker="怎么计费" title="…" cards={[{ icon: 'database', name: '存储', unit: 'GB·月', note: '…' }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="shelf-gallery"></a>

## 190 · Shelf gallery `<ShelfGallery />`

**Position**: Scenario · 3–4 examples with images; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `uses`

3–4 image cards in a row; the current one widens and gets an accent outline while the others step back; each press → the next card comes forward.

**Use when**

- 3–4 uses, scenarios or cases, each with one image
- Walk through them one by one but keep all of them visible

**Avoid**

- No images: use PanelCards
- The image is evidence and details must be read: use Screenshot

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–4
- image is only a decor- illustration or a real screenshot, and alt is required
- title ≤ 8 chars; body ≤ 36 chars

**Interaction**: build: reveal — the first card starts in front, then each step brings the next one; in the complete state the last card is in front

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ image: { src, alt, credit? }, title, body }[]` | yes | Left to right |

**Example**

```tsx
<ShelfGallery kicker="用在哪里" title="…" items={[{ image: { src: lake, alt: '…', credit: '插图：AI 生成（示意）' }, title: '数据湖', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="tab-spec"></a>

## 191 · Tabbed spec card `<TabSpec />`

**Position**: Parallel · introduce 2–4 options one by one; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p5` — `oss`

A row of tabs on top, one large spec card below (text on the left, parameter rows on the right); each press → switch to the next tab and the card swaps to the next item.

**Use when**

- Introduce 2–4 products, options or open-source projects one by one
- Every item has one paragraph and 2–4 parameters

**Avoid**

- Everything must be compared at once: use CompareTable
- Every item must stay visible when printed: split into several slides

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 2–4
- tab ≤ 12 Latin characters or ≤ 6 chars; name ≤ 16 chars; body ≤ 60 chars
- specs 2–4: label ≤ 4 chars, value ≤ 22 chars

**Interaction**: build: reveal — each step switches one tab; the complete state (print) shows the last item

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ tab, name, body, specs: { label, value }[] }[]` | yes | One item per tab |

**Example**

```tsx
<TabSpec kicker="自建" title="…" items={[{ tab: 'Ceph RGW', name: 'Ceph 对象网关', body: '…', specs: [{ label: 'S3', value: '…' }] }, …]} source="…" />
```

<a id="token-stream"></a>

## 192 · Token stream `<TokenStream />`

**Position**: AI · how text is cut into tokens; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `tokens`

One sentence is cut into numbered token pieces that appear group by group, with the total in the top-right corner; each group has its own background colour and one line of note below.

**Use when**

- Explain tokenisation, billing units or how context gets used up
- Show the audience how many tokens one sentence takes

**Avoid**

- There are no real tokeniser results and no illustrative mark: the split must come from a real tokeniser, or label must say “illustrative”
- More than 40 tokens: split the slide or use ContextWindow

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- groups 2–5, 6–40 tokens in total, each ≤ 8 characters
- label ≤ 16 chars
- notes match the number of groups, each ≤ 22 chars
- spaces show as middle dots

**Interaction**: In build mode each press → one group of tokens and its note appear

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `groups` | `string[][]` | yes | Tokens by group, each group one build step |
| `label` | `string` |  | Note above, e.g. “illustrative: split for demo only” |
| `notes` | `string[]` |  | One note per group |

**Example**

```tsx
<TokenStream kicker="计费单位" title="按 token 计价" label="示意" groups={[['你是', '一名', '助手'], ['请', '检查']]} notes={['可命中缓存', '新输入']} />
```

<a id="moe-router"></a>

## 193 · Expert routing `<MoeRouter />`

**Position**: AI · how a mixture of experts picks experts; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `moe`

Tokens on the left, a router in the middle, routed expert cells on the right (up to 384) plus the shared experts; each step moves to the next token and the picked k cells light up.

**Use when**

- Introduce an MoE model: total experts, how many are active per token
- Show visually that total parameters are large but active parameters are small

**Avoid**

- It is not a mixture-of-experts structure
- What each expert does: the lit cell positions are usually illustrative, not the real assignment

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- experts 8–384; cols default 24, for 384 use 32
- shared 0–2
- routes 1–4, token ≤ 6 characters, note ≤ 20 chars
- caption ≤ 30 chars; when picks are not the real routing, caption must say “illustrative”
- requires build: 'reveal'; without steps it shows the last token

**Interaction**: → route the next token; the lit cells change with it

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `experts` | `number` | yes | Number of routed experts |
| `cols` | `number` |  | Grid columns, default 24 |
| `shared` | `number` |  | Number of shared experts |
| `routes` | `{ token, picks: number[], note? }[]` | yes | Expert indices picked per token (starting at 0) |
| `caption` | `string` |  | Note below the grid |

**Example**

```tsx
<MoeRouter kicker="混合专家" title="每个 token 只走 6 个专家" experts={384} cols={32} shared={1} routes={[{ token: '函数', picks: [17, 58, 140, 201, 266, 349] }]} caption="亮格位置为示意" />
```

<a id="context-window"></a>

## 194 · Context window `<ContextWindow />`

**Position**: AI · capacity and length marks; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `context`

A long capacity bar with ticks; several spans fill in from the left step by step (for example training length, max output, window limit), each with a big number and a note below.

**Use when**

- Introduce lengths measured from 0 such as context length, max output or training length
- Compare how many times longer one span is than another

**Avoid**

- The lengths are parallel rather than nested: use BarChart
- More than 4 spans

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- spans 1–4, label ≤ 8 characters, note ≤ 20 chars
- ticks 3–6, label ≤ 6 characters
- totalLabel ≤ 8 characters

**Interaction**: Each press → fill in one more span

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `total` | `number` | yes | Window capacity (same unit as spans) |
| `totalLabel` | `string` | yes | Large label for the capacity |
| `ticks` | `{ value, label }[]` | yes | Ticks |
| `spans` | `{ value, label, note }[]` | yes | Spans from 0, each one build step |

**Example**

```tsx
<ContextWindow kicker="上下文" title="1M 窗口" total={1000} totalLabel="1M tokens" ticks={[{ value: 0, label: '0' }, { value: 1000, label: '1M' }]} spans={[{ value: 384, label: '384K', note: '单次最大输出' }]} />
```

<a id="score-matrix"></a>

## 195 · Benchmark matrix `<ScoreMatrix />`

**Position**: AI · several models × several benchmarks; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `lead`

Rows are benchmarks and columns are models; each cell holds a score and a thin bar scaled to that row's top score; the focus model's column is coloured in, and the row's top score gets a dot. Rows appear one by one.

**Use when**

- Comparison tables from model cards or technical reports
- How one set of models ranks across 3–7 benchmarks

**Avoid**

- Only two models: use Dumbbell or DeltaBars
- Scores with different definitions (subsets, different metrics) in one row: split them first or record them as —

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- models 3–7, each ≤ 10 characters
- rows 3–7, name ≤ 16 characters, metric ≤ 10 characters
- 1 decimal place by default, decimals can be set per row
- scores must come from the official table; source must state “self-run” or the evaluator

**Interaction**: Each press → one row appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `models` | `string[]` | yes | Column names |
| `focus` | `number` | yes | Index of the focus column |
| `rows` | `{ name, metric?, values: (number \| null)[], decimals? }[]` | yes | One benchmark per row, null shows as — |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Default decimal places |

**Example**

```tsx
<ScoreMatrix kicker="成绩" title="智能体任务" models={['A', 'B', 'C']} focus={2} rows={[{ name: 'Terminal-Bench 2.1', metric: 'Pass@1', values: [89.1, 88.8, 90.6] }]} />
```

<a id="model-spec-card"></a>

## 196 · Model spec card `<ModelSpecCard />`

**Position**: AI · identity card for one model; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `spec`

A card on the left: version tag, model name and capability chips, with bars comparing total and active parameters at the bottom; 4–8 spec rows appear one by one on the right.

**Use when**

- Introduce the core specs of one model (or any system with a total and an actual figure)
- Put total parameters vs active parameters where they are seen at a glance

**Avoid**

- Several models must be compared: use ScoreMatrix or CompareTable
- More than 8 spec rows: split the slide or use SpecSheet

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- name ≤ 18 characters; tag ≤ 16 characters
- chips 2–5, each ≤ 8 chars
- params.total and active each ≤ 4 digits; activeLabel ≤ 6 chars
- specs 4–8: label ≤ 6 chars, value ≤ 18 chars

**Interaction**: Each press → one spec row appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `name` | `string` | yes | Model name |
| `tag` | `string` |  | Small tag at the top of the card |
| `chips` | `string[]` |  | Capability chips |
| `params` | `{ total, active, unit, activeLabel? }` | yes | Total and active parameters |
| `specs` | `{ label, value }[]` | yes | Spec rows, each one build step |

**Example**

```tsx
<ModelSpecCard kicker="规格" title="大模型，小激活" name="Model-X" params={{ total: 552, active: 16, unit: 'B' }} specs={[{ label: '层数', value: '40 层' }, …]} />
```

<a id="token-price-grid"></a>

## 197 · Token price grid `<TokenPriceGrid />`

**Position**: AI · priced per token; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `price`

Rows are token types (cache hit, cache miss, output) and columns are tiers or models; each cell holds a price and a bar on a log scale, so a hundredfold gap stays readable. The focus column is coloured in.

**Use when**

- API prices: input / output, cache, peak and off-peak
- Prices differ by one or two orders of magnitude

**Avoid**

- Only one or two prices: use BigStat or PricingTiers
- Compare in linear proportion: use BarChart (the bars here are log scale)

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- columns 2–4, each ≤ 10 characters
- rows 2–4: label ≤ 10 chars, note ≤ 10 chars
- price ≤ 6 characters (decimal point included)
- unit ≤ 16 characters

**Interaction**: Each press → one price row appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `columns` | `string[]` | yes | Tiers or models |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Index of the focus column |
| `rows` | `{ label, note?, values: number[] }[]` | yes | One token type per row, each row one build step |
| `currency` | `string` |  | Currency symbol, default $ |
| `unit` | `string` | yes | Pricing unit |

**Example**

```tsx
<TokenPriceGrid kicker="价格" title="单价" columns={['闲时', '高峰']} focus={0} unit="美元 / 百万 tokens" rows={[{ label: '输出', values: [0.6, 1.2] }]} />
```

<a id="attention-mask"></a>

## 198 · Attention mask `<AttentionMask />`

**Position**: AI · illustrative attention pattern; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `attention`

A query × key cell grid, with 2–4 patterns on the right (full attention, causal, local window, sparse); each step switches the mask and the lit cells and the lit-cells / total-cells ratio change with it.

**Use when**

- Explain sparse attention, sliding windows or causal masks
- Explain why long context saves compute

**Avoid**

- Real attention weights must be shown: this is an illustrative diagram, not a heatmap (use Heatmap)
- More than 4 patterns

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- size 8–16; window and stride are small integers
- modes 2–4: label ≤ 10 chars, note ≤ 40 chars
- label (above the grid) ≤ 12 chars, default “illustrative”
- requires build: 'reveal'; without steps it shows the last pattern

**Interaction**: → switch to the next mask

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `size` | `number` |  | Cells per side, default 12 |
| `window` | `number` |  | Local window width, default 3 |
| `stride` | `number` |  | Spacing of the global columns in sparse mode, default 4 |
| `modes` | `{ label, kind: 'full' \| 'causal' \| 'window' \| 'sparse', note }[]` | yes | One pattern per build step |
| `label` | `string` |  | Caption above the grid |

**Example**

```tsx
<AttentionMask kicker="注意力" title="不是每个词都要看全部" modes={[{ label: '全注意力', kind: 'full', note: '…' }, { label: '稀疏', kind: 'sparse', note: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="effort-dial"></a>

## 199 · Parameter dial `<EffortDial />`

**Position**: AI · one adjustable parameter; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `effort`

A half-circle dial shows the parameter's min and max and 2–4 stops; each step turns the needle to the next stop, the current value shows below the dial, and the matching note on the right is highlighted.

**Use when**

- Adjustable parameters such as reasoning effort, temperature or thinking budget
- Explain where each stop is used

**Avoid**

- A completion rate or share must be shown: use Gauge
- More than 4 stops

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- param ≤ 20 characters
- stops 2–4: value between min and max, label ≤ 8 chars, note ≤ 36 chars
- requires build: 'reveal'; without steps it points at the last stop

**Interaction**: → the needle turns to the next stop

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `param` | `string` | yes | Parameter name |
| `min` | `number` | yes | Minimum |
| `max` | `number` | yes | Maximum |
| `stops` | `{ value, label, note }[]` | yes | Stops, each one build step |

**Example**

```tsx
<EffortDial kicker="推理强度" title="想多久，可以调" param="reasoning_effort" min={1} max={100} stops={[{ value: 1, label: '最低档', note: '…' }, { value: 100, label: '最高档', note: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="peak-clock"></a>

## 200 · Time-of-day clock `<PeakClock />`

**Position**: AI · time slots within one day; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `peak`

A 24-hour ring marks 1–4 windows (peak, maintenance window) in the accent colour, with the time zone in the centre; the legend and 2–4 notes appear one by one on the right.

**Use when**

- Peak / off-peak pricing, rate-limit windows or maintenance windows
- Windows cross midnight or split into several parts, so a table is unclear

**Avoid**

- Values changing over one day must be shown: use LineChart
- A schedule spanning several days: use Gantt

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- windows 1–4, hours are numbers from 0 to 24
- zone ≤ 8 characters
- onLabel and offLabel each ≤ 6 chars
- notes 2–4: title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: Each press → one note appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `windows` | `{ from, to }[]` | yes | Windows to highlight |
| `zone` | `string` | yes | Time zone |
| `onLabel` | `string` |  | Name of the highlighted window, default “Peak” |
| `offLabel` | `string` |  | Name of the remaining time |
| `notes` | `{ title, body }[]` | yes | Notes, each one build step |

**Example**

```tsx
<PeakClock kicker="高峰时段" title="每天两段高峰" windows={[{ from: 1, to: 4 }, { from: 6, to: 10 }]} zone="UTC" notes={[{ title: '高峰时段', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="alias-route"></a>

## 201 · Alias routing `<AliasRoute />`

**Position**: AI · several names point to the same version; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p4` — `alias`

Left: 2–5 API names or aliases (old names dashed), with lines that converge on one accent card on the right naming the version actually serving requests.

**Use when**

- Model aliases, migrating old names, version mapping
- Opening by confirming which model is meant

**Avoid**

- One-to-many or many-to-many mappings: use FlowDiagram or Sankey
- Names longer than 30 characters

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- sources 2–5: name ≤ 30 monospace characters, tag ≤ 6 chars
- target.label ≤ 14 chars, name ≤ 26 monospace characters, note ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: Each press → one name and its line appear

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `sources` | `{ name, tag?, legacy? }[]` | yes | Names on the left, one build step each |
| `target` | `{ label, name, note? }` | yes | Target version on the right |

**Example**

```tsx
<AliasRoute kicker="先核对名字" title="三个 API 名，同一个模型" sources={[{ name: 'model-flash', tag: '当前' }, { name: 'model-v4-flash', tag: '旧名', legacy: true }]} target={{ label: '实际版本', name: 'Model-V4.1-Flash' }} />
```

<a id="globe-cover"></a>

## 202 · Globe cover `<GlobeCover />`

**Position**: Opening · slide 1; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `cover`

Title, lead and up to 3 facts on the left; on the right an offline dot-matrix globe (orthographic projection) with a few glowing arcs drifting along great circles. The globe is decorative only.

**Use when**

- The topic involves global networks, cross-border business or geographic spread
- Openings in a dark style (mono-studio and similar)

**Avoid**

- Arcs should show real traffic or routes: use ArcGlobe with a source
- The topic has nothing to do with geography: use Cover

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- facts 0–3: value ≤ 8 characters, label ≤ 10 chars
- arcs 0–8, both ends must lie in the hemisphere that center faces

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small text above the title |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `facts` | `{ value, label }[]` |  | Facts at the bottom |
| `center` | `[lon, lat]` | yes | Globe orientation |
| `arcs` | `{ from: [lon, lat], to: [lon, lat] }[]` |  | Decorative arcs |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source of the fact |

**Example**

```tsx
<GlobeCover kicker="公司介绍" title={['Cloudflare', '是做什么的']} center={[10, 25]} arcs={[{ from: [-74, 40.7], to: [-0.1, 51.5] }]} facts={[{ value: '348', label: '个城市' }]} />
```

<a id="arc-globe"></a>

## 203 · Arc globe `<ArcGlobe />`

**Position**: Data · one place to many; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `arc`

On an offline dot-matrix globe, glowing great-circle arcs run from one hub to 2–8 places, with a matching list on the right; in build steps the arcs draw one by one.

**Use when**

- One center distributing to many places: origin and caches, headquarters and branches, egress destinations
- All places lie in the same hemisphere

**Avoid**

- Places spread over both sides of the globe: use DotMap
- Comparing values per place: use DotMap or BarChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- places 2–8, name ≤ 6 chars, note ≤ 14 chars
- hub.name ≤ 6 chars
- listTitle ≤ 10 chars
- All places must lie in the hemisphere that center faces, or the arcs run round the back
- Map precision is 2°, so only city-level or larger locations fit

**Interaction**: Each press → one arc draws and its list row lights up

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
