| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `hub` | `{ name, lon, lat, note? }` | yes | Start point |
| `places` | `{ name, lon, lat, note? }[]` | yes | End points, one build step each |
| `center` | `[lon, lat]` |  | Globe orientation, defaults to hub |
| `listTitle` | `string` |  | List title |

**Example**

```tsx
<ArcGlobe kicker="示意" title="…" hub={{ name: '源站', lon: -77.5, lat: 39 }} center={[-25, 22]} places={[{ name: '伦敦', lon: -0.1, lat: 51.5 }, …]} />
```

<a id="pop-grid"></a>

## 204 · Site dot grid `<PopGrid />`

**Position**: Data · how many, split how; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `pop`

Each glowing dot stands for one site (city, store, node); dots are colored by group into a grid, with the total and each group's count on the right.

**Use when**

- 20–500 similar objects split into 2–8 groups
- The audience should feel the volume and see the composition at once

**Avoid**

- Geography matters: use DotMap
- More than 500 items: use Treemap or ShareBar

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- groups 2–8, name ≤ 8 chars
- Total 20–500
- totalLabel ≤ 10 chars
- unit ≤ 2 characters

**Interaction**: Each press → one group of dots and its legend row light up

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `groups` | `{ name, count }[]` | yes | Groups, one build step each |
| `totalLabel` | `string` | yes | Caption under the total |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit of the count |

**Example**

```tsx
<PopGrid kicker="网络规模" title="348 个城市" groups={[{ name: '亚洲', count: 71 }, …]} totalLabel="个城市" />
```

<a id="request-journey"></a>

## 205 · Request path `<RequestJourney />`

**Position**: Mechanism · where one request goes; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `journey`

3–6 stops sit along a glowing line, each with an icon, name and note; in build steps a glowing packet hops on to the next stop.

**Use when**

- A request, data or an order passes through several layers in turn
- Explaining the order a system handles things

**Avoid**

- There are branches or loops: use FlowDiagram
- More than 6 stops: split the slide or use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- hops 3–6: name ≤ 6 chars, note ≤ 30 chars, tag ≤ 10 characters
- Icons must come from the icon-set names and must not repeat on one slide

**Interaction**: Each press → the packet moves one stop and the line lights up behind it

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `hops` | `{ icon, name, note, tag? }[]` | yes | The stops, one build step each |

**Example**

```tsx
<RequestJourney kicker="怎么工作" title="一个请求经过哪几站" hops={[{ icon: 'routing', tag: 'DNS', name: 'DNS 解析', note: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="shield-layers"></a>

## 206 · Shield layers `<ShieldLayers />`

**Position**: Mechanism · defense in depth; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `shield`

2–5 glowing half rings on the left surround the protected core, with a list on the right of what each layer blocks, outer to inner; in build steps the layers light up one at a time.

**Use when**

- Multi-layer security, filtering or moderation mechanisms
- Stressing the order: outer layers block first, inner layers catch the rest

**Avoid**

- Layers have no inner-outer order: use LayerStack
- You need the detail steps inside each layer: use RequestJourney

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- layers 2–5 (outermost first): name ≤ 10 chars, note ≤ 34 chars, tag ≤ 10 characters
- core ≤ 4 chars

**Interaction**: Each press → one ring and its note light up

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `layers` | `{ name, note, tag? }[]` | yes | Outer to inner, one build step per layer |
| `core` | `string` | yes | The protected object |

**Example**

```tsx
<ShieldLayers kicker="安全" title="从外到内的几层过滤" core="源站" layers={[{ name: '网络层 DDoS 防护', tag: 'L3/4', note: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="spike-chart"></a>

## 207 · Spike chart `<SpikeChart />`

**Position**: Data · a sudden surge; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `spike`

A glowing area line whose maximum is scaled up and labeled with a halo, plus up to 3 callouts that can appear one by one; suits dark styles.

**Use when**

- One series has a clear peak or surge
- Time series such as traffic, attack volume or request volume

**Avoid**

- Comparing several series: use LineChart
- The values have no clear peak: use LineChart or BarChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- x 3–24, each ≤ 8 characters
- unit ≤ 6 characters; peak including the unit ≤ 12 characters
- callouts 0–3, text ≤ 24 chars, do not place them on the peak
- note ≤ 50 chars; explain estimates here

**Interaction**: Each press → one callout appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `x` | `string[]` | yes | X-axis labels |
| `values` | `number[]` | yes | Values |
| `unit` | `string` |  | Unit |
| `decimals` | `number` |  | Decimal places |
| `callouts` | `{ at, text }[]` |  | Callouts, one build step each |
| `note` | `string` |  | Note under the chart |

**Example**

```tsx
<SpikeChart kicker="安全" title="…" x={['2023', '2024', '2025']} values={[14, 21.3, 47.1]} unit=" 百万次" note="前两年为估算" />
```

<a id="outage-board"></a>

## 208 · Outage board `<OutageBoard />`

**Position**: Evidence · how one incident unfolded; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `outage`

Like a status page: one time track per service, with outages and partial impact marked in two fills, and dashed vertical lines through every row at key moments.

**Use when**

- Reviewing which services were affected when in one incident
- A timeline within one day, on the scale of hours

**Avoid**

- Spans several days or months: use Gantt
- Only one timeline: use Timeline

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 2–6: name ≤ 10 chars, note ≤ 20 chars
- ticks 3–8, times written HH:MM, all on the same day
- events 0–3, label ≤ 10 chars, adjacent events at least 15% of the window apart
- each legend item ≤ 6 chars

**Interaction**: Each press → one service track appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `from` | `string` | yes | Window start, HH:MM |
| `to` | `string` | yes | Window end, HH:MM |
| `ticks` | `string[]` | yes | Ticks |
| `rows` | `{ name, note?, spans: { from, to, state: 'down' \| 'degraded' }[] }[]` | yes | Services, one build step per row |
| `events` | `{ at, label }[]` |  | Key moments |
| `zone` | `string` |  | Time zone |
| `legend` | `{ down, degraded }` |  | Legend text |

**Example**

```tsx
<OutageBoard kicker="可靠性" title="…" from="11:00" to="17:30" ticks={['11:00', …]} rows={[{ name: '核心 CDN', spans: [{ from: '11:28', to: '14:30', state: 'down' }] }, …]} zone="UTC" />
```

<a id="reach-rings"></a>

## 209 · Reach rings `<ReachRings />`

**Position**: Data · how far coverage reaches; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `reach`

2–4 glowing concentric rings around a center point, with a radar sweep passing slowly; one row per ring on the right gives the distance or latency and how much it covers.

**Use when**

- Coverage counted in distance, latency or time bands
- Statements about how many users are within N

**Avoid**

- The bands do not nest: use BarChart
- More than 4 bands: use LineChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rings 2–4 (innermost first): label ≤ 8 characters, value ≤ 6 characters, text ≤ 30 chars
- center ≤ 4 chars

**Interaction**: Each press → one ring and its note light up

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `rings` | `{ label, value, text }[]` | yes | Inner to outer, one build step per ring |
| `center` | `string` | yes | Center label |

**Example**

```tsx
<ReachRings kicker="网络" title="离用户有多近" center="用户" rings={[{ label: '50 ms', value: '95%', text: '…' }]} />
```

<a id="server-rack"></a>

## 210 · Server rack `<ServerRack />`

**Position**: Mechanism · where it runs; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `rack`

A dark rack with 4–8 slots, each labeled with one service and a blinking indicator, plus 1–3 points on the right; in build steps the slots power on one at a time.

**Use when**

- Showing which services run on one machine or node
- A plain picture of infrastructure or deployment layout

**Avoid**

- Explaining hierarchy: use ArchLayers
- Explaining calls between services: use FlowDiagram

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- units 4–8: name ≤ 10 chars, tag ≤ 12 characters
- points 1–3, each ≤ 36 chars
- label ≤ 12 characters

**Interaction**: Each press → one slot powers on

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `units` | `{ name, tag? }[]` | yes | Slots, one build step each |
| `points` | `string[]` |  | Points |
| `label` | `string` |  | Small label above the rack |

**Example**

```tsx
<ServerRack kicker="怎么工作" title="每台服务器都跑全部服务" units={[{ name: 'DNS', tag: 'dns' }, …]} points={['…']} />
```

<a id="trace-timeline"></a>

## 211 · Signal timeline `<TraceTimeline />`

**Position**: Background · how it developed; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p3` — `history`

The history sits on a glowing signal line: each event is a waveform spike with a glowing node, text alternates above and below, and a pulse runs along the line.

**Use when**

- 4–7 points in history
- History slides on network, telecom or technology topics

**Avoid**

- More than 7 points or a lot of text per item: use Timeline
- Duration matters: use Gantt

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- events 4–7: date ≤ 10 characters, title ≤ 10 chars, note ≤ 26 chars (≤ 18 chars with 7 events)

**Interaction**: Each press → one event appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `events` | `{ date, title, note? }[]` | yes | Events, one build step each |

**Example**

```tsx
<TraceTimeline kicker="历程" title="…" events={[{ date: '2009', title: '公司成立', note: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="query-plan"></a>

## 212 · Query plan tree `<QueryPlan />`

**Position**: Technology · how one query is executed; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `plan`

An EXPLAIN-style plan tree with nodes and links drawn top-down; SQL can sit above it, and one node can be marked in the accent color.

**Use when**

- Hierarchical structures such as database query plans, compiler syntax trees or call trees
- The audience should read from the root down, level by level

**Avoid**

- More than 7 nodes or more than 4 levels: split the slide or use IssueTree
- Nodes are not in a parent-child relation: use NodeGraph

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- sql 1–3 lines, each ≤ 56 characters
- plan ≤ 4 levels, ≤ 7 nodes, ≤ 3 leaves
- op ≤ 18 characters; detail ≤ 24 characters; meta ≤ 16 characters
- note ≤ 50 chars

**Interaction**: With SlideDef.build = 'reveal', each press → the next level expands

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `sql` | `string[]` |  | The query above, one line per item |
| `plan` | `PlanNode { op, detail?, meta?, hot?, children? }` | yes | Root of the plan tree |
| `note` | `string` |  | One line of reading under the tree |

**Example**

```tsx
<QueryPlan kicker="EXPLAIN" title="…" sql={['EXPLAIN SELECT …']} plan={{ op: 'Hash Join', detail: 'o.customer_id = c.id', children: [{ op: 'Index Scan', hot: true }, { op: 'Hash', children: [{ op: 'Seq Scan' }] }] }} />
```

<a id="schema-er"></a>

## 213 · Table relationship diagram `<SchemaEr />`

**Position**: Technology · data model; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `schema`

2–4 tables left to right, listing column names, types and PK/FK, with foreign keys drawn as elbow lines to the referenced column and optional cardinality labels.

**Use when**

- Data models, foreign key constraints, references between API objects
- Few enough tables and columns to fit on one screen

**Avoid**

- More than 4 tables or more than 6 columns each: draw only the key tables
- A relation skips a neighboring table: reorder or split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- tables 2–4; name ≤ 14 characters; cols 2–6, name ≤ 14 characters, type ≤ 12 characters
- drop 0–3
- links ≤ 4, only between adjacent tables; label ≤ 6 characters
- note ≤ 50 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press → the next table and its links appear

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `tables` | `{ name, cols: { name, type, key? }[], drop? }[]` | yes | Tables, in left-to-right order |
| `links` | `{ from, to, label? }[]` | yes | Foreign keys, written as 'table.column' |
| `note` | `string` |  | One line of reading under the diagram |

**Example**

```tsx
<SchemaEr kicker="一致性" title="…" tables={[{ name: 'customers', cols: [{ name: 'id', type: 'bigint', key: 'pk' }, …] }, …]} links={[{ from: 'orders.customer_id', to: 'customers.id', label: 'N:1' }]} />
```

<a id="result-grid"></a>

## 214 · Query and result `<ResultGrid />`

**Position**: Technology · Data · one query and the table it returns; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `support`

The query sits in a dark code window on the left, the result table on the right; one column can take the accent color and some rows a darker fill, with a status line at the bottom.

**Use when**

- One query carries a small table of facts
- Showing what an SQL or API query returns

**Avoid**

- More than 7 rows or 5 columns: keep only the key rows
- No query, only a table: use CompareTable or SpecSheet

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- sql 2–6 lines, each ≤ 40 characters
- columns 2–5, each ≤ 14 characters
- rows 3–7, each cell ≤ 14 characters
- status ≤ 24 characters
- note ≤ 60 chars

**Interaction**: The result table is one build step

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `sql` | `string[]` | yes | The query, one line per item |
| `columns` | `string[]` | yes | Column names |
| `rows` | `string[][]` | yes | Result rows |
| `mark` | `number` |  | Index of the accent-colored column |
| `hot` | `number[]` |  | Rows with a darker fill |
| `status` | `string` |  | Status line under the table |
| `note` | `string` |  | One line of reading under the query |

**Example**

```tsx
<ResultGrid kicker="版本节奏" title="…" sql={['SELECT version, eol', '  FROM …']} columns={['version', 'eol']} rows={[['18', '2030-11-14'], …]} mark={1} status="(5 rows)" />
```

<a id="code-callouts"></a>

## 215 · Code callouts `<CodeCallouts />`

**Position**: Technology · walking through code line by line; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `postgis`

A dark code window with line numbers, numbered dots beside the annotated lines and matching notes in the right column; each build step lights one note and its line.

**Use when**

- The key lines of a config, SQL statement or script
- Each note maps to a specific line

**Avoid**

- Comparing a change: use CodeDiff
- Showing a command and its output: use Terminal

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- code 4–12 lines, each ≤ 52 characters
- file ≤ 20 characters
- notes 2–4: title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press → the next note and its lines light up; lines not yet covered stay readable

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `code` | `string[]` | yes | The code, one line per item |
| `file` | `string` |  | Window title |
| `notes` | `{ lines: number[], title, body }[]` | yes | Callout notes, lines counted from 1 |
| `sql` | `boolean` |  | Highlight SQL keywords, default true |

**Example**

```tsx
<CodeCallouts kicker="可扩展性" title="…" file="postgis.sql" code={['CREATE EXTENSION postgis;', …]} notes={[{ lines: [1], title: '加载扩展', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="request-response"></a>

## 216 · Request and response `<RequestResponse />`

**Position**: Technology · one round trip; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `returning`

Two code windows: the outgoing request on the left, the returned response on the right, with a labeled arrow between them and a light dot running left to right.

**Use when**

- One API call, an SQL statement and its return, a protocol round trip
- Both request and response fit in a few lines of text

**Avoid**

- Several round trips or several parties: use SessionSequence
- Only a command and its output, with no two ends: use Terminal

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- label ≤ 10 chars; file ≤ 20 characters
- lines 2–9, each ≤ 36 characters
- via ≤ 8 chars
- note ≤ 50 chars

**Interaction**: The arrow and the response are one build step; the light dot stops under reduced motion and in print

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `request` | `{ label, file?, lines }` | yes | The request |
| `response` | `{ label, file?, lines }` | yes | The response |
| `via` | `string` |  | Label under the arrow |
| `note` | `string` |  | One line of reading below |

**Example**

```tsx
<RequestResponse kicker="一次往返" title="…" request={{ label: '客户端发送', lines: ['UPDATE …'] }} via="一次往返" response={{ label: '服务器返回', lines: ['UPDATE 1'] }} />
```

<a id="beam-flow"></a>

## 217 · Beam flow diagram `<BeamFlow />`

**Position**: Technology · Architecture · many in, one out; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `process`

Inputs in the left column, one hub in the middle, outputs on the right, joined by curves with light dots moving along them to show where data or requests go.

**Use when**

- One hub connecting several sources and destinations: a gateway, a database process, a message bus
- Direction matters more than hierarchy

**Avoid**

- There is a layered relation: use ArchLayers
- There are sequential steps: use FlowDiagram
- Nodes form an arbitrary mesh: use NodeGraph

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- inputs and outputs 2–4 each; label ≤ 10 chars or ≤ 18 characters; sub ≤ 14 chars
- hub as above
- each heads item ≤ 8 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode the input column, the hub and the output column appear in turn; the light dots stop under reduced motion and in print

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `inputs` | `{ label, sub? }[]` | yes | Left column |
| `hub` | `{ label, sub? }` | yes | The hub in the middle |
| `outputs` | `{ label, sub? }[]` | yes | Right column |
| `heads` | `[string, string]` |  | Headings for the left and right columns |

**Example**

```tsx
<BeamFlow kicker="架构" title="…" inputs={[{ label: 'psql' }, …]} hub={{ label: 'postmaster' }} outputs={[{ label: '共享缓冲区' }, …]} />
```

<a id="tuple-versions"></a>

## 218 · Version timeline `<TupleVersions />`

**Position**: Technology · Concurrency · several versions of one row; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `mvcc`

On a transaction number axis each version is a bar from xmin to xmax, with versions still valid in the accent color; a snapshot is a dashed vertical line labeled below with the version it sees.

**Use when**

- MVCC, multiple versions, snapshot isolation
- Any valid-time span where one object holds different values over time

**Avoid**

- More than 4 versions
- Time is real dates and the scale must be exact: use Gantt

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- versions 2–4; value ≤ 20 characters
- snapshots 0–2; label ≤ 10 chars
- range takes two integers
- axis ≤ 12 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press → one version appears, then the snapshot last

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `versions` | `{ value, xmin, xmax? }[]` | yes | Versions, oldest first |
| `snapshots` | `{ at, label }[]` |  | Snapshot positions |
| `range` | `[number, number]` | yes | Axis range |
| `axis` | `string` |  | Axis caption |

**Example**

```tsx
<TupleVersions kicker="MVCC" title="…" range={[100, 120]} versions={[{ value: 'balance = 100', xmin: 101, xmax: 108 }, …]} snapshots={[{ at: 105, label: '快照 105' }]} />
```

<a id="btree-index"></a>

## 219 · B-tree lookup `<BTreeIndex />`

**Position**: Technology · Index · lookup path; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `btree`

Index pages and keys are drawn by level; pages on the lookup path get an accent outline and the matching key is inverted, with one note per level on the right.

**Use when**

- Lookups that narrow the range level by level: B-tree, skip list, hierarchical directory
- Explaining why it is fast

**Avoid**

- More than 3 levels or more than 4 pages per level
- Not a lookup but plain hierarchy: use Pyramid or IssueTree

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- levels 2–3; ≤ 4 pages per level; 1–4 keys per page, each ≤ 5 characters
- target ≤ 8 characters
- notes one line per level, ≤ 30 chars
- leafNote ≤ 24 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press → the lookup goes one level deeper

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `levels` | `string[][][]` | yes | Level → page → key |
| `path` | `number[]` | yes | Index of the page visited at each level |
| `target` | `string` | yes | The key to look up |
| `notes` | `string[]` |  | One note per level |
| `leafNote` | `string` |  | Note at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<BTreeIndex kicker="索引" title="…" target="42" levels={[[['30', '60']], [['10', '20'], ['40', '50'], ['70', '80']]]} path={[0, 1]} />
```

<a id="json-tree"></a>

## 220 · Document and paths `<JsonTree />`

**Position**: Technology · Data · querying a document; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `jsonb`

An indented JSON document sits in a dark window on the left; on the right are a few path expressions with their returned values, and each step marks the lines that expression picks.

**Use when**

- How values are read from JSON or YAML config
- Path syntax such as JSONB operators, JSONPath or jq

**Avoid**

- The document exceeds 13 lines: keep only the relevant part
- Comparing two documents: use CodeDiff

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- doc 6–13 lines; depth 0–4; text ≤ 34 characters
- queries 2–4: expr ≤ 40 characters, result ≤ 24 characters, note ≤ 20 chars
- file ≤ 20 characters

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press → the next query appears and the lines it selects light up

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `doc` | `{ depth, text, pick? }[]` | yes | Document lines; pick is the index of the query that selects this line |
| `queries` | `{ expr, result, note? }[]` | yes | Path expressions |
| `file` | `string` |  | Window title |

**Example**

```tsx
<JsonTree kicker="JSONB" title="…" doc={[{ depth: 0, text: '{' }, { depth: 1, text: '"name": "Ada",', pick: 0 }, …]} queries={[{ expr: "profile ->> 'name'", result: 'Ada' }, …]} />
```

<a id="session-sequence"></a>

## 221 · Session sequence diagram `<SessionSequence />`

**Position**: Technology · Concurrency · what happens first; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p2` — `isolation`

2–3 sessions each get a column, events run top to bottom in time order with t1, t2… on the left; waits use dashed boxes and the final result uses the accent color.

**Use when**

- Transaction isolation, lock waits, races
- Call ordering between two or three parties

**Avoid**

- More than 7 events
- Only one party: use StepTrack or Timeline
- A process spanning teams: use Swimlane

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- lanes 2–3, each ≤ 10 chars
- events 3–7: text ≤ 30 characters, note ≤ 12 chars
- axis ≤ 4 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press → the next event appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `lanes` | `string[]` | yes | Session names |
| `events` | `{ lane, text, note?, kind?: 'wait' \| 'ok' }[]` | yes | Events in time order |
| `axis` | `string` |  | Time axis label, default is time |

**Example**

```tsx
<SessionSequence kicker="隔离" title="…" lanes={['会话 A', '会话 B']} events={[{ lane: 0, text: 'BEGIN;' }, { lane: 1, text: 'UPDATE …', note: '等待 A', kind: 'wait' }, …]} />
```

<a id="badge-hero"></a>

## 222 · Badge hero `<BadgeHero />`

**Position**: Opening · website-style hero; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `hero`

A centered pill badge (section name plus one status line), a large title, a lead and 1–2 drawn buttons; a wide image sits in a window that rises from the bottom edge.

**Use when**

- Opening a deck that introduces a product, project or release
- A wide decorative illustration or real screenshot is available

**Avoid**

- No image: use Cover or Statement
- The buttons are drawn only and cannot be clicked live; put a real link in note or on the closing slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- kicker ≤ 6 chars; badge ≤ 18 chars
- title 1 line ≤ 12 chars (2 lines at most)
- lead ≤ 60 chars
- actions 1–2, label ≤ 10 chars or ≤ 18 Latin characters
- note ≤ 30 chars
- image a wide image whose top matters (about 290u of height shows), credit ≤ 20 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small accent label inside the badge |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Large title |
| `badge` | `string` |  | Text inside the badge |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One-sentence lead |
| `actions` | `{ label, kind?: 'primary' \| 'ghost' \| 'code', icon? }[]` |  | The drawn buttons |
| `note` | `string` |  | Small monospace text under the buttons |
| `image` | `{ src, alt, credit?, focus? }` | yes | An imported decor- illustration or a real screenshot |

**Example**

```tsx
<BadgeHero kicker="介绍" badge="当前稳定版 · Fedora Linux 44" title="认识 Fedora Linux" lead="…" actions={[{ label: '从这里开始' }, { label: 'fedoraproject.org', kind: 'ghost' }]} image={{ src: hero, alt: '…', credit: '插图：AI 生成（装饰）' }} />
```

<a id="tab-panels"></a>

## 223 · Tab panels `<TabPanels />`

**Position**: Expansion · several sides of one topic; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `process`

A horizontal tab bar with an accent underline under the current tab; below it one large card shows the current tab's title, body and 0–3 points, with room for one number on the right. Each press → switch to the next tab.

**Use when**

- A process or topic has 3–5 parallel sides and you want to cover one at a time
- Each side has about the same amount of content

**Avoid**

- Items must be compared at once: use Matrix or CompareTable
- Each tab needs a screenshot: use TabbedShot
- In print and in the overview only the tab set by open shows; the other tabs stay hidden

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- tabs 3–5; tab ≤ 6 chars or ≤ 12 Latin characters
- title ≤ 20 chars; body ≤ 80 chars
- points 0–3, each ≤ 22 chars
- stat.value ≤ 8 characters, stat.label ≤ 12 chars; with stat, points is at most 2

**Interaction**: With build: 'focus', each press → the next tab is selected and the content changes

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `tabs` | `{ tab, icon?, title, body, points?, stat? }[]` | yes | Tabs and their content |
| `open` | `number` |  | Tab shown in the full state, default 0 |

**Example**

```tsx
<TabPanels kicker="一个版本怎么来" title="从开发树到正式版" tabs={[{ tab: 'Rawhide', icon: 'branch', title: '新软件先进入开发树', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="edition-picker"></a>

## 224 · Edition picker `<EditionPicker />`

**Position**: Options · introducing a few editions or plans one by one; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `editions`

3–6 single-choice cards (3 per row): icon, tag, name, description and a fit line at the bottom, with a radio dot in the top right. The selected card gets an accent outline and a check; each press → the next card is selected.

**Use when**

- A product's editions, plans or releases, each with its own use
- Going through them one by one, saying who each one fits

**Avoid**

- Prices must be compared: use PricingTiers
- More than 6 options: split the slide or use FilterChips

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- options 3–6
- name ≤ 12 Latin characters or ≤ 8 chars; tag ≤ 6 chars
- desc ≤ 40 chars (≤ 30 chars with 6 options)
- fit ≤ 24 chars (≤ 14 chars with 6 options)

**Interaction**: With build: 'focus', each press → the selection moves to the next card

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `options` | `{ name, icon?, tag?, desc, fit? }[]` | yes | The option cards |
| `selected` | `number` |  | Card selected in the full state, default 0 |
| `fitLabel` | `string` |  | Label of the fit line, default is fit |

**Example**

```tsx
<EditionPicker kicker="Editions" title="六个正式版本" options={[{ name: 'Workstation', icon: 'laptop', tag: '桌面', desc: '搭载最新 GNOME 桌面的版本。', fit: '笔记本和台式机' }, …]} />
```

<a id="release-train"></a>

## 225 · Release train `<ReleaseTrain />`

**Position**: Time · overlapping version lifecycles; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `lifecycle`

One row per version: version number, state pill (end of life / maintained / latest / beta / in development) and dates on the left; on the right a bar from release to end of maintenance on a true time scale, and a vertical line marks today. Estimated end dates fade out.

**Use when**

- Support periods of software versions or product generations overlap
- Showing how many versions are maintained right now

**Avoid**

- Dates but no spans: use Milestones
- A span over 6 years: the bars get too short, so split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- rows 3–6, in time order
- version ≤ 10 characters; note ≤ 20 characters
- start / end / from / until / today are written YYYY-MM; a span of 18–48 months fits best
- todayLabel ≤ 8 chars
- Dates that are estimates need estimate set and a note in source

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press → one row appears

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `rows` | `{ version, from, until?, state: 'eol' \| 'supported' \| 'current' \| 'beta' \| 'dev', note?, estimate? }[]` | yes | Version rows; omitting until means it continues |
| `start` | `string` | yes | Axis start, YYYY-MM |
| `end` | `string` | yes | Axis end, YYYY-MM |
| `today` | `string` |  | Today line, YYYY-MM |
| `todayLabel` | `string` |  | Label of the today line, default is today |
| `stateLabels` | `Partial<Record<state, string>>` |  | Override the state pill text, each ≤ 4 chars |

**Example**

```tsx
<ReleaseTrain kicker="生命周期" title="每版维护约 13 个月" start="2025-01" end="2027-09" today="2026-10" rows={[{ version: 'Fedora 44', from: '2026-04', until: '2027-05', state: 'current', estimate: true }, …]} />
```

<a id="mega-menu"></a>

## 226 · Mega menu `<MegaMenu />`

**Position**: Overview · the categories of one family; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `family`

A website navigation bar (a text brand name on the left, a few menu items, one of them expanded), with the expanded mega menu below: 2–4 columns, each column a monospaced heading and 2–5 entries (name + one-line description).

**Use when**

- An overview with a few top categories and a few items in each, such as a product line, a version family or a documentation index
- You want the audience to see the whole picture at a glance, the way a website menu shows it

**Avoid**

- Each item needs a detailed explanation: use IconGrid or split the slide
- There is only one level of categories: use Agenda or IconGrid

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- brand ≤ 10 characters, text only, no logo drawn
- menu 3–6 items, each ≤ 6 chars or ≤ 10 Latin characters
- columns 2–4; heading ≤ 8 chars
- items 2–5 per column; name ≤ 14 characters, desc ≤ 16 chars (4 columns: ≤ 10 chars)

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press reveals one column

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `brand` | `string` | yes | Text on the left of the navigation bar |
| `menu` | `string[]` | yes | Menu items |
| `active` | `number` |  | Expanded menu item, default 0 |
| `columns` | `{ heading, icon?, items: { name, desc? }[] }[]` | yes | Menu columns |

**Example**

```tsx
<MegaMenu kicker="版本家族" title="官网把 Fedora 分成四类下载" brand="Fedora" menu={['Editions', 'Atomic', 'Spins', 'Labs']} columns={[{ heading: 'Editions', icon: 'monitor', items: [{ name: 'Workstation', desc: 'GNOME 桌面' }, …] }, …]} />
```

<a id="path-crumbs"></a>

## 227 · Path breadcrumbs `<PathCrumbs />`

**Position**: Relationship · upstream, downstream and flow; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `upstream`

A row of arrow-shaped breadcrumbs (name + small monospaced text), with the last section (or the one set by focus) filled in the accent colour; a note card sits under each section, joined by thin lines.

**Use when**

- A one-way chain such as upstream to downstream, or source to finished product
- 3–5 stages, one or two sentences each

**Avoid**

- The chain forks or loops: use FlowDiagram or NodeGraph
- Each step needs a lot of text: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- crumbs 3–5
- label ≤ 8 chars or ≤ 14 Latin characters (5 crumbs: ≤ 10 Latin characters)
- sub ≤ 16 characters
- body ≤ 50 chars; tag ≤ 8 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press reveals one breadcrumb section and its card together

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `crumbs` | `{ label, sub?, body, tag? }[]` | yes | Stages, upstream first |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Highlighted stage, default the last one |

**Example**

```tsx
<PathCrumbs kicker="和 RHEL 的关系" title="Fedora 是上游" crumbs={[{ label: 'Rawhide', sub: 'Fedora 开发树', body: '…' }, { label: 'ELN', … }, …]} />
```

<a id="filter-chips"></a>

## 228 · Filter chips `<FilterChips />`

**Position**: Classification · filter one set by tag; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `variants`

A row of filter chips (with counts) above, 4–12 small cards below; each press selects the next chip, cards that belong to it keep their outline and the rest turn grey. One card can belong to several chips.

**Use when**

- A set of items has overlapping categories and you want to go through them one group at a time
- There are many items but each needs only one sentence

**Avoid**

- Categories do not overlap and there are only two or three: use Matrix or group them with IconGrid
- Items need a detailed explanation: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- chips 2–6, each ≤ 6 chars or ≤ 10 Latin characters
- cards 4–12 (4 per row); title ≤ 16 Latin characters or ≤ 8 chars; desc ≤ 22 chars (12 cards: ≤ 16 chars)
- in holds the chip indexes, at least 1

**Interaction**: With build: 'focus': each press switches to the next filter and cards outside that group turn grey

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `chips` | `string[]` | yes | Filter chips |
| `cards` | `{ title, desc?, icon?, in: number[] }[]` | yes | Cards and the chips they belong to |
| `active` | `number` |  | Chip selected in the complete state, default -1 (all lit) |

**Example**

```tsx
<FilterChips kicker="按需挑选" title="同一个桌面，两种做法" chips={['Atomic', 'Spins', '平铺窗口']} cards={[{ title: 'Silverblue', desc: '基于 GNOME', in: [0] }, …]} />
```

<a id="cta-block"></a>

## 229 · Call to action block `<CtaBlock />`

**Position**: Closing · what to do next; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `start`

One large rounded block in a light accent tint across the slide: on the left the kicker, the big title, the lead, a copy box (monospaced address or command) and buttons; on the right 2–4 numbered step cards that appear one by one.

**Use when**

- After the talk, tell the audience how to start, where to go and which command to run
- There is one clear address or command

**Avoid**

- You only want to sum up the point: use Closing or Recap
- There are more than 4 steps: use StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title 1–2 lines, ≤ 10 chars per line
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- command ≤ 44 characters; commandLabel ≤ 10 chars
- actions 1–3, label ≤ 10 chars
- steps 2–4: title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 30 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press reveals one step

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `command` | `string` |  | Address or command inside the copy box |
| `commandLabel` | `string` |  | Label above the copy box |
| `actions` | `{ label, kind?: 'primary' \| 'ghost' \| 'code', icon? }[]` |  | Buttons drawn on the slide |
| `steps` | `{ title, body }[]` | yes | Numbered steps |

**Example**

```tsx
<CtaBlock kicker="开始使用" title={['先在 U 盘上', '试一试']} command="fedoraproject.org/workstation/download" actions={[{ label: '下载 Workstation' }]} steps={[{ title: '制作启动盘', body: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="footer-closing"></a>

## 230 · Footer closing `<FooterClosing />`

**Position**: Ending · resources and links; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `end`

A closing slide shaped like a website footer: title, lead and buttons at the top left, 2–4 link columns at the top right, a thin rule at the bottom (note + source) and a line of huge fading text sitting on the bottom edge of the slide.

**Use when**

- The last slide lists places to go: documentation, community, downloads
- The subject has a short Latin-script name for the big text at the bottom

**Avoid**

- You need questions or contacts: use Thanks or ContactPanel
- There are more than 15 links: keep only the most used

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title 1–2 lines, ≤ 10 chars per line; lead ≤ 40 chars
- actions 0–2
- columns 2–4: heading ≤ 8 chars, links 2–5, each ≤ 26 Latin characters or ≤ 13 chars (4 columns: ≤ 18 Latin characters)
- wordmark 3–10 Latin characters, decorative text only, no logo drawn
- note ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: In build mode, each press reveals one link column

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `columns` | `{ heading, links: string[] }[]` | yes | Link columns |
| `actions` | `{ label, kind? }[]` |  | Buttons drawn on the slide |
| `wordmark` | `string` | yes | Big text at the bottom |
| `note` | `string` |  | Small text at the bottom left |

**Example**

```tsx
<FooterClosing kicker="谢谢" title={['想继续了解，', '从这些地方开始']} columns={[{ heading: '阅读', links: ['docs.fedoraproject.org'] }, …]} wordmark="Fedora" />
```

<a id="faq-accordion"></a>

## 231 · Accordion Q&A `<FaqAccordion />`

**Position**: Q&A · open one at a time; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/v3-p1` — `faq`

Kicker, title, lead and optional buttons on the left; a column of questions divided by thin rules on the right, each row with a number, the question and a round +/− button. Only one answer is open at a time; each press closes the previous one and opens the next.

**Use when**

- 3–6 common questions you want to go through one by one
- The answers are short, one or two sentences

**Avoid**

- You want all questions and answers at once: use Faq
- The questions are steps: use ProcessAccordion or StepTrack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items 3–6; q ≤ 24 chars (≤ 18 chars on one line)
- a ≤ 70 chars
- title 1–2 lines, ≤ 8 chars per line; lead ≤ 50 chars
- Print and overview show only the one item set by open

**Interaction**: With build: 'focus': each press opens the next item and closes the previous one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Small section label above the title, 2–8 chars |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Slide title; pass an array to break lines manually, one item per line |
| `lead` | `string` |  | One or two lead sentences, ≤ 60 chars |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source for the facts; required when the slide contains data or claims |
| `hint` | `ReactNode` |  | Action hint in the header centre; may use <kbd> |
| `items` | `{ q, a }[]` | yes | Questions and answers |
| `open` | `number` |  | Item open in the complete state, default 0 |
| `actions` | `{ label, kind? }[]` |  | Buttons under the lead, 0–1 |

**Example**

```tsx
<FaqAccordion kicker="常见问题" title={['五个常被问到的', '问题']} items={[{ q: '谁来做决定？', a: '…' }, …]} />
```

<a id="media-expand"></a>

## 232 · Small window expand `<MediaExpand />`

**Position**: Motion · a screenshot or photo grows from a small window to the full slide; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/motion` — `expand`

The screenshot first appears as a small window next to the title; with build: 'reveal', one press expands it to fill the slide, the title steps aside and a caption appears over the image. When jumping to the slide, printing or with reduced motion, the expanded view is shown directly.

**Use when**

- Product demos and cases: let people recognise the screen first, then look at the details
- One image is the point of the slide

**Avoid**

- The screenshot needs annotations: use Screenshot
- The subject sits near the image edge and would be cropped when expanded

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- caption ≤ 24 chars
- src is a real screenshot or a photo with its source stated
- Use build: 'reveal' (1 step only)

**Interaction**: Press → expand the image

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `src` | `string` | yes | Image |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Image description |
| `caption` | `string` | yes | One line over the image once it is expanded |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<MediaExpand
  kicker="小窗放大"
  title={['先认出画面，', '再看清细节']}
  src={overview}
  alt="总览视图截图"
  caption="总览视图：所有页面按顺序排开"
/>
```

<a id="scatter-order"></a>

## 233 · Scatter to order `<ScatterOrder />`

**Position**: Motion · scattered cards fall into place one by one along a path; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/motion` — `scatter`

4–6 cards start scattered and tilted; with build: 'reveal', each press drops one card into its place, and once all are in place the connecting line appears. When jumping to the slide, the arranged path is shown directly.

**Use when**

- Turn scattered items into ordered steps
- A process, a method or a preparation checklist

**Avoid**

- The steps loop: use Cycle
- Each step needs a detailed explanation: use a Process template

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps 4–6
- title ≤ 8 chars
- body ≤ 28 chars
- slide title ≤ 22 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars

**Interaction**: Press → cards fall into place one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `steps` | `{ title, body }[]` | yes | In reading order |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<ScatterOrder
  kicker="散点成序"
  title="一份演示从散乱的笔记开始"
  steps={[{ title: '收集事实', body: '…' }, { title: '写故事线', body: '…' }, { title: '配模板', body: '…' }, { title: '检查看图', body: '…' }]}
/>
```

<a id="tilt-shot"></a>

## 234 · Tilt to straight `<TiltShot />`

**Position**: Motion · a screenshot in perspective straightens, then the points are covered one by one; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/motion` — `tilt`

Title and 2–3 points on the left, a screenshot entering at a perspective angle on the right. With build: 'reveal', the first press straightens the screenshot, and each press after that reveals one point. When jumping to the slide, the straightened screenshot and all points are shown directly.

**Use when**

- Introducing the interface of software, a tool or a console
- Give the overall impression first, then cover 2–3 highlights

**Avoid**

- You need to mark positions on the screenshot: use Screenshot
- There is no real screenshot

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- points 2–3: title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 20 chars
- src must be a real screenshot

**Interaction**: Press → straighten the screenshot, then reveal the points one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `src` | `string` | yes | Screenshot |
| `alt` | `string` | yes | Screenshot description |
| `ratio` | `number` |  | Width ÷ height, default 16/10 |
| `points` | `{ title, body }[]` | yes | Points |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<TiltShot
  kicker="倾斜摆正"
  title={['组件库：', '先看全貌再细读']}
  src={kit}
  alt="组件库页面截图"
  points={[{ title: '按位置分组', body: '…' }, { title: '每个模板带上限', body: '…' }]}
/>
```

<a id="converge-shots"></a>

## 235 · Images converge `<ConvergeShots />`

**Position**: Motion · several images stand side by side, then converge into one focus image; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/motion` — `converge`

3–4 images stand side by side, each with a label. With build: 'reveal', the first press brings them together, the focus image grows to the front and the others stack behind it; the second press shows the caption. When jumping to the slide, the converged view is shown directly.

**Use when**

- Several screens or views all end in one result
- Show the range first, then focus on the one to talk about

**Avoid**

- Every image needs detail: use Screenshot or split the slide
- The images differ a lot in aspect ratio

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- shots 3–4, ideally all 16:9
- label ≤ 10 chars
- caption ≤ 36 chars
- slide title ≤ 22 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- Screenshots are evidence; photos only set the mood

**Interaction**: Press → converge on the focus image; press again to show the caption

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `shots` | `{ src, alt, label }[]` | yes | Images |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Image placed in front at the end, default 0 |
| `caption` | `string` |  | Caption that appears on the second press |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<ConvergeShots
  kicker="多图汇聚"
  title="四个入口，一份演示"
  shots={[{ src: slide, alt: '…', label: '放映' }, { src: overview, alt: '…', label: '总览' }, { src: kit, alt: '…', label: '组件库' }]}
  caption="…"
/>
```

<a id="step-video"></a>

## 236 · Step through video `<StepVideo />`

**Position**: Motion · each press plays one segment and stops on the frame to talk about; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/motion` — `video`

A video on the left, 2–4 stop notes on the right. With build: 'reveal', each press plays up to the next stop and pauses there, and the matching note appears. Going back or jumping to the slide shows the stop frame directly; when jumping to the slide, printing or with reduced motion it stops at the last stop.

**Use when**

- A screen recording or an animated chart you want to talk over while it plays
- A change with 2–4 moments worth stopping at

**Avoid**

- The video is long or large: it is inlined into a single HTML file
- You need sound: the video is always muted

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- stops 2–4, at increases in seconds
- title ≤ 8 chars
- body ≤ 30 chars
- slide title ≤ 22 chars
- lead ≤ 40 chars
- video ideally ≤ 15 s and ≤ 1 MB (trim and compress with ffmpeg first)

**Interaction**: Press → play to the next stop

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `src` | `string` | yes | Video file (import) |
| `label` | `string` | yes | Description of the video content |
| `start` | `number` |  | Seconds shown before the first press, default 0 |
| `stops` | `{ at, title, body }[]` | yes | Stops |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<StepVideo
  kicker="逐段播放"
  title="十年里，差距怎么拉开"
  src={race}
  label="各国太阳能发电量排名动画"
  stops={[{ at: 1, title: '2015 年', body: '…' }, { at: 4.4, title: '2021 年', body: '…' }]}
/>
```

<a id="exhibit-cover"></a>

## 237 · Exhibition cover `<ExhibitCover />`

**Position**: Opening · a huge narrow serif title with a date block in the corner; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-biennale` — `cover`

A huge title fills the left half, a light date block sits at the top right, and 3–4 columns of small text divided by thin rules run along the bottom. Designed for the biennale style; other styles work too.

**Use when**

- The first slide of an exhibition, a cultural project or an annual review
- The subject is a period of time or one edition of an event

**Avoid**

- You need one representative number: use Cover
- The title is longer than 3 lines

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title 2–3 lines, ≤ 8 chars per line
- badge 1–2 lines, ≤ 10 characters per line
- meta 3–4 columns, value ≤ 24 chars

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Large title |
| `badge` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Date block at the top right |
| `meta` | `{ label, value }[]` | yes | Information columns at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<ExhibitCover
  kicker="年度回顾"
  title={['太阳能的', '十年']}
  badge={['2015 —', '2025']}
  meta={[{ label: '数据', value: 'Our World in Data' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="year-bars"></a>

## 238 · Numbers with year bars `<YearBars />`

**Position**: Data · two big numbers with thin bars by year; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-biennale` — `bars`

1–2 big numbers on the left (unit as superscript), 3–7 thin horizontal bars ordered by year on the right, the latest year in the accent colour. Designed for the biennale style; other styles work too.

**Use when**

- One metric grows year by year, with one or two takeaway numbers
- An annual review, visitor numbers or revenue

**Avoid**

- You are comparing several series: use LineChart
- There are more than 7 years: use LineChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- stats 1–2: value ≤ 5 characters, unit ≤ 3, label ≤ 12 chars, body ≤ 24 chars
- bars 3–7, earliest to latest
- slide title ≤ 22 chars
- source is required

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `stats` | `{ value, unit?, label, body? }[]` | yes | Big numbers |
| `bars` | `{ year, value }[]` | yes | Year bars |
| `format` | `(v: number) => string` |  | Format of the number at the end of each bar |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<YearBars
  kicker="全球太阳能发电"
  title="十年涨到约 11 倍"
  stats={[{ value: '2,779', unit: 'TWh', label: '2025 年发电量' }]}
  bars={[{ year: '2015', value: 256 }, …]}
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="number-tiles"></a>

## 239 · Number tiles `<NumberTiles />`

**Position**: Content · 3–4 tall colour blocks, each with a huge number; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-stencil` — `tiles`

A row of tall colour blocks, each with a huge number above and a title and one-line description below. In the stencil style the four colours are ochre, magenta, orange and cyan; other styles use the card colour and accent-coloured numbers. With build: 'reveal' the blocks appear one by one.

**Use when**

- Principles, rules or phases, 3–4 of them
- One sentence is enough for each

**Avoid**

- Each one needs a longer explanation: use NumberedColumns
- There are more than 4

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- tiles 3–4
- title ≤ 10 chars
- body ≤ 36 chars
- slide title ≤ 22 chars

**Interaction**: Press → blocks appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `tiles` | `{ title, body }[]` | yes | Colour blocks |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<NumberTiles
  kicker="规矩"
  title="先守规矩，再谈好看"
  tiles={[{ title: '只组合模板', body: '…' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="score-table"></a>

## 240 · Score table `<ScoreTable />`

**Position**: Comparison · a black header row, a coloured tag in every cell; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-stencil` — `score`

2–4 options side by side against 3–5 criteria, each cell a short coloured tag in one of four tones: good, mid, bad or plain. Designed for the stencil style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the rows appear one by one.

**Use when**

- Score a few options against the same criteria
- The verdict fits in two or three characters

**Avoid**

- A full sentence is needed in each cell: use CompareTable
- Only two things are compared: use Contrast

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- columns 2–4, each ≤ 10 chars
- rows 3–5, label ≤ 8 chars
- text ≤ 8 characters per cell
- slide title ≤ 22 chars

**Interaction**: Press → rows appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `columns` | `string[]` | yes | Options |
| `rows` | `{ label, cells: { text, tone? }[] }[]` | yes | Criteria and scores |
| `corner` | `string` |  | Header in the top-left corner |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<ScoreTable
  kicker="三种出口"
  title="放映、PDF、PPTX 怎么选"
  columns={['HTML 放映', 'PDF', 'PPTX']}
  rows={[{ label: '离线打开', cells: [{ text: '可以', tone: 'good' }, …] }]}
/>
```

<a id="metric-mosaic"></a>

## 241 · Metric mosaic `<MetricMosaic />`

**Position**: Data · a tall bar, a title block, small number tiles and a big number tile fit into one slide; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-neo` — `mosaic`

A tall accent-coloured bar holding one number on the left, the title block in the middle, 2–3 small number tiles at the top right and one big number at the bottom right. Designed for the fluorescent grid style; other styles work too.

**Use when**

- Explain one set of related numbers on a single slide
- A data slide for a launch or a growth review

**Avoid**

- The numbers have no main and secondary: use KpiBoard
- You need a trend: use LineChart

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- side value ≤ 7 characters
- small 2–3: value ≤ 6 characters, label ≤ 14 chars
- hero value ≤ 6 characters, label ≤ 16 chars
- title 1–2 lines, ≤ 6 chars per line
- source is required

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `side` | `{ value, label }` | yes | Tall bar on the left |
| `small` | `{ value, label }[]` | yes | Small number tiles |
| `hero` | `{ value, label }` | yes | Big number tile |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<MetricMosaic
  kicker="全球太阳能 · 2025"
  title={['十年，', '约十一倍']}
  side={{ value: '11×', label: '…' }}
  small={[{ value: '8.7%', label: '占全球发电' }]}
  hero={{ value: '2,779', label: '…' }}
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="step-grid"></a>

## 242 · Next-steps grid `<StepGrid />`

**Position**: Closing · a large title block plus three step blocks; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-neo` — `steps`

The top row holds one large accent-coloured title block and one pattern block; the bottom row holds three step blocks (time, title, description, small note), the last one in dark. Designed for the fluorescent grid style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the blocks light up one by one.

**Use when**

- Next steps or an action plan at the end
- Three things that happen in order

**Avoid**

- There are more than 3 steps: use Roadmap
- You are only summing up the points: use Closing

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps exactly 3
- when ≤ 8 chars, title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 40 chars, meta ≤ 16 characters
- title ≤ 2 lines × 8 chars

**Interaction**: Press → blocks light up one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `steps` | `{ when, title, body, meta? }[]` | yes | Three steps |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<StepGrid
  kicker="下一步"
  title="三步做一份"
  steps={[{ when: '今天', title: '建一个项目', body: '…', meta: 'pcafe init' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="framed-stats"></a>

## 243 · Framed numbers `<FramedStats />`

**Position**: Data · 3–4 number boxes with heavy borders; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-block-frame` — `stats`

A row of number boxes with thick black borders and hard shadows, each with a theme-coloured dot in the top-right corner. Designed for the block frame style; other styles work too.

**Use when**

- 3–4 key numbers side by side
- An overview of the size of an event, a product or a project

**Avoid**

- The numbers need explanation: use KpiBoard
- There is only one number: use BigStat

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- stats 3–4
- value ≤ 6 characters
- label ≤ 12 chars
- slide title ≤ 22 chars
- numbers need a source

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `stats` | `{ value, label }[]` | yes | Numbers |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<FramedStats
  kicker="一眼看全"
  title="这个引擎里有什么"
  stats={[{ value: '251', label: '个模板' }, …]}
  source="…"
/>
```

<a id="index-list"></a>

## 244 · Column index `<IndexList />`

**Position**: Content · numbered entries in two columns, divided by thin rules; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-cobalt-grid` — `index`

4–6 numbered entries split into two columns, each with a title and a one-line description, separated by thin rules, with the titles in the theme colour. Designed for the index style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the entries appear one by one.

**Use when**

- A contents list, an index or six entry points
- A few parallel topics, one sentence each

**Avoid**

- The entries have an order: use a Process template
- Each entry needs more room: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- entries 4–6
- title ≤ 12 chars
- body ≤ 40 chars
- slide title ≤ 22 chars

**Interaction**: Press → entries appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `entries` | `{ title, body }[]` | yes | Entries, left column first, then the right |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<IndexList
  kicker="文档目录"
  title="文档里的六个入口"
  entries={[{ title: '写作流程', body: '…' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="agenda-mosaic"></a>

## 245 · Agenda mosaic `<AgendaMosaic />`

**Position**: Opening · five topics as colour blocks of different sizes; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-forest` — `agenda`

One tall dark block holds the first topic; the other four are smaller blocks in the theme colour, the paper colour and a light theme colour, each with a number, a title and a small tag. Designed for the forest style; other styles work too.

**Use when**

- An agenda slide right after the opening, with exactly five topics

**Avoid**

- There are not five topics: use Agenda or PillAgenda
- The topics need descriptions: use Agenda

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items exactly 5
- title ≤ 12 chars
- tag ≤ 6 chars
- slide title ≤ 16 chars
- aside ≤ 16 characters

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `aside` | `string` |  | Small text right of the title |
| `items` | `{ title, tag }[]` | yes | Five topics |

**Example**

```tsx
<AgendaMosaic
  kicker="议程"
  title="今天讲五件事"
  items={[{ title: '为什么用模板写演示', tag: '背景' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="method-steps"></a>

## 246 · Method steps `<MethodSteps />`

**Position**: Process · numbered step cards alternating dark and light; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-emerald` — `method`

3–4 step cards alternating dark and paper colour, each with a big number, a title and a description, and a bottom line saying who does it and what it produces. Designed for the emerald style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the cards light up one by one.

**Use when**

- A fixed way of working or a collaboration process
- Each step needs to name who owns it

**Avoid**

- There are more than 4 steps: use Roadmap
- The steps loop: use Cycle

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- steps 3–4
- title ≤ 8 chars
- body ≤ 44 chars
- who and when ≤ 8 characters each
- slide title ≤ 22 chars

**Interaction**: Press → cards light up one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `steps` | `{ title, body, who?, when? }[]` | yes | Steps |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<MethodSteps
  kicker="工作方法"
  title="做一份演示的四步"
  steps={[{ title: '列故事线', body: '…', who: '作者', when: '大纲' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="priority-columns"></a>

## 247 · Three priority columns `<PriorityColumns />`

**Position**: Content · three full-height columns, huge numbers, one column in the theme colour; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-platform` — `priorities`

A thick rule under the big title, then three full-height columns below, each with a huge number, a tag, a title and a description, with one column filled in the theme colour. Designed for the manifesto style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the columns appear one by one.

**Use when**

- Three priorities, bottom lines or commitments
- A rallying talk, a manifesto or quarterly priorities

**Avoid**

- There are not exactly three: use NumberedColumns
- Numbers are needed: use KpiBoard

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- items exactly 3
- tag ≤ 6 chars, title ≤ 10 chars, body ≤ 40 chars
- title 1–2 lines, ≤ 8 chars per line
- lead ≤ 50 chars

**Interaction**: Press → columns appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `items` | `{ tag, title, body }[]` | yes | Three columns |
| `focus` | `number` |  | Column in the theme colour, default 1 (middle) |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<PriorityColumns
  kicker="底线"
  title={['写演示的', '三条底线']}
  items={[{ tag: '版式', title: '只组合模板', body: '…' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="chapter-number"></a>

## 248 · Chapter number `<ChapterNumber />`

**Position**: Section break · a huge chapter number with a short title; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-pink-script` — `chapter`

A glowing huge chapter number on the left, a short title and a paragraph on the right, and one line of small text at each bottom corner. Designed for the night pink style (dark); other styles work too.

**Use when**

- The start of a chapter
- Give the audience a sense of place in a long deck

**Avoid**

- The chapter has no number: use SectionBreak
- You need to list the chapter's key points: use ChapterProgress

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- num 1–2 characters
- title 2–3 lines, ≤ 8 chars per line
- body ≤ 60 chars
- foot two items, ≤ 16 characters each

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `num` | `string` | yes | Chapter number |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `body` | `string` |  | Description |
| `foot` | `[string, string]` |  | Two lines of small text at the bottom |

**Example**

```tsx
<ChapterNumber
  kicker="第二章"
  num="02"
  title={['一个文件，', '到哪都能放']}
  body="…"
/>
```

<a id="stack-bars"></a>

## 249 · Stepped layers `<StackBars />`

**Position**: Structure · horizontal bars offset layer by layer; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/sig-broadside` — `stack`

3–6 horizontal bars offset to the right layer by layer from top to bottom, the colour deepening from the theme colour, each with a name on the left and one line of description on the right. Designed for the broadside style; other styles work too. With build: 'reveal' the layers appear one by one.

**Use when**

- A layered structure: a technology stack, an organisation hierarchy or priorities
- Layers that depend on the ones below

**Avoid**

- The layers have a quantity relationship: use Funnel or Pyramid
- Each layer needs a detailed explanation: use LayerStack

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- layers 3–6, top to bottom
- name ≤ 8 chars
- note ≤ 20 chars
- slide title ≤ 22 chars

**Interaction**: Press → layers appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `layers` | `{ name, note }[]` | yes | Layers, top to bottom |
| `source` | `string` |  | Source |

**Example**

```tsx
<StackBars
  kicker="分层"
  title="一份演示由五层叠成"
  layers={[{ name: '内容', note: 'src/decks 里的 deck 文件' }, …]}
/>
```

<a id="chat-demo"></a>

## 250 · Chat demo `<ChatDemo />`

**Position**: Realistic UI · show how it works through a short conversation; **Reference slide**: `src/decks/mockui` — `chat`

Title, lead and 0–3 notes on the left; a hand-built chat window on the right, where the last reply can carry a result card. The window always carries the label 'illustrative interface'. With build: 'reveal', each press reveals one message.

**Use when**

- A tool, an assistant or a support product: show one typical interaction
- When that is clearer than listing features

**Avoid**

- You have a real screenshot: use Screenshot or TiltShot
- There are more than 5 messages: split the slide

**Content limits** (exceeding them breaks the layout; `npm run qa` reports it)

- title ≤ 2 lines × 10 chars
- lead ≤ 50 chars
- messages 2–5, each ≤ 40 chars
- card 2–4 rows
- notes 0–3, each ≤ 20 chars
- app does not use a real brand name unless it is your own product
- source states that it is illustrative

**Interaction**: Press → messages appear one by one

**Props**

| Name | Type | Required | Notes |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `kicker` | `string` | yes | Section label |
| `title` | `string \| string[]` | yes | Title |
| `lead` | `string` |  | Lead |
| `app` | `string` | yes | Window name |
